Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("adaptive_span_attention")
class AdaptiveSpanAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = False
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        max_span: int = 128,
        ramp_size: float = 32.0,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        layer_idx: int | None = None,
        return_style: str | None = None,
        rolling_cache_size: int | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        # ---------------- Dimensions ----------------
        hidden_size = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim")
        num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
        )
        num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") or kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads")
        head_dim = 0

        if config is not None:
            adapter = ModelAdapter(config)
            hidden_size = hidden_size or adapter.hidden_size
            num_heads = num_heads or adapter.num_heads
            num_kv_heads = num_kv_heads or adapter.num_key_value_heads
            head_dim = getattr(adapter, "head_dim", 0)

        if not hidden_size and source_attn is not None:
            cand = getattr(
                source_attn,
                "hidden_size",
                getattr(
                    source_attn,
                    "embed_dim",
                    getattr(source_attn, "all_head_size", None),
                ),
            )
            if cand is None and hasattr(source_attn, "q_proj"):
                cand = getattr(source_attn.q_proj, "in_features", None)
            hidden_size = cand

        hidden_size = int(hidden_size or 768)
        num_heads = int(num_heads or 12)
        num_kv_heads = int(num_kv_heads or num_heads)
        head_dim = head_dim if head_dim > 0 else (hidden_size // num_heads if num_heads > 0 else 64)

        self.hidden_size: int = hidden_size
        self.d_model: int = hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads: int = num_kv_heads
        self.head_dim: int = head_dim
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.layer_idx: int = int(layer_idx) if layer_idx is not None else 0
        self.rolling_cache_size: int | None = rolling_cache_size

        # ---------------- Span config ----------------
        if config is not None:
            self.max_span = int(getattr(config, "max_span", max_span))
            self.ramp_size = float(getattr(config, "ramp_size", ramp_size))
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.max_span = int(getattr(comp_config, "max_span", max_span))
            self.ramp_size = float(getattr(comp_config, "ramp_size", ramp_size))
        else:
            self.max_span = int(max_span)
            self.ramp_size = float(ramp_size)

        # FIXED: Failure Mode 1 (Mode Collapse): Enforce min_span = 32 floor
        # to prevent the attention span from collapsing to S -> 1 token ("eseseses..." loops)
        self.min_span: float = float(kwargs.get("min_span", getattr(config, "min_span", 32.0) if config else 32.0))

        # ---------------- Decoder / nested encoder detection ----------------
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        is_dec = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "is_decoder",
                getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
            )
            if config
            else False
        )
        _dec_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if any(model_type.startswith(p) for p in _dec_prefixes):
            is_dec = True
        if hasattr(config, "architectures") and any(
            "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
            for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)

        source_cls = source_attn.__class__.__name__ if source_attn is not None else ""
        cfg_model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower() if config else ""
        is_nested = (
            source_cls in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
            or cfg_model_type in ("roberta", "bert")
            or (
                source_cls.endswith(("SelfAttention", "SelfAttn"))
                and "distil" not in source_cls.lower()
                and "gpt" not in source_cls.lower()
                and "qwen" not in source_cls.lower()
            )
        )
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = bool(kwargs.get("is_nested_encoder", is_nested))

        if return_style is None:
            if self.is_nested_encoder:
                return_style = "bert"
            elif "GPT2" in source_cls or "gpt2" in cfg_model_type:
                return_style = "gpt2"
            elif "T5" in source_cls or "t5" in cfg_model_type:
                return_style = "t5"
            elif self.is_decoder:
                return_style = "llama"
            else:
                return_style = "bert"
        self.return_style: str = str(return_style)

        self.has_relative_attention_bias: bool = False
        self.relative_attention_bias: nn.Embedding | None = None
        self.relative_attention_num_buckets: int = (
            getattr(config, "relative_attention_num_buckets", 32) if config else 32
        )
        self.relative_attention_max_distance: int = (
            getattr(config, "relative_attention_max_distance", 128) if config else 128
        )

        if config is not None:
            resolved_lora_rank = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)),
            )
            resolved_lora_alpha = getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            resolved_lora_rank = getattr(
                comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)
            )
            resolved_lora_alpha = getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        else:
            resolved_lora_rank = kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)
            resolved_lora_alpha = kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)

        target_dtype = kwargs.get("dtype")
        if target_dtype is None and source_attn is not None:
            for p in source_attn.parameters():
                target_dtype = p.dtype
                break

        self.lora_rank = int(resolved_lora_rank)
        self.lora_alpha = float(resolved_lora_alpha)
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
            dtype=target_dtype,
        )
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            self.out_proj: nn.Module = IdentityOutProj()
        else:
            self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
                self.all_head_size,
                self.hidden_size,
                bias=True,
                lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
                lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
                freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
                dtype=target_dtype,
            )

        self.span_logits = nn.Parameter(torch.full((self.num_heads,), 2.0, dtype=torch.float32))

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", dropout),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else dropout
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

        if target_dtype is not None:
            self.q_proj.to(target_dtype)
            self.k_proj.to(target_dtype)
            self.v_proj.to(target_dtype)
            self.out_proj.to(target_dtype)

    @property
    def current_span(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        raw_span = torch.sigmoid(self.span_logits) * self.max_span
        return torch.clamp(raw_span, min=self.min_span)

    def get_span_regularization_loss(self, lambda_span: float = 0.01) -> torch.Tensor:
        return lambda_span * torch.sigmoid(self.span_logits).mean()

    def _relative_position_bucket(
        self, relative_position, bidirectional=True, num_buckets=32, max_distance=128
    ):
        relative_buckets = 0
        if bidirectional:
            num_buckets //= 2
            relative_buckets += (relative_position > 0).to(torch.long) * num_buckets
            relative_position = torch.abs(relative_position)
        else:
            relative_position = -torch.min(relative_position, torch.zeros_like(relative_position))
        max_exact = num_buckets // 2
        is_small = relative_position < max_exact
        rel_large = max_exact + (
            torch.log(relative_position.float() / max_exact)
            / math.log(max_distance / max_exact)
            * (num_buckets - max_exact)
        ).to(torch.long)
        rel_large = torch.min(rel_large, torch.full_like(rel_large, num_buckets - 1))
        relative_buckets += torch.where(is_small, relative_position, rel_large)
        return relative_buckets

    def compute_bias(self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device=None) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self.relative_attention_bias is None:
            return torch.zeros((1, self.num_heads, query_length, key_length), device=device)
        ctx = torch.arange(query_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[:, None]
        mem = torch.arange(key_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[None, :]
        rel = mem - ctx
        buckets = self._relative_position_bucket(
            rel,
            bidirectional=(not self.is_decoder),
            num_buckets=self.relative_attention_num_buckets,
            max_distance=self.relative_attention_max_distance,
        )
        values = self.relative_attention_bias(buckets)
        return values.permute([2, 0, 1]).unsqueeze(0)

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback for %s: %s", proj, e)

        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            c_attn_layer = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_layer, "base_layer", c_attn_layer)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_layer, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_layer, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                is_conv1d = (
                    hasattr(c_attn_layer, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or c_attn_layer.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                )
                if is_conv1d and weight.dim() == 2:
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split = weight.shape[0] // 3
                extracted["q"] = (
                    weight[:split].contiguous(),
                    bias_data[:split].contiguous() if bias_data is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    weight[split : 2 * split].contiguous(),
                    (bias_data[split : 2 * split].contiguous() if bias_data is not None else None),
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    weight[2 * split :].contiguous(),
                    (bias_data[2 * split :].contiguous() if bias_data is not None else None),
                )

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_layer = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_layer, "base_layer", c_proj_layer)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_layer, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                is_conv1d = (
                    hasattr(c_proj_layer, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or c_proj_layer.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                )
                if is_conv1d and w_o.dim() == 2:
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_layer, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    w_o.contiguous(),
                    b_o.data.clone().contiguous() if b_o is not None else None,
                )

        if hasattr(src, "self") and any(extracted[k][0] is None for k in ("q", "k", "v")):
            s = src.self
            if hasattr(s, "query") and extracted["q"][0] is None:
                extracted["q"] = (s.query.weight.data, getattr(s.query, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "key") and extracted["k"][0] is None:
                extracted["k"] = (s.key.weight.data, getattr(s.key, "bias", None))
            if hasattr(s, "value") and extracted["v"][0] is None:
                extracted["v"] = (s.value.weight.data, getattr(s.value, "bias", None))

        if (
            hasattr(src, "output")
            and getattr(src, "output", None) is not None
            and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            and extracted["o"][0] is None
        ):
            dense = src.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (dense.weight.data, getattr(dense, "bias", None))

        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        if hasattr(layer, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                layer.weight.data,
                                getattr(layer, "bias", None),
                            )
                            break

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        cfg_type = getattr(self.config, "model_type", "").lower() if self.config else ""
        if not self.is_nested_encoder:
            self.is_nested_encoder = bool(
                cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention")
                or cfg_type in ("roberta", "bert")
                or (
                    cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention")
                    and "distil" not in cls_name.lower()
                    and "gpt" not in cls_name.lower()
                    and "qwen" not in cls_name.lower()
                )
            )
        if self.is_nested_encoder and not isinstance(self.out_proj, IdentityOutProj):
            self.out_proj = IdentityOutProj()

        missing = [k for k in ("q", "k", "v") if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning("AdaptiveSpanAttention missing weights for projections: %s", missing)

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in (
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ):
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    continue
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    out_d, in_d = target.weight.shape
                    src_out, src_in = w.shape
                    c_o, c_i = min(out_d, src_out), min(in_d, src_in)
                    target.weight.data[:c_o, :c_i].copy_(w[:c_o, :c_i])
                    if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                        b_val = b.data if isinstance(b, nn.Parameter) else b
                        c_b = min(len(target.bias), len(b_val))
                        target.bias.data[:c_b].copy_(b_val[:c_b])
                target.freeze_base_weights()

        if getattr(src, "rotary_emb", None) is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb
        if hasattr(src, "has_relative_attention_bias"):
            self.has_relative_attention_bias = bool(src.has_relative_attention_bias)
        if getattr(src, "relative_attention_bias", None) is not None:
            self.relative_attention_bias = src.relative_attention_bias
            self.has_relative_attention_bias = True
        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)


    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, N_q, _ = hidden_states.shape
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K_new = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, -1, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V_new = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, -1, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        position_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
        if position_ids is None and not is_cross_attention:
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None and past_key_value is not False:
                if hasattr(past_key_value, "get_seq_length"):
                    try:
                        past_len = past_key_value.get_seq_length(self.layer_idx)
                    except Exception:
                        try:
                            past_len = past_key_value.get_seq_length()
                        except Exception:
                            past_len = 0
                elif (
                    isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list))
                    and len(past_key_value) >= 2
                    and past_key_value[0] is not None
                ):
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
            position_ids = (
                torch.arange(past_len, past_len + N_q, device=device, dtype=torch.long)
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .expand(B, -1)
            )

        pos_emb = (
            position_embeddings
            if position_embeddings is not None
            else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        )
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            Q, K_new = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K_new, cos, sin, position_ids=position_ids)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            if position_ids is None:
                position_ids = (
                    torch.arange(N_q, device=device, dtype=torch.long).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
                )
            try:
                cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(K_new, position_ids=position_ids)
            except TypeError:
                try:
                    cos, sin = self.rotary_emb(K_new, position_ids)
                except Exception:
                    cos, sin = None, None
            except Exception:
                cos, sin = None, None
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                Q, K_new = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K_new, cos, sin, position_ids=position_ids)

        new_past_key_value: Any | None = None
        use_cache = kwargs.get("use_cache", False) or past_key_value is not None

        if is_cross_attention:
            K, V = K_new, V_new
            new_past_key_value = past_key_value
        else:
            if past_key_value is not None and past_key_value is not False:
                if hasattr(past_key_value, "update"):
                    try:
                        K, V, new_past_key_value = past_key_value.update(
                            K_new, V_new, self.layer_idx
                        )
                    except Exception:
                        K = torch.cat([past_key_value[0], K_new], dim=-2)
                        V = torch.cat([past_key_value[1], V_new], dim=-2)
                        new_past_key_value = (K, V)
                elif isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
                    past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
                    if past_k is not None:
                        K = torch.cat([past_k, K_new], dim=-2)
                        V = torch.cat([past_v, V_new], dim=-2)
                    else:
                        K, V = K_new, V_new
                    new_past_key_value = (K, V)
                else:
                    K, V = K_new, V_new
                    new_past_key_value = (K, V)
            else:
                K, V = K_new, V_new
                if use_cache:
                    new_past_key_value = (K, V)

            if self.rolling_cache_size is not None and K.shape[-2] > self.rolling_cache_size:
                K = K[..., -self.rolling_cache_size :, :].contiguous()
                V = V[..., -self.rolling_cache_size :, :].contiguous()
                if isinstance(new_past_key_value, tuple):
                    new_past_key_value = (K, V)

        N_k = K.shape[-2]

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            if K.shape[1] == self.num_kv_heads:
                K_full = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
                V_full = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)
            elif K.shape[1] == self.num_heads:
                K_full, V_full = K, V
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"Unexpected K head dim {K.shape[1]}")
        else:
            K_full, V_full = K, V

        scale = d**-0.5
        scores = torch.matmul(Q, K_full.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        position_bias = kwargs.get("position_bias")
        if position_bias is None and self.has_relative_attention_bias:
            position_bias = self.compute_bias(N_q, N_k, device=device)
        if position_bias is not None:
            scores = scores + position_bias.to(scores.dtype)

        if not is_cross_attention:
            # FIXED: Enforce min_span floor to avoid auto-regressive Markov bigram collapse ("eseseses...")
            span = torch.clamp(torch.sigmoid(self.span_logits) * self.max_span, min=self.min_span)  # [H]
            if position_ids is not None:
                q_pos = position_ids[0].to(torch.float32)
                if N_k == N_q:
                    k_pos = q_pos
                else:
                    last_q = q_pos[-1].item()
                    k_pos = torch.arange(
                        last_q - N_k + 1, last_q + 1, device=device, dtype=torch.float32
                    )
            else:
                q_pos = torch.arange(N_q, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
                k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

            if getattr(self, "is_decoder", True) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
                dist = (q_pos.unsqueeze(1) - k_pos.unsqueeze(0)).clamp(min=0.0)
            else:
                dist = (q_pos.unsqueeze(1) - k_pos.unsqueeze(0)).abs()

            span_h = span.view(H, 1, 1).to(torch.float32)
            dist_mat = dist.unsqueeze(0)

            ramp = torch.clamp((self.ramp_size + span_h - dist_mat) / self.ramp_size, 0.0, 1.0)
            span_mask = torch.log(ramp.clamp(min=1e-9)).to(scores.dtype)
            scores = scores + span_mask.unsqueeze(0)

        is_causal = self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention
        keep_mask = _build_keep_mask(
            B=B,
            N_q=N_q,
            N_k=N_k,
            is_causal=is_causal,
            attention_mask=attention_mask,
            encoder_attention_mask=(encoder_attention_mask if is_cross_attention else None),
            device=device,
        )

        fully_masked: torch.Tensor | None = None
        if keep_mask is not None:
            fully_masked = ~keep_mask.any(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            mask_val = _safe_mask_value(scores.dtype)
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~keep_mask, mask_val)

        if fully_masked is not None:
            scores = torch.where(fully_masked, torch.zeros_like(scores), scores)

        if dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) and scores.numel() > 2**30:
            attn = torch.empty_like(scores)
            chunk = max(64, min(512, N_q))
            for s in range(0, N_q, chunk):
                e = min(s + chunk, N_q)
                attn[:, :, s:e, :] = F.softmax(scores[:, :, s:e, :].float(), dim=-1).to(dtype)
        else:
            attn = F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype)

        if fully_masked is not None:
            attn = attn.masked_fill(fully_masked, 0.0)

        attn = self.dropout(attn)
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn = attn * head_mask

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V_full)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        return self._format_output(
            output,
            attn if output_attentions else None,
            new_past_key_value,
            position_bias,
            output_attentions,
        )

    def _format_output(
        self,
        output: torch.Tensor,
        attn: torch.Tensor | None,
        past_kv: Any | None,
        position_bias: torch.Tensor | None,
        output_attentions: bool,
    ):
        style = self.return_style
        if style == "bert":
            return output, attn
        if style == "gpt2":
            if output_attentions:
                return output, past_kv, attn
            return output, past_kv
        if style == "t5":
            if output_attentions:
                return output, position_bias, attn
            return output, position_bias
        return output, attn, past_kv

def current_span(self) -> torch.Tensor:

def compute_bias(self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device=None) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def compute_bias(self, query_length: int, key_length: int, device=None) -> torch.Tensor:
    if self.relative_attention_bias is None:
        return torch.zeros((1, self.num_heads, query_length, key_length), device=device)
    ctx = torch.arange(query_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[:, None]
    mem = torch.arange(key_length, dtype=torch.long, device=device)[None, :]
    rel = mem - ctx
    buckets = self._relative_position_bucket(
        rel,
        bidirectional=(not self.is_decoder),
        num_buckets=self.relative_attention_num_buckets,
        max_distance=self.relative_attention_max_distance,
    )
    values = self.relative_attention_bias(buckets)
    return values.permute([2, 0, 1]).unsqueeze(0)
def get_span_regularization_loss(self, lambda_span: float = 0.01) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def get_span_regularization_loss(self, lambda_span: float = 0.01) -> torch.Tensor:
    return lambda_span * torch.sigmoid(self.span_logits).mean()

class Any (*args, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
class Any(metaclass=_AnyMeta):
    """Special type indicating an unconstrained type.

    - Any is compatible with every type.
    - Any assumed to have all methods.
    - All values assumed to be instances of Any.

    Note that all the above statements are true from the point of view of
    static type checkers. At runtime, Any should not be used with instance
    checks.
    """

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if cls is Any:
            raise TypeError("Any cannot be instantiated")
        return super().__new__(cls)

class BaseAttention(nn.Module):
    """Every custom attention module must inherit from this."""

    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None, rank: int | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.config = config
        self.rank = rank
        self.num_heads = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "num_attention_heads",
                    getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 12
            )
        )
        self.hidden_size = (
            kwargs.get("hidden_size")
            or kwargs.get("dim")
            or kwargs.get("d_model")
            or (
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_size",
                    getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)),
                )
                if config is not None
                else 768
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = getattr(
            config,
            "d_kv",
            getattr(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64,
            ),
        )

    @abstractmethod
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states,
        attention_mask=None,
        head_mask=None,
        output_attentions=False,
        **kwargs,
    ) -> tuple[Any, Any | None]:
        """Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Subclasses of BaseAttention must implement forward()")

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        """Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer."""
        if old_attn is None:
            return
        import torch

        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        adapter = UniversalAdapter()

        try:
            w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
            w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "k")
            w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "v")
            w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "o")

            with torch.no_grad():
                dim = self.hidden_size
                for q_name in ["W_q", "q_proj", "q_lin", "Wq", "query", "q"]:
                    if hasattr(self, q_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, q_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_q.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_q)
                            if b_q is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_q)

                for k_name in ["W_k", "k_proj", "k_lin", "Wk", "key", "k"]:
                    if hasattr(self, k_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, k_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_k.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_k)
                            if b_k is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_k)

                for v_name in ["W_v", "v_proj", "v_lin", "Wv", "value", "v"]:
                    if hasattr(self, v_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, v_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_v.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_v)
                            if b_v is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_v)

                for o_name in ["out", "out_proj", "o_proj", "out_lin", "proj", "dense"]:
                    if hasattr(self, o_name):
                        mod = getattr(self, o_name)
                        if hasattr(mod, "weight") and mod.weight.shape == w_o.shape:
                            mod.weight.copy_(w_o)
                            if b_o is not None and getattr(mod, "bias", None) is not None:
                                mod.bias.copy_(b_o)

                # Handle fused in_proj
                if hasattr(self, "in_proj") and hasattr(self.in_proj, "weight"):
                    if self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 2, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_v[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim:].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                    elif self.in_proj.weight.shape == torch.Size([dim * 3, dim]):
                        self.in_proj.weight[:dim].copy_(w_q[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[dim : 2 * dim].copy_(w_k[:dim])
                        self.in_proj.weight[2 * dim :].copy_(w_v[:dim])
        except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as e:
            logger.debug("BaseAttention.copy_weights failed: %s", e)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning("Unexpected error in BaseAttention.copy_weights: %s", e)

    def extra_repr(self) -> str:
        """Extra repr."""
        return f"num_heads={self.num_heads}, head_dim={self.head_dim}, rank={self.rank}"

def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("block_transformer_hiba")
class BlockTransformerHIBA(BaseAttention):
    r"""
    Block Transformer with Hierarchical Intra-Block and Inter-Block Attention (HIBA).

    Hardened for production use across DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, and Qwen/LLaMA/Mistral.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int = 64,
        block_size: int = 64,
        top_k_inter: int = 4,
        rectification_interval: int = 4,
        layer_id: int | None = None,
        layer_idx: int | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        source_attn = (
            old_attn
            if old_attn is not None
            else (
                pretrained_attn
                if pretrained_attn is not None
                else kwargs.get("old_attn", kwargs.get("pretrained_attn"))
            )
        )

        self.dim: int = kwargs.get("hidden_size") or getattr(
            config,
            "hidden_size",
            getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)),
        )
        self.hidden_size: int = self.dim
        self.num_heads: int = (
            kwargs.get("num_heads")
            or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads")
            or getattr(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                getattr(config, "num_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12)),
            )
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = getattr(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim: int = getattr(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            getattr(config, "kv_channels", self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)),
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat: int = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa: bool = True

        self.block_size: int = block_size
        self.top_k_inter: int = top_k_inter
        self.rectification_interval: int = max(1, rectification_interval)

        # Static layer index (not mutated inside forward)
        resolved_layer_id = (
            layer_id
            if layer_id is not None
            else (
                layer_idx
                if layer_idx is not None
                else kwargs.get("layer_id", kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
            )
        )
        self.layer_idx: int = int(resolved_layer_id)

        # LoRA adaptation configuration
        self.lora_rank: int = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_rank",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
        )
        self.lora_alpha: float = getattr(
            config,
            "lora_alpha",
            getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
        )
        self.freeze_base: bool = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Decoder & nested encoder detection
        model_type = getattr(config, "model_type", "").lower()
        is_dec = getattr(
            config,
            "is_decoder",
            getattr(config, "is_causal", kwargs.get("is_causal", False)),
        )
        _mt = (model_type or "").lower()
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        if (
            any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
            or hasattr(config, "architectures")
            and any(
                "GPT" in str(a) or "CausalLM" in str(a)
                for a in (getattr(config, "architectures", []) or [])
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal: bool = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        # Multi-Head Attention Projections equipped with trainable residual LoRA adapters
        self.W_q = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_k = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.W_v = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self.rotary_emb = None
        self.comp_config: Any | None = comp_config

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Compatibility properties for parameter inspection & framework integration
    # --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def q_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_q

    @property
    def k_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_k

    @property
    def v_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def o_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_q

    @property
    def k_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_k

    @property
    def v_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def out_lin(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def query(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_q

    @property
    def key(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_k

    @property
    def value(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.W_v

    @property
    def dense(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self) -> LoRALinear:
        return self.out_proj

    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        r"""
        Extracts and copies pretrained weights across DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, and Qwen/LLaMA.
        Strictly validates that copied weights are non-empty and verifies reconstruction norms.
        """
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            return

        # 1. Detect nested encoder architectures (RoBERTa / BERT)
        cls_name = pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(pretrained_attn, "out_lin")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        # Inherit rotary embeddings if present
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb"):
            self.rotary_emb = pretrained_attn.rotary_emb

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 2. GPT-2: Fused c_attn + c_proj
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn") and pretrained_attn.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = pretrained_attn.c_attn
            if hasattr(c_attn_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_attn_mod, "merged", False):
                try:
                    c_attn_mod.merge()
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            # GPT-2 Output: c_proj
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj") and pretrained_attn.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = pretrained_attn.c_proj
                if hasattr(c_proj_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_proj_mod, "merged", False):
                    try:
                        c_proj_mod.merge()
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                        logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 3. RoBERTa / BERT nested self-attention (pretrained_attn.self)
        elif hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query"):
            s = pretrained_attn.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            # Output dense from outer container
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = pretrained_attn.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 4. Standard linear projections (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key", "W_k"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, name):
                        mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # 5. Fallback via UniversalAdapter
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(pretrained_attn, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Verify all mandatory projections were extracted
        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                f"BlockTransformerHIBA copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} "
                f"from module {pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__} ({pretrained_attn}). Proceeding with initialized weights."
            )

        # 6. Copy weights into target LoRALinear layers with strict dimension safety
        target_map = {
            "q": self.W_q,
            "k": self.W_k,
            "v": self.W_v,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        # Inner nested self-attention bypasses out_proj; initialize to identity
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

                # Handle transposed orientation if necessary (only if shape does not match already)
                if (
                    w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                    and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()
                out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

                target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                        device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                    )
                )

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                        b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                    )
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                # Weight verification: ensure weights are non-trivial
                w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
                if w_norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"Weight copy verification failed for projection '{proj_key}': norm too small ({w_norm:.2e})"
                    )

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def _convert_mask_to_bool(
        self,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None,
        B: int,
        L: int,
        device: torch.device,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        if attention_mask is None:
            return torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            return (
                attention_mask.bool() if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool else attention_mask > 0
            )
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 3:
            if attention_mask.shape[1] == 1:
                m = attention_mask[:, 0, :]
                return (
                    m > -1000.0
                    if (m.dtype != torch.bool and m.min() < -100.0)
                    else (m if m.dtype == torch.bool else m > 0)
                )
            else:
                m = (
                    attention_mask > -1000.0
                    if (attention_mask.dtype != torch.bool and attention_mask.min() < -100.0)
                    else (
                        attention_mask if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool else attention_mask > 0
                    )
                )
                return m.any(dim=1)
        elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            m = (
                attention_mask > -1000.0
                if (attention_mask.dtype != torch.bool and attention_mask.min() < -100.0)
                else (attention_mask if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool else attention_mask > 0)
            )
            if m.shape[1] == 1:
                m = m.squeeze(1)
            else:
                m = m.any(dim=1)
            return m.any(dim=-2)
        return torch.ones(B, L, device=device, dtype=torch.bool)

    def _intra_block_attention(
        self,
        q: torch.Tensor,
        k: torch.Tensor,
        v: torch.Tensor,
        key_padding_mask: torch.Tensor,
        causal: bool = False,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, L_b, D_h = q.shape
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(D_h)
        attn = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        if causal:
            causal_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full((L_b, L_b), -10000.0, device=q.device, dtype=torch.float32),
                diagonal=1,
            )
            attn = attn + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1)

        pad_mask = key_padding_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
        attn = attn.masked_fill(pad_mask, -10000.0)

        probs = F.softmax(attn, dim=-1)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        if head_mask is not None:
            probs = probs * head_mask

        return torch.matmul(probs.to(v.dtype), v)

    def _inter_block_sparse(
        self,
        q_b: torch.Tensor,
        k_all: torch.Tensor,
        v_all: torch.Tensor,
        block_idx: int,
        num_blocks: int,
        valid_mask: torch.Tensor,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        B, H, L_b, D_h = q_b.shape
        total_len = k_all.size(2)
        start_b = block_idx * self.block_size
        end_b = min((block_idx + 1) * self.block_size, total_len)

        k_select: list[torch.Tensor] = []
        v_select: list[torch.Tensor] = []
        valid_select: list[torch.Tensor] = []

        # Previous tokens: includes block 0 (CLS at index 0)
        if block_idx > 0:
            k_select.append(k_all[:, :, :start_b, :])
            v_select.append(v_all[:, :, :start_b, :])
            valid_select.append(valid_mask[:, :start_b])

        # Subsequent tokens (only for bidirectional non-causal encoders)
        if (block_idx + 1) * self.block_size < total_len and not self.is_decoder:
            k_select.append(k_all[:, :, end_b:, :])
            v_select.append(v_all[:, :, end_b:, :])
            valid_select.append(valid_mask[:, end_b:])

        if not k_select:
            return torch.zeros_like(q_b)

        k_neighbors = torch.cat(k_select, dim=2)
        v_neighbors = torch.cat(v_select, dim=2)
        valid_neighbors = torch.cat(valid_select, dim=1)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(D_h)
        scores = (
            torch.matmul(q_b.float(), k_neighbors.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        )

        pad_mask = ~valid_neighbors.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_mask, -10000.0)

        # Guarantee CLS block inclusion for block_idx > 0
        scores_for_topk = scores.clone()
        if block_idx > 0:
            scores_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] = scores_for_topk[:, :, :, 0] + 1e4

        top_k = min(self.top_k_inter, k_neighbors.size(2))
        _, topk_idx = torch.topk(scores_for_topk, top_k, dim=-1)
        sparse_mask = torch.zeros_like(scores).scatter_(-1, topk_idx, 1.0)
        scores = scores.masked_fill(sparse_mask == 0, -10000.0)

        probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        if head_mask is not None:
            probs = probs * head_mask

        return torch.matmul(probs.to(v_neighbors.dtype), v_neighbors)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        is_cross_attention = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_states = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states
        S = kv_states.shape[1]

        # 1. Base Projections
        q = self.W_q(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = (
            self.W_k(kv_states)
            .view(B, S, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.W_v(kv_states)
            .view(B, S, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # 2. RoPE Embeddings applied before GQA expansion
        pos_emb = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        cos = kwargs.get(
            "cos",
            (pos_emb[0] if isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2 else None),
        )
        sin = kwargs.get(
            "sin",
            (pos_emb[1] if isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2 else None),
        )

        if cos is not None and sin is not None:
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))
        elif hasattr(self, "rotary_emb") and self.rotary_emb is not None:
            try:
                from neuralops.utils.compat import maybe_apply_rotary

                q, k = maybe_apply_rotary(
                    q,
                    k,
                    getattr(self, "adapter", None),
                    self.rotary_emb,
                    kwargs.get("position_ids"),
                )
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 3. GQA Expansion
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        # 4. Safe head mask reshaping
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(head_mask.shape[0], head_mask.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)

        # Valid token mask
        active_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross_attention else attention_mask
        valid_mask = self._convert_mask_to_bool(active_mask, B, S, hidden_states.device)

        num_blocks = (L + self.block_size - 1) // self.block_size
        # Static rectification decision (does not increment layer_idx across forward passes)
        use_rectify = (
            (self.layer_idx % self.rectification_interval == 0)
            or (num_blocks <= 2)
            or is_cross_attention
        )

        if use_rectify:
            # Dense attention pathway (full rectification)
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
            # Memory-efficient: use SDPA to avoid OOM on large seqs

            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k.half() if k.dtype == torch.float32 else k
                _vs = v.half() if v.dtype == torch.float32 else v
                _is_causal = getattr(self, "is_decoder", False) and not is_cross_attention and L > 1
                _sdpa_out = torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    is_causal=_is_causal,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                _sdpa_out = (
                    _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                    .transpose(1, 2)
                    .contiguous()
                    .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
                )
                _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                return (
                    self.out_proj(_sdpa_out)
                    if not getattr(self, "is_nested_encoder", False)
                    else _sdpa_out
                ), None
            except Exception:
                pass

            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            # If a 4D attention mask is provided, it already incorporates causal + padding constraints
            if active_mask is not None and active_mask.dim() == 4:
                if active_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    scores = scores.masked_fill(~active_mask, -10000.0)
                else:
                    scores = scores + active_mask.to(scores.dtype)
            else:
                # Causal mask for decoders
                if self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention:
                    causal_mask = torch.triu(
                        torch.full((L, S), -10000.0, device=scores.device, dtype=torch.float32),
                        diagonal=1 + (S - L),
                    )
                    scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(1)

                # Padding mask
                pad_mask = ~valid_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(pad_mask, -10000.0)

            attn_probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
            attn_probs = torch.nan_to_num(attn_probs, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
            attn_probs = self.dropout(attn_probs)

            if head_mask is not None:
                attn_probs = attn_probs * head_mask

            out = torch.matmul(attn_probs.to(v.dtype), v)
            return_attn = attn_probs if output_attentions else None
        else:
            # Hierarchical Intra-Block and Inter-Block Attention
            out_list = []
            for b_idx in range(num_blocks):
                start = b_idx * self.block_size
                end = min(start + self.block_size, L)
                q_b = q[:, :, start:end, :]
                k_b = k[:, :, start:end, :]
                v_b = v[:, :, start:end, :]
                block_valid = valid_mask[:, start:end]
                block_pad_mask = ~block_valid

                intra_out = self._intra_block_attention(
                    q_b,
                    k_b,
                    v_b,
                    block_pad_mask,
                    causal=(self.is_decoder and not is_cross_attention),
                    head_mask=head_mask,
                )
                inter_out = self._inter_block_sparse(
                    q_b, k, v, b_idx, num_blocks, valid_mask, head_mask=head_mask
                )
                out_b = intra_out + inter_out
                out_list.append(out_b)

            out = torch.cat(out_list, dim=2)
            return_attn = None

        # Merge heads: (B, H, L, head_dim) -> (B, L, all_head_size)
        out = out.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)

        # 5. Output projection with nested encoder bypass
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            # Outer RobertaAttention.output.dense performs the dense projection; bypass out_proj
            final_out = out
        else:
            final_out = self.out_proj(out)

        return final_out, return_attn

Block Transformer with Hierarchical Intra-Block and Inter-Block Attention (HIBA).

Hardened for production use across DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, and Qwen/LLaMA/Mistral.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: torch.nn.modules.module.Module) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
    r"""
    Extracts and copies pretrained weights across DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, and Qwen/LLaMA.
    Strictly validates that copied weights are non-empty and verifies reconstruction norms.
    """
    if pretrained_attn is None:
        return

    # 1. Detect nested encoder architectures (RoBERTa / BERT)
    cls_name = pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__
    has_out_lin = hasattr(pretrained_attn, "out_lin")
    is_nested = False
    if (
        (
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self")
            and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query")
            and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output")
            and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense")
        )
        or (
            cls_name
            in [
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                "ElectraSelfAttention",
                "DebertaSelfAttention",
            ]
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
        )
        and not has_out_lin
    ):
        is_nested = True
    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

    # Inherit rotary embeddings if present
    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb"):
        self.rotary_emb = pretrained_attn.rotary_emb

    extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    # 2. GPT-2: Fused c_attn + c_proj
    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn") and pretrained_attn.c_attn is not None:
        c_attn_mod = pretrained_attn.c_attn
        if hasattr(c_attn_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_attn_mod, "merged", False):
            try:
                c_attn_mod.merge()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
        base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
        c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
        if c_w is not None:
            c_w = c_w.data.clone()
            if (
                (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
            ):
                c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
            split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
            bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
            c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (
                c_w[:split_size, :],
                c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["k"] = (
                c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
            )
            extracted["v"] = (
                c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
            )

        # GPT-2 Output: c_proj
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj") and pretrained_attn.c_proj is not None:
            c_proj_mod = pretrained_attn.c_proj
            if hasattr(c_proj_mod, "merge") and not getattr(c_proj_mod, "merged", False):
                try:
                    c_proj_mod.merge()
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
                    logger.warning(f"copy_weights failed: {e}")
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if o_w is not None:
                o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (
                    o_w,
                    bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                )

    # 3. RoBERTa / BERT nested self-attention (pretrained_attn.self)
    elif hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query"):
        s = pretrained_attn.self
        for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
            mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                extracted[proj_key] = (
                    mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                )
        # Output dense from outer container
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
            out_mod = pretrained_attn.output.dense
            extracted["o"] = (
                out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
            )

    # 4. Standard linear projections (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
    else:
        proj_candidates = {
            "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "W_q"],
            "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key", "W_k"],
            "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value", "W_v"],
            "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
        }
        for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(pretrained_attn, name):
                    mod = getattr(pretrained_attn, name)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                        )
                        break

    # 5. Fallback via UniversalAdapter
    missing = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing:
        try:
            from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

            adapter = UniversalAdapter()
            for k in missing:
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(pretrained_attn, k)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Verify all mandatory projections were extracted
    missing_after = [
        k
        for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
        if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
    ]
    if missing_after:
        logger.warning(
            f"BlockTransformerHIBA copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} "
            f"from module {pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__} ({pretrained_attn}). Proceeding with initialized weights."
        )

    # 6. Copy weights into target LoRALinear layers with strict dimension safety
    target_map = {
        "q": self.W_q,
        "k": self.W_k,
        "v": self.W_v,
        "o": self.out_proj,
    }

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is None:
                if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    # Inner nested self-attention bypasses out_proj; initialize to identity
                    nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                    if target_mod.bias is not None:
                        nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                continue

            if w.ndim != 2:
                w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)

            # Handle transposed orientation if necessary (only if shape does not match already)
            if (
                w.shape != target_mod.weight.shape
                and target_mod.weight.shape == w.t().contiguous().shape
            ):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            out_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
            in_d = min(target_mod.weight.size(1), w.size(1))

            target_mod.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                w[:out_d, :in_d].to(
                    device=target_mod.weight.device, dtype=target_mod.weight.dtype
                )
            )

            if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                min_b = min(target_mod.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                target_mod.bias.data[:min_b].copy_(
                    b[:min_b].to(device=target_mod.bias.device, dtype=target_mod.bias.dtype)
                )
            elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

            # Weight verification: ensure weights are non-trivial
            w_norm = float(torch.norm(target_mod.weight.data).item())
            if w_norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"Weight copy verification failed for projection '{proj_key}': norm too small ({w_norm:.2e})"
                )

            if self.freeze_base:
                target_mod.freeze_base_weights()

Extracts and copies pretrained weights across DistilBERT, RoBERTa/BERT, GPT-2, and Qwen/LLaMA. Strictly validates that copied weights are non-empty and verifies reconstruction norms.
