@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "eat", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True
)
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register(
    "eat_attention",
    supports_causal=True,
    supports_bidirectional=True,
    supports_gqa=True,
)
class EATBlock(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        d_model: int | Any = 768,
        n_heads: int = 12,
        prune_layer: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        layer_id: int | None = None,
        layer_idx: int | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn

        # ---- EAT-FIX-10: config None-safety ----
        cfg_obj = getattr(config, "text_config", config) if config is not None else None
        hidden_size = getattr(cfg_obj, "hidden_size", None) if cfg_obj is not None else None
        num_heads = getattr(cfg_obj, "num_attention_heads", None) if cfg_obj is not None else None

        if hidden_size is None and source_attn is not None:
            for cand in ("q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "c_attn", "qkv"):
                mod = getattr(source_attn, cand, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    w = mod.weight
                    hidden_size = w.shape[1] if w.ndim == 2 else None
                    break
                elif isinstance(mod, torch.Tensor) and mod.ndim == 2:
                    hidden_size = mod.shape[1]
                    break
        if hidden_size is None:
            hidden_size = kwargs.get(
                "hidden_size",
                kwargs.get("d_model", d_model if isinstance(d_model, int) else 768),
            )
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = kwargs.get(
                "num_attention_heads",
                kwargs.get("num_heads", kwargs.get("n_heads", n_heads)),
            )

        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank if rank is not None else 64)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        self.d_model = int(hidden_size)
        self.dim = self.d_model
        self.n_heads = int(num_heads)
        self.num_heads = self.n_heads

        # ---- num_kv_heads ----
        cfg_kv = None
        if config is not None:
            cfg_kv = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if cfg_kv is None:
            cfg_kv = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", self.num_heads)
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(int(cfg_kv), self.num_heads))
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        # ---- head_dim ----
        cfg_hd = getattr(config, "head_dim", None) if config else None
        self.head_dim = int(
            cfg_hd or kwargs.get("head_dim") or (self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads))
        )

        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        resolved_layer_id = (
            layer_id
            if layer_id is not None
            else (
                layer_idx
                if layer_idx is not None
                else kwargs.get("layer_id", kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
            )
        )
        self.layer_idx = int(resolved_layer_id)

        # ---- LoRA ----
        self.lora_rank = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                (
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
                    if comp_config is not None
                    else kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)
                ),
            )
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                (
                    getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
                    if comp_config is not None
                    else kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)
                ),
            )
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        model_type = str(getattr(config, "model_type", "") if config else "").lower()
        is_dec = False
        if config is not None:
            is_dec = bool(
                getattr(config, "is_decoder", False) or getattr(config, "is_causal", False)
            )
        if any(
            model_type.startswith(p)
            for p in (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
        ):
            is_dec = True
        if kwargs.get("is_decoder", False) or kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        if source_attn is not None:
            if getattr(source_attn, "is_decoder", False) or hasattr(source_attn, "c_attn"):
                is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder

        # EAT-FIX-03: never mark DistilBERT-like as nested
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        # ---- projections ----
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        dropout_p = 0.1
        if config is not None:
            dropout_p = float(
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "dropout",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                )
            )
        elif comp_config is not None:
            dropout_p = float(getattr(comp_config, "dropout", 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- EAT-FIX-07: pruning only when explicitly requested ----
        self.prune_layer: Any | None = None
        if prune_layer is not None:
            self.prune_layer = prune_layer
        else:
            enable_pruning = False
            keep_ratio = None
            if comp_config is not None:
                enable_pruning = bool(getattr(comp_config, "enable_pruning", False))
                keep_ratio = getattr(comp_config, "keep_ratio", None)
            if kwargs.get("enable_pruning", False):
                enable_pruning = True
            if kwargs.get("keep_ratio") is not None:
                keep_ratio = kwargs["keep_ratio"]

            if enable_pruning and ProgressiveTokenPruning is not None:
                effective_ratio = float(keep_ratio) if keep_ratio is not None else 0.7
                try:
                    self.prune_layer = ProgressiveTokenPruning(
                        config=config,
                        comp_config=comp_config,
                        keep_ratio=effective_ratio,
                        layer_id=self.layer_idx,
                        layer_idx=self.layer_idx,
                    )
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("EAT: ProgressiveTokenPruning not created: %s", e)
                    self.prune_layer = None

        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None
        )
        self.comp_config = comp_config

        # EAT-FIX-09
        self._orig_storage_bytes = 0
        self._comp_storage_bytes = 0

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def out_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def q_proj(self):
        return self.q_lin

    @property
    def k_proj(self):
        return self.k_lin

    @property
    def v_proj(self):
        return self.v_lin

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights (EAT-FIX-11)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, pretrained_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        if pretrained_attn is None:
            return

        try:
            for p in pretrained_attn.parameters():
                self._orig_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

        cls_name = pretrained_attn.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = any(
            hasattr(pretrained_attn, a)
            for a in ("out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj", "out_proj", "dense")
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb") and pretrained_attn.rotary_emb is not None:
            self.rotary_emb = pretrained_attn.rotary_emb

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        def _unwrap(m):
            return getattr(m, "base_layer", m)

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_attn") and pretrained_attn.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = pretrained_attn.c_attn
            if (
                hasattr(c_attn_mod, "merge")
                and callable(c_attn_mod.merge)
                and not getattr(c_attn_mod, "merged", False)
            ):
                try:
                    c_attn_mod.merge()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("c_attn merge skipped: %s", e)
            base_c = _unwrap(c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or hasattr(c_attn_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_c, "nf")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (c_w[:split], c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split : 2 * split],
                    c_b[split : 2 * split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split :],
                    c_b[2 * split :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "c_proj") and pretrained_attn.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = pretrained_attn.c_proj
                if (
                    hasattr(c_proj_mod, "merge")
                    and callable(c_proj_mod.merge)
                    and not getattr(c_proj_mod, "merged", False)
                ):
                    try:
                        c_proj_mod.merge()
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.debug("c_proj merge skipped: %s", e)
                base_o = _unwrap(c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                        or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. Nested (BERT / RoBERTa)
        elif hasattr(pretrained_attn, "self") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.self, "query"):
            s = pretrained_attn.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (
                        m.weight.data.clone(),
                        b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(pretrained_attn, "output") and hasattr(pretrained_attn.output, "dense"):
                d = pretrained_attn.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (
                    d.weight.data.clone(),
                    b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules (DistilBERT, Qwen, LLaMA)
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(pretrained_attn, name):
                        m = getattr(pretrained_attn, name)
                        if hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (
                                m.weight.data.clone(),
                                b.data.clone() if b is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        # UniversalAdapter fallback
        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(pretrained_attn, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            logger.warning(
                "EATBlock copy_weights: missing %s; using initialized weights",
                missing_after,
            )

        def _orient(w, in_dim):
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in (
                ("q", self.q_lin),
                ("k", self.k_lin),
                ("v", self.v_lin),
                ("o", self.out_lin),
            ):
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2:
                    w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if (
                    w.shape != target.weight.shape
                    and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape
                ):
                    w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data.zero_()
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(
                    w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype)
                )

                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data.zero_()
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

                # EAT-FIX-15: warn (not raise)
                n = float(torch.linalg.norm(target.weight.data.float()).item())
                if n < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("EAT: weight for '%s' near-zero norm (%.2e)", key, n)

                if self.freeze_base:
                    target.freeze_base_weights()

        # ---- EAT-FIX-09: track compressed ----
        try:
            for m in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
                for p in m.parameters():
                    self._comp_storage_bytes += p.numel() * p.element_size()
        except Exception:
            pass

    @classmethod
    def from_distilbert_pretrained(cls, old_attn, model_config=None, **kwargs):
        return cls(config=model_config, pretrained_attn=old_attn, **kwargs)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        dtype_in = hidden_states.dtype

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        kv_len = kv_input.shape[1]

        q = (
            self.q_lin(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k = (
            self.k_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v = (
            self.v_lin(kv_input)
            .view(B, kv_len, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # ---- RoPE ----
        cos = kwargs.get("cos")
        sin = kwargs.get("sin")
        pos_emb = (
            position_embeddings
            if position_embeddings is not None
            else kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        )
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) == 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]

        if cos is not None and sin is not None and not is_cross:
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None and not is_cross:
            try:
                pos_ids = position_ids
                if pos_ids is None:
                    pos_ids = (
                        torch.arange(kv_len, device=hidden_states.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
                    )
                cos_, sin_ = self.rotary_emb(k, seq_len=kv_len)
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos_, sin_, pos_ids)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("EAT: rotary apply failed: %s", e)

        # ---- GQA ----
        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)

        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and not is_cross and L > 1)

        # ---- EAT-FIX-05: unified causal + mask ----
        attn_mask_combined = None
        if eff_mask is not None or is_causal:
            if eff_mask is not None:
                if eff_mask.dim() == 2:
                    attn_mask_combined = (eff_mask > 0)[:, None, None, :]  # (B,1,1,N_kv)
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 3:
                    attn_mask_combined = (eff_mask > -1e4)[:, None, :, :]
                elif eff_mask.dim() == 4:
                    attn_mask_combined = eff_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    attn_mask_combined = None

            if is_causal:
                causal = torch.tril(
                    torch.ones(L, kv_len, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool)
                )[None, None, :, :]
                if attn_mask_combined is not None:
                    attn_mask_combined = attn_mask_combined & causal
                else:
                    attn_mask_combined = causal

        # ---- Dense attention ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        if attn_mask_combined is not None:
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~attn_mask_combined, -1e4)
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype_in)
            probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)
            attn_weights_for_return = probs
        else:
            # Fast SDPA path
            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k.half() if k.dtype == torch.float32 else k
                _vs = v.half() if v.dtype == torch.float32 else v
                _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    is_causal=is_causal,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                probs = None
                attn_weights_for_return = None
                _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out.to(dtype_in)
            except Exception:
                scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
                if is_causal:
                    causal = (
                        torch.triu(
                            torch.full(
                                (L, kv_len),
                                -1e4,
                                device=scores.device,
                                dtype=torch.float32,
                            ),
                            diagonal=1 + (kv_len - L),
                        )
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                        .unsqueeze(0)
                    )
                    scores = scores + causal
                scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4)
                probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype_in)
                probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
                probs = self.dropout(probs)
                attn_weights_for_return = probs
                _sdpa_out = None

        # ---- EAT-FIX-06: head_mask ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            hm = head_mask.to(dtype_in)
            if hm.dim() == 1:
                hm = hm.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 2:
                hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], hm.shape[1], 1, 1)
            elif hm.dim() == 3:
                hm = hm.unsqueeze(1)

            if probs is not None:
                probs = probs * hm
            else:
                _sdpa_out = _sdpa_out * hm

        # ---- Context ----
        if probs is not None:
            context = torch.matmul(probs.to(v.dtype), v)
        else:
            context = _sdpa_out

        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)

        # ---- Output projection ----
        # EAT-FIX-03: is_nested_encoder is only True for truly nested containers
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = context
        else:
            out = self.out_lin(context)

        # EAT-FIX-01: no padding/truncation hack
        if out.shape[-1] != D:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"EATBlock: output dim {out.shape[-1]} != hidden_size {D}. "
                f"Check num_heads * head_dim == hidden_size."
            )

        # ---- Progressive Token Pruning (eval only) ----
        if self.prune_layer is not None and not self.training:
            try:
                out, _ = self.prune_layer(out)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("EAT: pruning skipped: %s", e)

        return out, (attn_weights_for_return if output_attentions else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

class ExpertChoiceMoE (d_model: int,
n_experts: int = 2,
capacity_factor: float = 1.0,

Expand source code 
class ExpertChoiceMoE(nn.Module):
    r"""
    Expert Choice Routing MoE layer.
    Each expert selects top-k tokens with the highest affinity scores.
    Equipped with zero-initialized final layers for step-0 representation preservation.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        d_model: int,
        n_experts: int = 2,
        capacity_factor: float = 1.0,
        noise_std: float = 0.05,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.n_experts = n_experts
        self.capacity_factor = capacity_factor
        self.noise_std = noise_std
        self.aux_loss: torch.Tensor = torch.tensor(0.0)

        self.router_ln = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model)
        self.router = nn.Linear(d_model, n_experts, bias=False)
        nn.init.uniform_(self.router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)

        expert_hidden_dim = max(32, d_model // 4)
        self.experts = nn.ModuleList(
            [
                nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(d_model, expert_hidden_dim),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(expert_hidden_dim, d_model),
                )
                for _ in range(n_experts)
            ]
        )

        # Active MoE expert representation
        for expert in self.experts:
            nn.init.normal_(expert[2].weight, std=0.02)
            if expert[2].bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(expert[2].bias)

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        r"""Forward execution using Expert Choice routing."""
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_flat = x.view(-1, D)
        T = x_flat.size(0)

        if self.d_model == D:
            logits = self.router(self.router_ln(x_flat))
        else:
            normed = F.layer_norm(x_flat, (D,))
            if not hasattr(self, "_dynamic_router") or self._dynamic_router.in_features != D:
                self._dynamic_router = nn.Linear(D, self.n_experts, bias=False).to(
                    device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
                )
                nn.init.uniform_(self._dynamic_router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)
            logits = self._dynamic_router(normed)

        if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
            logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

        # FIXED: Converted to Causal Token-Choice MoE routing to prevent future token leakage.
        # Expert-Choice applies softmax over the sequence (dim=0), which catastrophically leaks future tokens into past states.
        # We now apply softmax over the experts (dim=-1) and select top-k experts per token.
        gate_scores = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype=logits.dtype)
        
        # Select top-2 experts per token (standard MoE)
        top_k_experts = min(2, self.n_experts)
        topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k_experts, dim=-1)

        out_flat = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
        for e in range(self.n_experts):
            # Find tokens assigned to expert e
            expert_mask = topk_indices == e
            token_indices = expert_mask.any(dim=-1).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
            
            if token_indices.numel() == 0:
                continue

            # Get the gating values for the selected tokens
            # expert_mask is [T, top_k], so we need to extract the matching topk_vals
            matched_vals = topk_vals[expert_mask]

            selected_x = x_flat[token_indices]
            
            if selected_x.size(-1) == self.d_model:
                expert_out = self.experts[e](selected_x)
            else:
                if (
                    not hasattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                    or getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}").in_features != D
                ):
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_in_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(D, self.d_model).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                    setattr(
                        self,
                        f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                        nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                    )
                proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")
                expert_out = proj_out(self.experts[e](proj_in(selected_x)))

            update_val = (
                expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
            ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)
            
            out_flat.index_add_(0, token_indices, update_val)

        # Auxiliary load balancing loss
        prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
        self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

        return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

Expert Choice Routing MoE layer. Each expert selects top-k tokens with the highest affinity scores. Equipped with zero-initialized final layers for step-0 representation preservation.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) ‑> torch.Tensor
Expand source code 
def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    r"""Forward execution using Expert Choice routing."""
    B, L, D = x.shape
    x_flat = x.view(-1, D)
    T = x_flat.size(0)

    if self.d_model == D:
        logits = self.router(self.router_ln(x_flat))
    else:
        normed = F.layer_norm(x_flat, (D,))
        if not hasattr(self, "_dynamic_router") or self._dynamic_router.in_features != D:
            self._dynamic_router = nn.Linear(D, self.n_experts, bias=False).to(
                device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype
            )
            nn.init.uniform_(self._dynamic_router.weight, -0.01, 0.01)
        logits = self._dynamic_router(normed)

    if self.training and self.noise_std > 0:
        logits = logits + torch.randn_like(logits) * self.noise_std

    # FIXED: Converted to Causal Token-Choice MoE routing to prevent future token leakage.
    # Expert-Choice applies softmax over the sequence (dim=0), which catastrophically leaks future tokens into past states.
    # We now apply softmax over the experts (dim=-1) and select top-k experts per token.
    gate_scores = F.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1).to(dtype=logits.dtype)
    
    # Select top-2 experts per token (standard MoE)
    top_k_experts = min(2, self.n_experts)
    topk_vals, topk_indices = torch.topk(gate_scores, top_k_experts, dim=-1)

    out_flat = torch.zeros_like(x_flat)
    for e in range(self.n_experts):
        # Find tokens assigned to expert e
        expert_mask = topk_indices == e
        token_indices = expert_mask.any(dim=-1).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
        
        if token_indices.numel() == 0:
            continue

        # Get the gating values for the selected tokens
        # expert_mask is [T, top_k], so we need to extract the matching topk_vals
        matched_vals = topk_vals[expert_mask]

        selected_x = x_flat[token_indices]
        
        if selected_x.size(-1) == self.d_model:
            expert_out = self.experts[e](selected_x)
        else:
            if (
                not hasattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
                or getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}").in_features != D
            ):
                setattr(
                    self,
                    f"_expert_in_proj_{e}",
                    nn.Linear(D, self.d_model).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                )
                setattr(
                    self,
                    f"_expert_out_proj_{e}",
                    nn.Linear(self.d_model, D).to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype),
                )
            proj_in = getattr(self, f"_expert_in_proj_{e}")
            proj_out = getattr(self, f"_expert_out_proj_{e}")
            expert_out = proj_out(self.experts[e](proj_in(selected_x)))

        update_val = (
            expert_out.to(out_flat.dtype) * matched_vals.unsqueeze(-1).to(out_flat.dtype)
        ).to(dtype=out_flat.dtype)
        
        out_flat.index_add_(0, token_indices, update_val)

    # Auxiliary load balancing loss
    prob_mean = F.softmax(logits.float() / 1.0, dim=-1).mean(dim=0)
    self.aux_loss = self.n_experts * torch.sum(prob_mean**2)

    return out_flat.view(B, L, D)

Forward execution using Expert Choice routing.

A placeholder identity operator that is argument-insensitive.

Args
-----=

any argument (unused)
kwargs
any keyword argument (unused)

Shape -----= - Input: :math:(*), where :math:* means any number of dimensions. - Output: :math:(*), same shape as the input.

Examples::

>>> m = nn.Identity(54, unused_argument1=0.1, unused_argument2=False)
>>> input = torch.randn(128, 20)
>>> output = m(input)
>>> print(output.size())

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("jamba", supports_causal=True, supports_bidirectional=True, supports_gqa=True)
class Jamba2Block(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(self, config: Any | None = None, d_model: int | None = None, n_heads: int | None = None, n_experts: int = 4, top_k: int = 2, rank: int | None = 64, pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None, old_attn: nn.Module | None = None, comp_config: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else old_attn
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config

        d_model = kwargs.get("d_model") or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or kwargs.get("dim") or d_model
        n_heads = kwargs.get("n_heads") or kwargs.get("num_heads") or kwargs.get("num_attention_heads") or n_heads

        if d_model is None and config is not None: d_model = getattr(config, "hidden_size", getattr(config, "d_model", getattr(config, "dim", 768)))
        if d_model is None and source_attn is not None:
            for attr in ("out_lin", "dense", "c_proj", "o_proj", "out_proj"):
                layer = getattr(source_attn, attr, None)
                if layer is not None and hasattr(layer, "out_features"):
                    d_model = layer.out_features
                    break
        if d_model is None: d_model = 768

        if n_heads is None and config is not None: n_heads = getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", getattr(config, "n_heads", 12))
        if n_heads is None and source_attn is not None: n_heads = getattr(source_attn, "num_attention_heads", getattr(source_attn, "n_heads", 12))
        if n_heads is None: n_heads = 12

        if config is not None:
            n_experts = int(getattr(config, "num_experts", getattr(config, "n_experts", n_experts)))
            top_k = int(getattr(config, "top_k", top_k))

        self.d_model = int(d_model)
        self.dim = self.d_model
        self.hidden_size = self.d_model
        self.n_heads = int(n_heads)
        self.num_heads = self.n_heads

        num_kv_heads = None
        if config is not None: num_kv_heads = getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if num_kv_heads is None: num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_kv_heads", kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads))
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(int(num_kv_heads), self.num_heads))

        self.head_dim = self.d_model // self.num_heads if self.num_heads > 0 else 64
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        self.supports_gqa = True

        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, kwargs.get("is_decoder"), source_attn, kwargs)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self._return_format: str = "llama"

        if config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(config, "lora_rank", getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(config, "lora_alpha", getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))))
        elif comp_config is not None:
            self.lora_rank = int(getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)))
            self.lora_alpha = float(getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)))
        else:
            self.lora_rank = int(kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16))
            self.lora_alpha = float(kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        self.source_attn = source_attn
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(source_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if source_attn is not None else None

        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(self.d_model, self.all_kv_head_size, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(self.all_head_size, self.d_model, bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha, freeze_base=self.freeze_base)

        self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj = self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin

        self.mamba = SVDMambaSub(self.d_model, config=config, rank=rank, is_decoder=self.is_decoder)
        self.moe = ExpertChoiceMoE(self.d_model, n_experts=n_experts, capacity_factor=1.0)

        # FIX 2: Pre-LayerNorms for Parallel Branches
        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model) # Mamba Norm
        self.norm3 = nn.LayerNorm(self.d_model) # MoE Norm

        # FIX 3: ReZero Scaling Parameters to ensure safe zero-shot KD initialization
        self.mamba_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.moe_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))

        dropout_p = float(getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1) if config else 0.1))
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        self._aux_loss_sum: float = 0.0
        self._aux_loss_count: int = 0

        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)
        self._return_format = _detect_return_format(source_attn, self.is_nested_encoder)

    @property
    def aux_loss(self) -> float:
        if self._aux_loss_count == 0: return 0.0
        return self._aux_loss_sum / self._aux_loss_count

    def reset_aux_loss(self) -> None:
        self._aux_loss_sum = 0.0
        self._aux_loss_count = 0

    def freeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for mod in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            if hasattr(mod, "freeze_base_weights"): mod.freeze_base_weights()

    def unfreeze_base_weights(self) -> None:
        for mod in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
            if hasattr(mod, "unfreeze_base_weights"): mod.unfreeze_base_weights()

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None: return
        cls_name = getattr(src, "__class__", type(src)).__name__
        has_out = any(hasattr(src, a) for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
        if cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (hasattr(src, "query") and not has_out): self.is_nested_encoder = True
        if hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src, "output"): self.is_nested_encoder = True

        extracted = {"q": (None, None), "k": (None, None), "v": (None, None), "o": (None, None)}

        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c, "base_layer", c)
            cw = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c, "weight", None))
            if cw is not None:
                cw = cw.data.clone()
                if (c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(c, "nf") or (cw.ndim == 2 and cw.shape[0] < cw.shape[1])): cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                split = cw.size(0) // 3
                cb = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c, "bias", None))
                cb = cb.data.clone() if cb is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (cw[:split], cb[:split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["k"] = (cw[split : 2 * split], cb[split : 2 * split] if cb is not None else None)
                extracted["v"] = (cw[2 * split :], cb[2 * split :] if cb is not None else None)

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                o = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(o, "base_layer", o)
                ow = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(o, "weight", None))
                if ow is not None:
                    ow = ow.data.clone()
                    if (o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(o, "nf") or (ow.ndim == 2 and ow.shape[0] < ow.shape[1])): ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                    bo = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(o, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (ow, bo.data.clone() if bo is not None else None)
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                m = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                    b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                    extracted[key] = (m.weight.data.clone(), b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                d = src.output.dense
                b = getattr(d, "bias", None)
                extracted["o"] = (d.weight.data.clone(), b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        m = getattr(src, n)
                        if m is not None and hasattr(m, "weight"):
                            w_data = m.weight.data.clone()
                            if (m.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D" or hasattr(m, "nf") or (key == "o" and w_data.ndim == 2 and w_data.shape[0] < w_data.shape[1])): w_data = w_data.t().contiguous()
                            b = getattr(m, "bias", None)
                            extracted[key] = (w_data, b.data.clone() if b is not None else None)
                            break

        missing = [k for k, (w, _) in extracted.items() if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None: extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Jamba2 UniversalAdapter fallback: %s", e)

        target_map = {"q": self.q_lin, "k": self.k_lin, "v": self.v_lin, "o": self.out_lin}

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target.weight)
                        if target.bias is not None: nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)
                    continue

                if w.ndim != 2: w = w.reshape(w.size(0), -1)
                if w.shape != target.weight.shape and w.t().contiguous().shape == target.weight.shape: w = w.t().contiguous()

                out_d = min(target.weight.size(0), w.size(0))
                in_d = min(target.weight.size(1), w.size(1))
                target.weight.data[:out_d, :in_d].copy_(w[:out_d, :in_d].to(target.weight.device, target.weight.dtype))

                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    mb = min(target.bias.numel(), b.numel())
                    target.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target.bias.device, target.bias.dtype))
                elif target.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target.bias)

                if self.freeze_base: target.freeze_base_weights()

        if hasattr(src, "rotary_emb") and src.rotary_emb is not None: self.rotary_emb = src.rotary_emb
        if self.freeze_base: self.freeze_base_weights()

    def _norm_dtype(self, norm: nn.LayerNorm, fallback: torch.dtype) -> torch.dtype:
        return norm.weight.dtype if getattr(norm, "weight", None) is not None else fallback

    def _format_output(self, out: torch.Tensor, attn_weights: torch.Tensor | None, present: Any | None) -> tuple:
        fmt = self._return_format
        if fmt == "bert": return (out, attn_weights) if attn_weights is not None else (out,)
        if fmt == "gpt2": return (out, present, attn_weights) if attn_weights is not None else (out, present)
        return (out, attn_weights, present) if attn_weights is not None else (out, present)

    def _normalize_head_mask(self, head_mask: torch.Tensor, attn: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        hm = head_mask.to(attn.dtype)
        if hm.dim() == 1: hm = hm.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 2:
            if hm.shape[-1] == self.num_heads: hm = hm.view(hm.shape[0], self.num_heads, 1, 1)
            else: hm = hm.view(1, hm.shape[-1], 1, 1)
        elif hm.dim() == 3: hm = hm.unsqueeze(2)
        return attn * hm

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ):
        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        layer_idx = int(kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0))
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        past_k, past_v, past_len, past_mamba = _read_cache(past_key_value, layer_idx)

        # =========================================================
        # FIX 1 & 2: Parallel Pre-LN Delta Logic
        # The module computes Parallel Deltas without re-adding the residual internally.
        # =========================================================
        
        # ---- Branch 1: Mamba (Pre-LN) ----
        mamba_out = 0
        present_mamba = None
        if self.mamba is not None:
            n1d = self._norm_dtype(self.norm1, target_dtype)
            x_norm1 = self.norm1(hidden_states.to(n1d)).to(target_dtype)
            m_out, present_mamba = self.mamba(x_norm1, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_mamba)
            mamba_out = m_out.to(target_dtype) * self.mamba_scale # FIX 3: ReZero

        # ---- Branch 2: Attention (Expects inputs to already be Pre-LN normalized by parent block) ----
        q = self.q_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.k_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.v_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            cos = cos.narrow(-2, past_len, L) if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else cos
            sin = sin.narrow(-2, past_len, L) if sin.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else sin
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)
        elif self.rotary_emb is not None:
            pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
            if pos_ids is None: pos_ids = torch.arange(past_len, past_len + L, device=hidden_states.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
            cos = sin = None
            for call in (lambda: self.rotary_emb(k, seq_len=L), lambda: self.rotary_emb(k, pos_ids), lambda: self.rotary_emb(pos_ids)):
                try: cos, sin = call(); break
                except Exception: continue
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        if past_k is not None:
            k = torch.cat([past_k.to(k.dtype), k], dim=-2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v.to(v.dtype), v], dim=-2)

        present_key_value = _write_cache(k, v, present_mamba, past_key_value, layer_idx)

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            k_exp = repeat_kv(k, self.kv_repeat)
            v_exp = repeat_kv(v, self.kv_repeat)
        else:
            k_exp, v_exp = k, v

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder and L > 1)
        use_sdpa = (head_mask is None and not output_attentions and attention_mask is None and past_len == 0)

        attn_weights = None
        if use_sdpa:
            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k_exp.half() if k_exp.dtype == torch.float32 else k_exp
                _vs = v_exp.half() if v_exp.dtype == torch.float32 else v_exp
                _sdpa_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(_qs, _ks, _vs, is_causal=is_causal, dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0)
                attn_out = _sdpa_out.to(target_dtype).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("Jamba2 SDPA path failed: %s", e)
                use_sdpa = False

        if not use_sdpa:
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k_exp.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
            if is_causal:
                kv_len = k_exp.shape[-2]
                causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, kv_len, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=torch.bool), diagonal=past_len)
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)
            if attention_mask is not None:
                if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                    valid = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, k_exp.shape[-2], hidden_states.device)
                    if valid is not None: scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 3: scores = scores + attention_mask.unsqueeze(1).float()
                elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                    if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool: scores = scores.masked_fill(~attention_mask, -1e4)
                    else: scores = scores + attention_mask.float()
            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(v_exp.dtype)
            probs = self.dropout(torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0))
            if head_mask is not None: probs = self._normalize_head_mask(head_mask, probs)
            attn_out = torch.matmul(probs, v_exp).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
            attn_weights = probs if output_attentions else None

        attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn_delta = attn_out if self.is_nested_encoder else self.out_lin(attn_out)

        # ---- Branch 3: MoE (Pre-LN) ----
        moe_out = 0
        if self.moe is not None:
            n3d = self._norm_dtype(self.norm3, target_dtype)
            x_norm3 = self.norm3(hidden_states.to(n3d)).to(target_dtype)
            moe_res = self.moe(x_norm3)
            if isinstance(moe_res, (tuple, list)): moe_res = moe_res[0]
            moe_out = moe_res.to(target_dtype) * self.moe_scale # FIX 3: ReZero
            self._accumulate_aux_loss()

        # FIX 1: Return the clean parallel delta ONLY (Outer block will add the residual!)
        final_delta = mamba_out + attn_delta + moe_out
        
        return self._format_output(final_delta, attn_weights, present_key_value)

    def _accumulate_aux_loss(self) -> None:
        aux = getattr(self.moe, "aux_loss", 0.0)
        v = float(aux.item()) if torch.is_tensor(aux) else float(aux)
        self._aux_loss_sum += v
        self._aux_loss_count += 1

    def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        def _sz(p: Any) -> int: return p.numel() * p.element_size() if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)) else 0
        out: dict[str, int] = {}
        for name, m in (("q_lin", self.q_lin), ("k_lin", self.k_lin), ("v_lin", self.v_lin), ("out_lin", self.out_lin), ("mamba", self.mamba), ("moe", self.moe)):
            out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters() if m is not None)
        out["total"] = sum(out.values())
        return out

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

prop aux_loss : float
Expand source code 
@property
def aux_loss(self) -> float:
    if self._aux_loss_count == 0: return 0.0
    return self._aux_loss_sum / self._aux_loss_count

def storage_bytes(self) -> dict[str, int]:
    def _sz(p: Any) -> int: return p.numel() * p.element_size() if isinstance(p, (nn.Parameter, torch.Tensor)) else 0
    out: dict[str, int] = {}
    for name, m in (("q_lin", self.q_lin), ("k_lin", self.k_lin), ("v_lin", self.v_lin), ("out_lin", self.out_lin), ("mamba", self.mamba), ("moe", self.moe)):
        out[name] = sum(_sz(p) for p in m.parameters() if m is not None)
    out["total"] = sum(out.values())
    return out

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("lola_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class LoLAAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        model_config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        cfg = config if config is not None else model_config

        def _cfg_get(name: str, fallback: Any) -> Any:
            if cfg is not None:
                val = getattr(cfg, name, None)
                if val is not None:
                    return val
            return fallback

        actual_rank = rank if rank is not None else _cfg_get("rank", 32)
        super().__init__(cfg, actual_rank)

        cfg_head_dim = getattr(cfg, "head_dim", None) if cfg is not None else None
        if cfg is not None:
            self.adapter = ModelAdapter(cfg)
            self.num_heads = self.adapter.num_heads or getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", 12)
            self.num_kv_heads = self.adapter.num_key_value_heads or getattr(
                cfg, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads
            )
            self.hidden_size = self.adapter.hidden_size or getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", 768)
            self.head_dim = int(
                cfg_head_dim
                or kwargs.get("head_dim")
                or self.adapter.head_dim
                or (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads)
            )
            self.kv_repeat = self.adapter.kv_repeat or 1
        else:
            self.adapter = None
            self.num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
            self.num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
            self.hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
            self.head_dim = int(kwargs.get("head_dim") or (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads))
            self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        _mt = str(getattr(cfg, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if cfg else ""
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        self.is_decoder = (
            (
                getattr(cfg, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(cfg, "causal", False)
                or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
                or bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
            )
            if cfg
            else bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        )

        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.rank: int = actual_rank
        self.lora_rank: int = _cfg_get("lora_rank", lora_rank)

        self.q_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )

        self.dropout: nn.Dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

        max_seq_len: int = (
            getattr(comp_config, "max_seq_len", 128) if comp_config is not None else 128
        )
        low_rank_cache_size: int = (
            getattr(comp_config, "low_rank_cache_size", 16) if comp_config is not None else 16
        )
        self.cache: LowRankCache = LowRankCache(max_len=max_seq_len, low_rank_k=low_rank_cache_size)
        self.LR_scale: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(4.0))
        self.sparse_gate: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-3.0))
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if pretrained_attn is not None else None
        )

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    def reset_cache(self) -> None:
        if hasattr(self, "cache") and hasattr(self.cache, "reset"):
            self.cache.reset()

    def _set_lowrank_factors(
        self,
        lowrank: LowRankLinear,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None,
    ) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(self.rank, U.size(1), Vt.size(0))
            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            lowrank.U.data.zero_()
            lowrank.V.data.zero_()
            out_d, in_d = lowrank.U.size(0), lowrank.V.size(1)
            lowrank.U.data[: min(out_d, U.size(0)), :r].copy_(
                (U[: min(out_d, U.size(0)), :r] * sqrt_s).to(lowrank.U.dtype)
            )
            lowrank.V.data[:r, : min(in_d, Vt.size(1))].copy_(
                (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, : min(in_d, Vt.size(1))]).to(lowrank.V.dtype)
            )
            if lowrank.bias is not None and bias is not None:
                b_val = bias.data if isinstance(bias, nn.Parameter) else bias
                lowrank.bias.data[: min(len(lowrank.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                    b_val[: min(len(lowrank.bias), len(b_val))]
                )

            lowrank.U.requires_grad = False
            lowrank.V.requires_grad = False
            if lowrank.bias is not None:
                lowrank.bias.requires_grad = False
            if lowrank.lora_rank > 0 and lowrank.lora_A is not None:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(lowrank.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
                lowrank.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if lowrank.lora_rank > 0 and lowrank.lora_B is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(lowrank.lora_B)
                lowrank.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
                k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
                v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
                q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
                extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
                extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                p_cls = getattr(c_proj_mod, "__class__", type(c_proj_mod)).__name__
                b_cls = getattr(base_o, "__class__", type(base_o)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or ("Conv1D" in b_cls)
                    or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or (
                        w_o.dim() == 2
                        and (
                            w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1]
                            or (
                                w_o.shape[0] == self.hidden_size
                                and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
                            )
                        )
                    )
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None)

        # RoBERTa / BERT nested
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None:
                    base_mod = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                    if hasattr(base_mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            base_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            (
                                base_mod.bias.data.clone()
                                if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                                else None
                            ),
                        )

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            base_dense = getattr(dense, "base_layer", dense)
            if hasattr(base_dense, "weight"):
                extracted["o"] = (
                    base_dense.weight.data.clone(),
                    (
                        base_dense.bias.data.clone()
                        if getattr(base_dense, "bias", None) is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                )

        # Modern decoders + DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        base_l = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
                        if hasattr(base_l, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                base_l.weight.data.clone(),
                                (
                                    base_l.bias.data.clone()
                                    if getattr(base_l, "bias", None) is not None
                                    else None
                                ),
                            )
                            break

        # Nested encoder detection
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning("Module copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights.")

        for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has "
                        f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f})"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for output projection has "
                    f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f})"
                )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    self._set_lowrank_factors(target, w_oriented, b)
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.U.size(0), target.V.size(1), self.rank)
                    target.U.data.zero_()
                    target.V.data.zero_()
                    target.U.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    target.V.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, N_q, D = hidden_states.shape
        N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # RoPE
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if (
            position_embeddings is not None
            and isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list))
            and len(position_embeddings) >= 2
        ):
            cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = d**-0.5

        _cfg_mt = (
            str(getattr(self.config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            if self.config is not None
            else ""
        )
        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or (getattr(self.config, "is_decoder", False) if self.config is not None else False)
            or any(
                _cfg_mt.startswith(p)
                for p in (
                    "gpt",
                    "qwen",
                    "llama",
                    "mistral",
                    "gemma",
                    "phi",
                    "opt",
                    "bloom",
                    "falcon",
                    "olmo",
                    "mpt",
                    "stablelm",
                )
            )
        )

        scores_lr = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4:
                if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~mask, -10000.0)
                else:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.to(scores_lr.dtype)
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.unsqueeze(1).to(scores_lr.dtype)
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
                if valid_m is not None:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                        (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                    )
            else:
                valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
                if valid_m is not None:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                        (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                    )

        if is_decoder and N_k >= N_q:
            causal_tril = (
                torch.tril(
                    torch.ones((N_q, N_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                    diagonal=N_k - N_q,
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -10000.0)

        # =============================================================
        # LL-FIX-01/02/03: cache only for streaming decoders with past_key_value.
        # For encoders (bidirectional), the cache would mix tokens across batches
        # and hold large view-retained buffers, causing ~70% VRAM bloat.
        # =============================================================
        use_cache = bool(is_decoder and past_key_value is not None and not is_cross_attention)

        if use_cache:
            self.cache.update(K, V)
            cached_k, cached_v = self.cache.get_cached()
        else:
            # Ensure stale state is dropped so batches stay isolated.
            self.cache.reset()
            cached_k, cached_v = None, None

        if cached_k is not None and cached_v is not None and cached_k.size(0) == (B * H):
            C = cached_k.size(1)
            K_cache = cached_k.view(B, H, C, d)
            V_cache = cached_v.view(B, H, C, d)
            scores_cache = (
                torch.matmul(Q, K_cache.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous())
                * scale
                * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.sparse_gate, min=-10.0, max=10.0))
            )
            scores = torch.cat(
                [
                    scores_lr * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.LR_scale, min=-10.0, max=10.0)),
                    scores_cache,
                ],
                dim=-1,
            )
            V_comb = torch.cat([V, V_cache], dim=2)
        else:
            scores = scores_lr
            V_comb = V

        attn = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(dtype)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn = attn * head_mask

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V_comb)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        return output, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Every custom attention module must inherit from this.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def copy_weights(self,
