Expand source code 
def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
    from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

    extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
        "q": (None, None),
        "k": (None, None),
        "v": (None, None),
        "o": (None, None),
    }

    for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
        try:
            w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
            if w is not None:
                extracted[proj] = (w, b)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # GPT-2 fused c_attn
    if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
        extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
    ):
        c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
        base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
        weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
        if weight is not None:
            weight = weight.data.clone()
            bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
            bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
            if (
                (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
            ):
                weight = weight.t().contiguous()
            split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
            q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
            k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
            v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
            q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
            v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
            extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
            extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
            extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

    if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
        base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
        w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
        if w_o is not None:
            w_o = w_o.data.clone()
            p_cls = getattr(c_proj_mod, "__class__", type(c_proj_mod)).__name__
            b_cls = getattr(base_o, "__class__", type(base_o)).__name__
            if (
                ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                or ("Conv1D" in b_cls)
                or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                or (
                    w_o.dim() == 2
                    and (
                        w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1]
                        or (
                            w_o.shape[0] == self.hidden_size
                            and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
                        )
                    )
                )
            ):
                w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
            b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
            extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None)

    # RoBERTa / BERT nested
    if hasattr(src, "self") and (
        extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
    ):
        s = src.self
        for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
            mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
            if mod is not None:
                base_mod = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                if hasattr(base_mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        base_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        (
                            base_mod.bias.data.clone()
                            if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                            else None
                        ),
                    )

    if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        dense = src.output.dense
        base_dense = getattr(dense, "base_layer", dense)
        if hasattr(base_dense, "weight"):
            extracted["o"] = (
                base_dense.weight.data.clone(),
                (
                    base_dense.bias.data.clone()
                    if getattr(base_dense, "bias", None) is not None
                    else None
                ),
            )

    # Modern decoders + DistilBERT
    named_map = {
        "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
        "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
        "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
        "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
    }
    for proj, names in named_map.items():
        if extracted[proj][0] is None:
            for name in names:
                if hasattr(src, name):
                    layer = getattr(src, name)
                    base_l = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
                    if hasattr(base_l, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj] = (
                            base_l.weight.data.clone(),
                            (
                                base_l.bias.data.clone()
                                if getattr(base_l, "bias", None) is not None
                                else None
                            ),
                        )
                        break

    # Nested encoder detection
    cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
    has_out_proj = any(
        hasattr(src, attr)
        for attr in (
            "dense",
            "out_proj",
            "c_proj",
            "out_lin",
            "o_proj",
            "wo",
            "o",
            "proj",
        )
    )
    is_nested = False
    if (
        hasattr(src, "self")
        and hasattr(src.self, "query")
        or (
            cls_name
            in (
                "BertSelfAttention",
                "RobertaSelfAttention",
                "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
            )
            or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
        )
        and not has_out_proj
    ):
        is_nested = True
    self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

    missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
    if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
        missing.append("o")
    if missing:
        logger.warning("Module copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights.")

    for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
        w = extracted[proj_key][0]
        if w is not None:
            norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
            if norm < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has "
                    f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f})"
                )

    if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
        norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
        if norm_o < 1e-4:
            logger.warning(
                f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for output projection has "
                f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f})"
            )

    def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
        if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
            return w.t().contiguous()
        return w

    with torch.no_grad():
        for proj_key, target in [
            ("q", self.q_proj),
            ("k", self.k_proj),
            ("v", self.v_proj),
            ("o", self.out_proj),
        ]:
            w, b = extracted[proj_key]
            if w is not None:
                in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                self._set_lowrank_factors(target, w_oriented, b)
            elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                min_d = min(target.U.size(0), target.V.size(1), self.rank)
                target.U.data.zero_()
                target.V.data.zero_()
                target.U.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                target.V.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                if target.bias is not None:
                    target.bias.data.zero_()

    if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
        self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

Universal fallback: copy pretrained weights from old attention layer.

def forward(
    self,
    hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
    past_key_value: Any | None = None,
    output_attentions: bool | None = False,
    output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
    return_dict: bool | None = True,
    *args: Any,
    **kwargs: Any,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
    key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
    is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
    kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

    B, N_q, D = hidden_states.shape
    N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
    H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
    device = hidden_states.device
    dtype = hidden_states.dtype

    Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
    V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

    # RoPE
    position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
    if (
        position_embeddings is not None
        and isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list))
        and len(position_embeddings) >= 2
    ):
        cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
        Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

    if self.kv_repeat > 1:
        K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
        V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

    scale = d**-0.5

    _cfg_mt = (
        str(getattr(self.config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
        if self.config is not None
        else ""
    )
    is_decoder = (
        self.is_decoder
        or (getattr(self.config, "is_decoder", False) if self.config is not None else False)
        or any(
            _cfg_mt.startswith(p)
            for p in (
                "gpt",
                "qwen",
                "llama",
                "mistral",
                "gemma",
                "phi",
                "opt",
                "bloom",
                "falcon",
                "olmo",
                "mpt",
                "stablelm",
            )
        )
    )

    scores_lr = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

    mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
    if mask is not None:
        if mask.dim() == 4:
            if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~mask, -10000.0)
            else:
                scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.to(scores_lr.dtype)
        elif mask.dim() == 3:
            scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.unsqueeze(1).to(scores_lr.dtype)
        elif mask.dim() == 2:
            valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
            if valid_m is not None:
                scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                    (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                )
        else:
            valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
            if valid_m is not None:
                scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                    (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                )

    if is_decoder and N_k >= N_q:
        causal_tril = (
            torch.tril(
                torch.ones((N_q, N_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                diagonal=N_k - N_q,
            )
            .unsqueeze(0)
            .unsqueeze(0)
        )
        scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -10000.0)

    # =============================================================
    # LL-FIX-01/02/03: cache only for streaming decoders with past_key_value.
    # For encoders (bidirectional), the cache would mix tokens across batches
    # and hold large view-retained buffers, causing ~70% VRAM bloat.
    # =============================================================
    use_cache = bool(is_decoder and past_key_value is not None and not is_cross_attention)

    if use_cache:
        self.cache.update(K, V)
        cached_k, cached_v = self.cache.get_cached()
    else:
        # Ensure stale state is dropped so batches stay isolated.
        self.cache.reset()
        cached_k, cached_v = None, None

    if cached_k is not None and cached_v is not None and cached_k.size(0) == (B * H):
        C = cached_k.size(1)
        K_cache = cached_k.view(B, H, C, d)
        V_cache = cached_v.view(B, H, C, d)
        scores_cache = (
            torch.matmul(Q, K_cache.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous())
            * scale
            * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.sparse_gate, min=-10.0, max=10.0))
        )
        scores = torch.cat(
            [
                scores_lr * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.LR_scale, min=-10.0, max=10.0)),
                scores_cache,
            ],
            dim=-1,
        )
        V_comb = torch.cat([V, V_cache], dim=2)
    else:
        scores = scores_lr
        V_comb = V

    attn = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(dtype)
    attn = self.dropout(attn)

    if head_mask is not None:
        if head_mask.dim() == 1:
            head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
        elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
            head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
        elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
            head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
        attn = attn * head_mask

    context = torch.matmul(attn, V_comb)
    context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)

    if self.is_nested_encoder:
        output = context
    else:
        output = self.out_proj(context)

    return output, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Must return (output, attention_weights_or_None).

        self.cache.reset()

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("lola_attention", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class LoLAAttention(BaseAttention):
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        model_config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        dropout: float = 0.1,
        comp_config: Any | None = None,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        cfg = config if config is not None else model_config

        def _cfg_get(name: str, fallback: Any) -> Any:
            if cfg is not None:
                val = getattr(cfg, name, None)
                if val is not None:
                    return val
            return fallback

        actual_rank = rank if rank is not None else _cfg_get("rank", 32)
        super().__init__(cfg, actual_rank)

        cfg_head_dim = getattr(cfg, "head_dim", None) if cfg is not None else None
        if cfg is not None:
            self.adapter = ModelAdapter(cfg)
            self.num_heads = self.adapter.num_heads or getattr(cfg, "num_attention_heads", 12)
            self.num_kv_heads = self.adapter.num_key_value_heads or getattr(
                cfg, "num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads
            )
            self.hidden_size = self.adapter.hidden_size or getattr(cfg, "hidden_size", 768)
            self.head_dim = int(
                cfg_head_dim
                or kwargs.get("head_dim")
                or self.adapter.head_dim
                or (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads)
            )
            self.kv_repeat = self.adapter.kv_repeat or 1
        else:
            self.adapter = None
            self.num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
            self.num_kv_heads = kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads)
            self.hidden_size = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
            self.head_dim = int(kwargs.get("head_dim") or (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads))
            self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        _mt = str(getattr(cfg, "model_type", "") or "").lower() if cfg else ""
        _decoder_prefixes = (
            "gpt",
            "qwen",
            "llama",
            "mistral",
            "gemma",
            "phi",
            "opt",
            "bloom",
            "falcon",
            "olmo",
            "mpt",
            "stablelm",
        )
        self.is_decoder = (
            (
                getattr(cfg, "is_decoder", False)
                or getattr(cfg, "causal", False)
                or any(_mt.startswith(p) for p in _decoder_prefixes)
                or bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
            )
            if cfg
            else bool(kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        )

        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.is_nested_encoder: bool = False

        self.rank: int = actual_rank
        self.lora_rank: int = _cfg_get("lora_rank", lora_rank)

        self.q_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.all_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LowRankLinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            self.rank,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            bias=True,
        )

        self.dropout: nn.Dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

        max_seq_len: int = (
            getattr(comp_config, "max_seq_len", 128) if comp_config is not None else 128
        )
        low_rank_cache_size: int = (
            getattr(comp_config, "low_rank_cache_size", 16) if comp_config is not None else 16
        )
        self.cache: LowRankCache = LowRankCache(max_len=max_seq_len, low_rank_k=low_rank_cache_size)
        self.LR_scale: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(4.0))
        self.sparse_gate: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-3.0))
        self.rotary_emb = (
            getattr(pretrained_attn, "rotary_emb", None) if pretrained_attn is not None else None
        )

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    def reset_cache(self) -> None:
        if hasattr(self, "cache") and hasattr(self.cache, "reset"):
            self.cache.reset()

    def _set_lowrank_factors(
        self,
        lowrank: LowRankLinear,
        weight: torch.Tensor,
        bias: torch.Tensor | None,
    ) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            W = weight.float()
            U, S, Vt = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)
            r = min(self.rank, U.size(1), Vt.size(0))
            sqrt_s = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(S[:r], min=1e-12))
            lowrank.U.data.zero_()
            lowrank.V.data.zero_()
            out_d, in_d = lowrank.U.size(0), lowrank.V.size(1)
            lowrank.U.data[: min(out_d, U.size(0)), :r].copy_(
                (U[: min(out_d, U.size(0)), :r] * sqrt_s).to(lowrank.U.dtype)
            )
            lowrank.V.data[:r, : min(in_d, Vt.size(1))].copy_(
                (sqrt_s.unsqueeze(1) * Vt[:r, : min(in_d, Vt.size(1))]).to(lowrank.V.dtype)
            )
            if lowrank.bias is not None and bias is not None:
                b_val = bias.data if isinstance(bias, nn.Parameter) else bias
                lowrank.bias.data[: min(len(lowrank.bias), len(b_val))].copy_(
                    b_val[: min(len(lowrank.bias), len(b_val))]
                )

            lowrank.U.requires_grad = False
            lowrank.V.requires_grad = False
            if lowrank.bias is not None:
                lowrank.bias.requires_grad = False
            if lowrank.lora_rank > 0 and lowrank.lora_A is not None:
                nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(lowrank.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
                lowrank.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            if lowrank.lora_rank > 0 and lowrank.lora_B is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(lowrank.lora_B)
                lowrank.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        for proj in ["q", "k", "v", "o"]:
            try:
                w, b = UniversalAdapter.get_weight_bias(src, proj)
                if w is not None:
                    extracted[proj] = (w, b)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            weight = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if weight is not None:
                weight = weight.data.clone()
                bias = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                bias_data = bias.data.clone() if bias is not None else None
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (weight.dim() == 2 and weight.shape[0] < weight.shape[1])
                ):
                    weight = weight.t().contiguous()
                split_size = weight.shape[0] // 3
                q_w = weight[:split_size, :]
                k_w = weight[split_size : 2 * split_size, :]
                v_w = weight[2 * split_size :, :]
                q_b = bias_data[:split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                k_b = bias_data[split_size : 2 * split_size] if bias_data is not None else None
                v_b = bias_data[2 * split_size :] if bias_data is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (q_w, q_b)
                extracted["k"] = (k_w, k_b)
                extracted["v"] = (v_w, v_b)

        if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
            base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
            w_o = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
            if w_o is not None:
                w_o = w_o.data.clone()
                p_cls = getattr(c_proj_mod, "__class__", type(c_proj_mod)).__name__
                b_cls = getattr(base_o, "__class__", type(base_o)).__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or ("Conv1D" in b_cls)
                    or hasattr(c_proj_mod, "nf")
                    or hasattr(base_o, "nf")
                    or (
                        w_o.dim() == 2
                        and (
                            w_o.shape[0] < w_o.shape[1]
                            or (
                                w_o.shape[0] == self.hidden_size
                                and w_o.shape[1] == self.hidden_size
                            )
                        )
                    )
                ):
                    w_o = w_o.t().contiguous()
                b_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                extracted["o"] = (w_o, b_o.data.clone() if b_o is not None else None)

        # RoBERTa / BERT nested
        if hasattr(src, "self") and (
            extracted["q"][0] is None or extracted["k"][0] is None or extracted["v"][0] is None
        ):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None:
                    base_mod = getattr(mod, "base_layer", mod)
                    if hasattr(base_mod, "weight"):
                        extracted[proj_key] = (
                            base_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                            (
                                base_mod.bias.data.clone()
                                if getattr(base_mod, "bias", None) is not None
                                else None
                            ),
                        )

        if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense") and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            dense = src.output.dense
            base_dense = getattr(dense, "base_layer", dense)
            if hasattr(base_dense, "weight"):
                extracted["o"] = (
                    base_dense.weight.data.clone(),
                    (
                        base_dense.bias.data.clone()
                        if getattr(base_dense, "bias", None) is not None
                        else None
                    ),
                )

        # Modern decoders + DistilBERT
        named_map = {
            "q": ["q_proj", "q_lin", "query"],
            "k": ["k_proj", "k_lin", "key"],
            "v": ["v_proj", "v_lin", "value"],
            "o": ["o_proj", "out_proj", "out_lin", "dense"],
        }
        for proj, names in named_map.items():
            if extracted[proj][0] is None:
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        layer = getattr(src, name)
                        base_l = getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)
                        if hasattr(base_l, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj] = (
                                base_l.weight.data.clone(),
                                (
                                    base_l.bias.data.clone()
                                    if getattr(base_l, "bias", None) is not None
                                    else None
                                ),
                            )
                            break

        # Nested encoder detection
        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_proj = any(
            hasattr(src, attr)
            for attr in (
                "dense",
                "out_proj",
                "c_proj",
                "out_lin",
                "o_proj",
                "wo",
                "o",
                "proj",
            )
        )
        is_nested = False
        if (
            hasattr(src, "self")
            and hasattr(src.self, "query")
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and not has_out_proj)
            )
            and not has_out_proj
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        missing = [k for k in ["q", "k", "v"] if extracted[k][0] is None]
        if not self.is_nested_encoder and extracted["o"][0] is None:
            missing.append("o")
        if missing:
            logger.warning("Module copy_weights warning. Proceeding with initialized weights.")

        for proj_key in ["q", "k", "v"]:
            w = extracted[proj_key][0]
            if w is not None:
                norm = torch.linalg.norm(w.float())
                if norm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for {proj_key} has "
                        f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm.item():.6f})"
                    )

        if extracted["o"][0] is not None:
            norm_o = torch.linalg.norm(extracted["o"][0].float())
            if norm_o < 1e-4:
                logger.warning(
                    f"LoLAAttention extracted weight for output projection has "
                    f"near-zero Frobenius norm ({norm_o.item():.6f})"
                )

        def _orient(w: torch.Tensor, in_dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
            if w.dim() == 2 and w.shape[1] != in_dim and w.shape[0] == in_dim:
                return w.t().contiguous()
            return w

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target in [
                ("q", self.q_proj),
                ("k", self.k_proj),
                ("v", self.v_proj),
                ("o", self.out_proj),
            ]:
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is not None:
                    in_dim = self.all_head_size if proj_key == "o" else self.hidden_size
                    w_oriented = _orient(w, in_dim)
                    self._set_lowrank_factors(target, w_oriented, b)
                elif proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                    min_d = min(target.U.size(0), target.V.size(1), self.rank)
                    target.U.data.zero_()
                    target.V.data.zero_()
                    target.U.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    target.V.data[:min_d, :min_d].fill_diagonal_(1.0)
                    if target.bias is not None:
                        target.bias.data.zero_()

        if hasattr(src, "is_decoder"):
            self.is_decoder = bool(src.is_decoder)

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        key_value_states = kwargs.get("key_value_states", encoder_hidden_states)
        is_cross_attention = key_value_states is not None
        kv_input = key_value_states if is_cross_attention else hidden_states

        B, N_q, D = hidden_states.shape
        N_k = kv_input.shape[1]
        H, d = self.num_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        Q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, N_q, H, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        K = self.k_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        V = self.v_proj(kv_input).view(B, N_k, self.num_kv_heads, d).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # RoPE
        position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if (
            position_embeddings is not None
            and isinstance(position_embeddings, (tuple, list))
            and len(position_embeddings) >= 2
        ):
            cos, sin = position_embeddings[0], position_embeddings[1]
            Q, K = apply_rotary_pos_emb(Q, K, cos, sin, kwargs.get("position_ids"))

        if self.kv_repeat > 1:
            K = repeat_kv(K, self.kv_repeat)
            V = repeat_kv(V, self.kv_repeat)

        scale = d**-0.5

        _cfg_mt = (
            str(getattr(self.config, "model_type", "") or "").lower()
            if self.config is not None
            else ""
        )
        is_decoder = (
            self.is_decoder
            or (getattr(self.config, "is_decoder", False) if self.config is not None else False)
            or any(
                _cfg_mt.startswith(p)
                for p in (
                    "gpt",
                    "qwen",
                    "llama",
                    "mistral",
                    "gemma",
                    "phi",
                    "opt",
                    "bloom",
                    "falcon",
                    "olmo",
                    "mpt",
                    "stablelm",
                )
            )
        )

        scores_lr = torch.matmul(Q, K.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        mask = attention_mask if attention_mask is not None else kwargs.get("mask")
        if mask is not None:
            if mask.dim() == 4:
                if mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~mask, -10000.0)
                else:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.to(scores_lr.dtype)
            elif mask.dim() == 3:
                scores_lr = scores_lr + mask.unsqueeze(1).to(scores_lr.dtype)
            elif mask.dim() == 2:
                valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
                if valid_m is not None:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                        (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                    )
            else:
                valid_m = get_token_valid_mask(mask, B, N_k, device)
                if valid_m is not None:
                    scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(
                        (~valid_m).unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -10000.0
                    )

        if is_decoder and N_k >= N_q:
            causal_tril = (
                torch.tril(
                    torch.ones((N_q, N_k), device=device, dtype=torch.bool),
                    diagonal=N_k - N_q,
                )
                .unsqueeze(0)
                .unsqueeze(0)
            )
            scores_lr = scores_lr.masked_fill(~causal_tril, -10000.0)

        # =============================================================
        # LL-FIX-01/02/03: cache only for streaming decoders with past_key_value.
        # For encoders (bidirectional), the cache would mix tokens across batches
        # and hold large view-retained buffers, causing ~70% VRAM bloat.
        # =============================================================
        use_cache = bool(is_decoder and past_key_value is not None and not is_cross_attention)

        if use_cache:
            self.cache.update(K, V)
            cached_k, cached_v = self.cache.get_cached()
        else:
            # Ensure stale state is dropped so batches stay isolated.
            self.cache.reset()
            cached_k, cached_v = None, None

        if cached_k is not None and cached_v is not None and cached_k.size(0) == (B * H):
            C = cached_k.size(1)
            K_cache = cached_k.view(B, H, C, d)
            V_cache = cached_v.view(B, H, C, d)
            scores_cache = (
                torch.matmul(Q, K_cache.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous())
                * scale
                * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.sparse_gate, min=-10.0, max=10.0))
            )
            scores = torch.cat(
                [
                    scores_lr * torch.sigmoid(torch.clamp(self.LR_scale, min=-10.0, max=10.0)),
                    scores_cache,
                ],
                dim=-1,
            )
            V_comb = torch.cat([V, V_cache], dim=2)
        else:
            scores = scores_lr
            V_comb = V

        attn = torch.nan_to_num(F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1), nan=0.0).to(dtype)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1)
            attn = attn * head_mask

        context = torch.matmul(attn, V_comb)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, N_q, self.all_head_size)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_proj(context)

        return output, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Expand source code 
class LoRALinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features,
        out_features,
        bias=True,
        lora_rank=0,
        lora_alpha=1.0,
        freeze_base=True,
        device=None,
        dtype=None,
    ):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features = int(in_features)
        self.out_features = int(out_features)
        self.lora_rank = int(lora_rank) if lora_rank and lora_rank > 0 else 0
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)
        self.scaling = self.lora_alpha / max(1, self.lora_rank)

        factory = {"device": device, "dtype": dtype}
        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, in_features, **factory))
        if bias:
            self.bias = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, **factory))
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.weight, a=math.sqrt(5))
        if self.bias is not None:
            fan_in, _ = nn.init._calculate_fan_in_and_fan_out(self.weight)
            bound = 1 / math.sqrt(fan_in) if fan_in > 0 else 0
            nn.init.uniform_(self.bias, -bound, bound)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(self.lora_rank, in_features, **factory))
            self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, self.lora_rank, **factory))
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        if freeze_base:
            self.weight.requires_grad = False
            if self.bias is not None:
                self.bias.requires_grad = False

    def forward(self, x):
        w = self.weight.to(x.dtype) if self.weight.dtype != x.dtype else self.weight
        b = (
            self.bias.to(x.dtype)
            if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != x.dtype)
            else self.bias
        )
        out = F.linear(x, w, b)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
            bb = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
            out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
        return out

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

def forward(self, x):
    w = self.weight.to(x.dtype) if self.weight.dtype != x.dtype else self.weight
    b = (
        self.bias.to(x.dtype)
        if (self.bias is not None and self.bias.dtype != x.dtype)
        else self.bias
    )
    out = F.linear(x, w, b)
    if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
        a = self.lora_A.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_A.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_A
        bb = self.lora_B.to(x.dtype) if self.lora_B.dtype != x.dtype else self.lora_B
        out = out + F.linear(F.linear(x, a), bb) * self.scaling
    return out

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class LowRankCache (max_len: int = 128, low_rank_k: int = 16)
Expand source code 
class LowRankCache(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, max_len: int = 128, low_rank_k: int = 16) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.max_len: int = max_len
        self.low_rank_k: int = low_rank_k
        self.register_buffer("recent_k", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.recent_k: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("recent_v", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.recent_v: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("sparse_k", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.sparse_k: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("sparse_v", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.sparse_v: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("low_rank_k_buf", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.low_rank_k_buf: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("low_rank_v_buf", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self.low_rank_v_buf: torch.Tensor
        self.register_buffer("_cache_proj", torch.zeros(0), persistent=False)
        self._cache_proj: torch.Tensor
        self._update_step: int = 0

    def reset(self) -> None:
        self.recent_k = torch.zeros(0, device=self.recent_k.device, dtype=self.recent_k.dtype)
        self.recent_v = torch.zeros(0, device=self.recent_v.device, dtype=self.recent_v.dtype)
        self.sparse_k = torch.zeros(0, device=self.sparse_k.device, dtype=self.sparse_k.dtype)
        self.sparse_v = torch.zeros(0, device=self.sparse_v.device, dtype=self.sparse_v.dtype)
        self.low_rank_k_buf = torch.zeros(
            0, device=self.low_rank_k_buf.device, dtype=self.low_rank_k_buf.dtype
        )
        self.low_rank_v_buf = torch.zeros(
            0, device=self.low_rank_v_buf.device, dtype=self.low_rank_v_buf.dtype
        )
        self._update_step = 0

    def update(self, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) -> None:
        B, H, N, D = K.shape
        k_flat = K.detach().reshape(B * H, N, D)
        v_flat = V.detach().reshape(B * H, N, D)

        # Recent cache
        if (
            self.recent_k.numel() == 0
            or self.recent_k.size(0) != B * H
            or self.recent_k.size(-1) != D
        ):
            self.recent_k = k_flat[:, -self.max_len :, :].contiguous().clone()
            self.recent_v = v_flat[:, -self.max_len :, :].contiguous().clone()
        else:
            self.recent_k = (
                torch.cat([self.recent_k, k_flat], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len :, :]
                .contiguous()
                .clone()
            )
            self.recent_v = (
                torch.cat([self.recent_v, v_flat], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len :, :]
                .contiguous()
                .clone()
            )

        # Sparse cache (sample every 4th token)
        sparse_idx = torch.arange(0, N, 4, device=K.device)
        sparse_k = k_flat[:, sparse_idx, :]
        sparse_v = v_flat[:, sparse_idx, :]
        if (
            self.sparse_k.numel() == 0
            or self.sparse_k.size(0) != B * H
            or self.sparse_k.size(-1) != D
        ):
            self.sparse_k = sparse_k[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :].contiguous().clone()
            self.sparse_v = sparse_v[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :].contiguous().clone()
        else:
            self.sparse_k = (
                torch.cat([self.sparse_k, sparse_k], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :]
                .contiguous()
                .clone()
            )
            self.sparse_v = (
                torch.cat([self.sparse_v, sparse_v], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :]
                .contiguous()
                .clone()
            )

        # Low-rank subspace cache
        if self.recent_k.size(1) >= self.low_rank_k:
            step = getattr(self, "_update_step", 0) + 1
            self._update_step = step
            if (
                step % 50 == 1
                or self.low_rank_k_buf.numel() == 0
                or self.low_rank_k_buf.size(0) != B * H
            ):
                with torch.no_grad():
                    rk = self.recent_k.float()
                    rv = self.recent_v.float()
                    if (
                        self._cache_proj.numel() == 0
                        or self._cache_proj.size(0) != D
                        or self._cache_proj.size(1) != self.low_rank_k
                        or self._cache_proj.device != rk.device
                    ):
                        proj = torch.empty(
                            D, self.low_rank_k, device=rk.device, dtype=torch.float32
                        )
                        nn.init.orthogonal_(proj)
                        self._cache_proj = proj

                    try:
                        y_k = torch.matmul(rk, self._cache_proj)
                        q_orth_k, _ = torch.linalg.qr(y_k)
                        self.low_rank_k_buf = torch.matmul(
                            q_orth_k,
                            torch.matmul(q_orth_k.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(), rk),
                        ).to(self.recent_k.dtype)

                        y_v = torch.matmul(rv, self._cache_proj)
                        q_orth_v, _ = torch.linalg.qr(y_v)
                        self.low_rank_v_buf = torch.matmul(
                            q_orth_v,
                            torch.matmul(q_orth_v.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(), rv),
                        ).to(self.recent_v.dtype)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    def get_cached(self) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if self.recent_k.numel() == 0:
            return None, None
        B_H = self.recent_k.size(0)
        tensors_k = [self.recent_k]
        tensors_v = [self.recent_v]
        if self.sparse_k.numel() > 0 and self.sparse_k.size(0) == B_H:
            tensors_k.append(self.sparse_k)
            tensors_v.append(self.sparse_v)
        if self.low_rank_k_buf.numel() > 0 and self.low_rank_k_buf.size(0) == B_H:
            tensors_k.append(self.low_rank_k_buf)
            tensors_v.append(self.low_rank_v_buf)
        return torch.cat(tensors_k, dim=1), torch.cat(tensors_v, dim=1)

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

def get_cached(self) ‑> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]
Expand source code 
def get_cached(self) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]:
    if self.recent_k.numel() == 0:
        return None, None
    B_H = self.recent_k.size(0)
    tensors_k = [self.recent_k]
    tensors_v = [self.recent_v]
    if self.sparse_k.numel() > 0 and self.sparse_k.size(0) == B_H:
        tensors_k.append(self.sparse_k)
        tensors_v.append(self.sparse_v)
    if self.low_rank_k_buf.numel() > 0 and self.low_rank_k_buf.size(0) == B_H:
        tensors_k.append(self.low_rank_k_buf)
        tensors_v.append(self.low_rank_v_buf)
    return torch.cat(tensors_k, dim=1), torch.cat(tensors_v, dim=1)
def reset(self) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def reset(self) -> None:
    self.recent_k = torch.zeros(0, device=self.recent_k.device, dtype=self.recent_k.dtype)
    self.recent_v = torch.zeros(0, device=self.recent_v.device, dtype=self.recent_v.dtype)
    self.sparse_k = torch.zeros(0, device=self.sparse_k.device, dtype=self.sparse_k.dtype)
    self.sparse_v = torch.zeros(0, device=self.sparse_v.device, dtype=self.sparse_v.dtype)
    self.low_rank_k_buf = torch.zeros(
        0, device=self.low_rank_k_buf.device, dtype=self.low_rank_k_buf.dtype
    )
    self.low_rank_v_buf = torch.zeros(
        0, device=self.low_rank_v_buf.device, dtype=self.low_rank_v_buf.dtype
    )
    self._update_step = 0
def update(self, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) ‑> None
Expand source code 
def update(self, K: torch.Tensor, V: torch.Tensor) -> None:
    B, H, N, D = K.shape
    k_flat = K.detach().reshape(B * H, N, D)
    v_flat = V.detach().reshape(B * H, N, D)

    # Recent cache
    if (
        self.recent_k.numel() == 0
        or self.recent_k.size(0) != B * H
        or self.recent_k.size(-1) != D
    ):
        self.recent_k = k_flat[:, -self.max_len :, :].contiguous().clone()
        self.recent_v = v_flat[:, -self.max_len :, :].contiguous().clone()
    else:
        self.recent_k = (
            torch.cat([self.recent_k, k_flat], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len :, :]
            .contiguous()
            .clone()
        )
        self.recent_v = (
            torch.cat([self.recent_v, v_flat], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len :, :]
            .contiguous()
            .clone()
        )

    # Sparse cache (sample every 4th token)
    sparse_idx = torch.arange(0, N, 4, device=K.device)
    sparse_k = k_flat[:, sparse_idx, :]
    sparse_v = v_flat[:, sparse_idx, :]
    if (
        self.sparse_k.numel() == 0
        or self.sparse_k.size(0) != B * H
        or self.sparse_k.size(-1) != D
    ):
        self.sparse_k = sparse_k[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :].contiguous().clone()
        self.sparse_v = sparse_v[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :].contiguous().clone()
    else:
        self.sparse_k = (
            torch.cat([self.sparse_k, sparse_k], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :]
            .contiguous()
            .clone()
        )
        self.sparse_v = (
            torch.cat([self.sparse_v, sparse_v], dim=1)[:, -self.max_len // 2 :, :]
            .contiguous()
            .clone()
        )

    # Low-rank subspace cache
    if self.recent_k.size(1) >= self.low_rank_k:
        step = getattr(self, "_update_step", 0) + 1
        self._update_step = step
        if (
            step % 50 == 1
            or self.low_rank_k_buf.numel() == 0
            or self.low_rank_k_buf.size(0) != B * H
        ):
            with torch.no_grad():
                rk = self.recent_k.float()
                rv = self.recent_v.float()
                if (
                    self._cache_proj.numel() == 0
                    or self._cache_proj.size(0) != D
                    or self._cache_proj.size(1) != self.low_rank_k
                    or self._cache_proj.device != rk.device
                ):
                    proj = torch.empty(
                        D, self.low_rank_k, device=rk.device, dtype=torch.float32
                    )
                    nn.init.orthogonal_(proj)
                    self._cache_proj = proj

                try:
                    y_k = torch.matmul(rk, self._cache_proj)
                    q_orth_k, _ = torch.linalg.qr(y_k)
                    self.low_rank_k_buf = torch.matmul(
                        q_orth_k,
                        torch.matmul(q_orth_k.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(), rk),
                    ).to(self.recent_k.dtype)

                    y_v = torch.matmul(rv, self._cache_proj)
                    q_orth_v, _ = torch.linalg.qr(y_v)
                    self.low_rank_v_buf = torch.matmul(
                        q_orth_v,
                        torch.matmul(q_orth_v.transpose(-2, -1).contiguous(), rv),
                    ).to(self.recent_v.dtype)
                except Exception:
                    pass
class LowRankLinear (in_features: int,
out_features: int,
rank: int,
lora_rank: int = 16,
bias: bool = True)
Expand source code 
class LowRankLinear(nn.Module):
    def __init__(
        self,
        in_features: int,
        out_features: int,
        rank: int,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        bias: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.in_features: int = in_features
        self.out_features: int = out_features
        self.rank: int = rank
        self.lora_rank: int = min(lora_rank, min(in_features, out_features))
        self.lora_scaling: float = (32.0 / self.lora_rank) if self.lora_rank > 0 else 1.0

        self.U: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(out_features, rank), requires_grad=False)
        self.V: nn.Parameter = nn.Parameter(torch.empty(rank, in_features), requires_grad=False)
        if bias:
            self.bias: nn.Parameter | None = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features), requires_grad=False
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("bias", None)

        if self.lora_rank > 0:
            self.lora_A: nn.Parameter | None = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(self.lora_rank, in_features)
            )
            self.lora_B: nn.Parameter | None = nn.Parameter(
                torch.empty(out_features, self.lora_rank)
            )
        else:
            self.register_parameter("lora_A", None)
            self.register_parameter("lora_B", None)

        self.reset_parameters()

    def reset_parameters(self) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            u_fp32 = torch.empty_like(self.U, dtype=torch.float32)
            v_fp32 = torch.empty_like(self.V, dtype=torch.float32)
            nn.init.orthogonal_(u_fp32)
            nn.init.orthogonal_(v_fp32)
            self.U.data.copy_(u_fp32.to(self.U.dtype))
            self.V.data.copy_(v_fp32.to(self.V.dtype))
            if self.bias is not None:
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bias)

        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None:
            nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(self.lora_A, a=math.sqrt(5))
            self.lora_A.requires_grad = True
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_B is not None:
            nn.init.zeros_(self.lora_B)
            self.lora_B.requires_grad = True

    @property
    def weight(self) -> torch.Tensor:
        w = torch.matmul(self.U, self.V)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            w = w + torch.matmul(self.lora_B, self.lora_A) * self.lora_scaling
        return w

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        Vx = F.linear(x, self.V)
        out = F.linear(Vx, self.U)
        if self.lora_rank > 0 and self.lora_A is not None and self.lora_B is not None:
            lora_out = F.linear(F.linear(x, self.lora_A), self.lora_B) * self.lora_scaling
            out = out + lora_out
        if self.bias is not None:
            out = out + self.bias
        return out

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class ModelAdapter (model_or_config: Any,
family: str | None = None)
Expand source code 
class ModelAdapter:
    """Read-only architecture contract used by module wrappers.

    Fix MF-05: `family` is normalized to a canonical internal name (e.g. "gpt2"
    becomes "gpt"), while the original name is preserved in `raw_family` for
    logging. Downstream code that inspects `adapter.family` will always see a
    canonical value; code that needs the raw value should use `raw_family`.
    """

    def __init__(self, model_or_config: Any, family: str | None = None):
        self.source = _config(model_or_config)
        raw_family = family or detect_family(model_or_config)
        # Normalize aliases to canonical internal family.
        self.raw_family = raw_family
        self.family = FAMILY_ALIASES.get(raw_family, raw_family)

        self.hidden_size = int(
            getattr(self.source, "hidden_size", getattr(self.source, "d_model", 0)) or 0
        )
        heads = getattr(
            self.source,
            "num_attention_heads",
            getattr(
                self.source,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(self.source, "num_decoder_attention_heads", 0),
            ),
        )
        self.num_heads = int(heads or 0)
        h_dim = getattr(self.source, "head_dim", None)
        self.head_dim = int(
            h_dim
            if h_dim is not None
            else (self.hidden_size // self.num_heads if self.num_heads else 0)
        )
        kv_heads = getattr(
            self.source,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            getattr(
                self.source,
                "num_key_value_heads_decoder",
                getattr(self.source, "num_decoder_attention_heads", self.num_heads or 0),
            ),
        )
        self.num_key_value_heads = int(kv_heads or self.num_heads or 0)
        if self.num_key_value_heads <= 0:
            self.num_key_value_heads = self.num_heads
        self.num_hidden_layers = int(
            getattr(self.source, "num_hidden_layers", getattr(self.source, "num_layers", 0))
        )
        self.q_proj_out = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_proj_out = self.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_key_value_heads))
        self.projection_aliases = {
            "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj"),
            "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj"),
            "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj"),
            "o": ("out_lin", "o_proj"),
        }

        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Fix MF-01/MF-02/MF-03/MF-04: Consistent causal-family handling.
        # ------------------------------------------------------------------
        # A family is causal if either:
        #   (a) its canonical family is in CAUSAL_FAMILIES, OR
        #   (b) its raw family is in CAUSAL_FAMILIES (defense in depth).
        self.causal = (self.family in CAUSAL_FAMILIES) or (raw_family in CAUSAL_FAMILIES)

        # mask_rank: 4 for causal decoders, 2 for bidirectional encoders, 3 otherwise.
        if self.causal:
            self.mask_rank = 4
        elif self.family in BIDIRECTIONAL_FAMILIES:
            self.mask_rank = 2
        else:
            self.mask_rank = 3

        self.bidirectional = not self.causal

        # position_embedding: rotary for modern decoders, absolute for BERT/GPT-2,
        # relative for T5, none for unknown.
        if self.family in ROTARY_FAMILIES or raw_family in ROTARY_FAMILIES:
            self.position_embedding = "rotary"
        elif (
            self.family in GPT_ABSOLUTE_POSITION_FAMILIES
            or raw_family in GPT_ABSOLUTE_POSITION_FAMILIES
            or self.family in {"bert", "roberta"}
        ):
            self.position_embedding = "absolute"
        elif self.family == "t5":
            self.position_embedding = "relative"
        else:
            self.position_embedding = "none"

        self.supports_gqa = (self.num_key_value_heads < self.num_heads) or self.family == "qwen3_5"
        self.supports_delta_net = self.family == "qwen3_5"
        self.supports_moe = self.family == "qwen3_5"

    def projection(self, module: Any, kind: str) -> Any:
        legacy, native = self.projection_aliases[kind]
        return getattr(module, native, getattr(module, legacy, None))

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "family": self.family,
            "raw_family": self.raw_family,
            "hidden_size": self.hidden_size,
            "num_heads": self.num_heads,
            "num_key_value_heads": self.num_key_value_heads,
            "q_proj_out": self.q_proj_out,
            "kv_proj_out": self.kv_proj_out,
            "kv_repeat": self.kv_repeat,
            "head_dim": self.head_dim,
            "num_hidden_layers": self.num_hidden_layers,
            "causal": self.causal,
            "mask_rank": self.mask_rank,
            "position_embedding": self.position_embedding,
            "supports_gqa": self.supports_gqa,
            "supports_delta_net": self.supports_delta_net,
            "supports_moe": self.supports_moe,
        }

class ProgressiveTokenPruning (config: Any | None = None,
