Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("progressive_token_pruning")
class ProgressiveTokenPruning(BaseAttention):
    """
    Progressive Token Pruning attention.

    Forward:
      1. Score each token (score_network or shared scorer).
      2. Force-keep the first token ([CLS]).
      3. Keep the top-k tokens (k = ceil(L · keep_ratio)).
      4. Compute attention over the reduced set of keys/values.
      5. Return output with the original (B, L, D) shape.

    KV-cache: supported for decoder inference.
    """

    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        keep_ratio: float = 0.8,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        shared_scorer: SharedTokenScorer | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        if rank is None:
            rank = 64
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        # PTP-FIX-12: dimension resolution.
        self.d_model = int(
            getattr(config, "hidden_size", None) or d_model or kwargs.get("hidden_size") or 768
        )
        self.dim = self.d_model
        self.num_heads = int(
            getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12))
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
            )
        )
        self.head_dim = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "head_dim",
                kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)),
            )
        )
        self.all_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.keep_ratio = float(
            getattr(comp_config, "keep_ratio", kwargs.get("keep_ratio", keep_ratio))
        )

        is_dec = getattr(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = int(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", 16)),
            )
            if config
            else 16
        )
        self.lora_alpha = float(
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # Projections
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.q_proj = self.q_lin
        self.k_proj = self.k_lin
        self.v_proj = self.v_lin
        self.out_proj = self.out_lin
        self.query = self.q_lin
        self.key = self.k_lin
        self.value = self.v_lin
        self.dense = self.out_lin  # PTP-FIX-10
        self.c_proj = self.out_lin  # PTP-FIX-10
        self.o_proj = self.out_lin  # PTP-FIX-10

        # PTP-FIX-04: optional shared scorer.
        if shared_scorer is not None:
            self.score_network = shared_scorer
            self._uses_shared_scorer = True
        else:
            self.score_network = SharedTokenScorer(self.dim, hidden=64)
            self._uses_shared_scorer = False

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        src = (
            pretrained_attn
            if pretrained_attn is not None
            else (old_attn if old_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn"))
        )
        if src is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def gate(self):
        return None

    @property
    def random_proj(self):
        return None

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in (
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                )
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                is_conv = (
                    c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                )
                if is_conv:
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split, :],
                    c_b[:split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split : 2 * split, :],
                    c_b[split : 2 * split] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    is_conv_o = (
                        c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D"
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    )
                    if is_conv_o:
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for key, sub in (("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")):
                mod = getattr(s, sub, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules
        else:
            candidates = {
                "q": ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query"),
                "k": ("k_lin", "k_proj", "key"),
                "v": ("v_lin", "v_proj", "value"),
                "o": ("out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"),
            }
            for key, names in candidates.items():
                for n in names:
                    if hasattr(src, n):
                        mod = getattr(src, n)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"ProgressiveTokenPruning copy_weights failed: missing "
                f"mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_lin,
            "k": self.k_lin,
            "v": self.v_lin,
            "o": self.out_lin,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[key]
                if w is None:
                    if key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    continue

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = float(torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item())
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "PTP: '%s' weight has near-zero norm (%.2e).",
                        key,
                        fnorm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for m in (self.q_lin, self.k_lin, self.v_lin, self.out_lin):
                m.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("PTP does not support cross-attention.")

        x = hidden_states
        B, L, D = x.shape

        valid_mask = get_token_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, L, x.device)

        # PTP-FIX-03: single assignment.
        scores = self.score_network(x)  # (B, L)
        # PTP-FIX-11: only force [CLS] if position 0 is valid.
        first_valid = valid_mask[:, 0].unsqueeze(-1)  # (B, 1)
        scores = torch.where(first_valid, scores, scores)
        # Force first token to top priority (only if valid).
        scores = scores.masked_fill(~valid_mask, -1e4)
        if first_valid.any():
            scores[:, 0] = torch.where(
                first_valid.squeeze(-1),
                torch.full_like(scores[:, 0], 1e4),
                scores[:, 0],
            )

        # FIXED: Global top-k pruning over the sequence axis violates causality.
        # Disable pruning for auto-regressive generation/causal models.
        if getattr(self, "is_decoder", False):
            k_tokens = L
        else:
            k_tokens = max(1, min(L, int(math.ceil(L * self.keep_ratio))))
            
        top_scores, top_indices = torch.topk(scores, k_tokens, dim=-1)
        top_indices, _ = torch.sort(top_indices, dim=-1)

        # Projections
        q = self.q_lin(x).view(B, L, self.num_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        k_new = self.k_lin(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        v_new = self.v_lin(x).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # ---------------- KV-cache (PTP-FIX-01) ----------------
        past_k = past_v = None
        past_len = 0
        if past_key_value is not None and self.is_decoder is not False:
            # Tuple cache
            if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
                past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
                past_len = past_k.shape[-2]
            # HF DynamicCache
            elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                idx = min(layer_idx, len(past_key_value.key_cache) - 1)
                past_k = past_key_value.key_cache[idx]
                past_v = past_key_value.value_cache[idx]
                past_len = past_k.shape[-2]

        # ---------------- RoPE with past_len offset (PTP-FIX-02) ----------------
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            if past_len > 0 and cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L:
                cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
                sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            q, k_new = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k_new, cos, sin)

        # ---------------- Gather pruned K/V ----------------
        # Note: PTP prunes tokens ONLY for the current chunk (new tokens).
        # With KV-cache, past tokens are always used in full.
        top_idx_exp = (
            top_indices.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, self.num_kv_heads, -1, self.head_dim)
        )
        k_pruned = torch.gather(k_new, 2, top_idx_exp)  # (B, H_kv, k_tokens, d)
        v_pruned = torch.gather(v_new, 2, top_idx_exp)

        if past_k is not None and past_v is not None:
            # Concatenate past (full) with current (pruned).
            k_all = torch.cat([past_k, k_pruned], dim=-2)
            v_all = torch.cat([past_v, v_pruned], dim=-2)
            # Keep track of original positions for causal masking.
            past_positions = torch.arange(past_len, device=x.device).view(1, 1, -1)
            past_positions = past_positions.expand(B, 1, -1)
            current_positions = (
                torch.gather(
                    top_indices,
                    1,
                    torch.arange(k_tokens, device=x.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1),
                )
                + past_len
            )  # positions in the full sequence
            k_positions = torch.cat(
                [past_positions, current_positions.unsqueeze(1)],
                dim=-1,
            )  # (B, 1, past_len + k_tokens)
        else:
            k_all = k_pruned
            v_all = v_pruned
            k_positions = top_indices.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, k_tokens)

        # ---------------- GQA repeat ----------------
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            k_all = repeat_kv(k_all, self.kv_repeat)
            v_all = repeat_kv(v_all, self.kv_repeat)

        # ---------------- Attention scores ----------------
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
        attn_scores = (
            torch.matmul(q.float(), k_all.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale
        )  # (B, H, L, past_len + k_tokens)

        # ---------------- Causal mask ----------------
        if self.is_decoder:
            q_pos = (past_len + torch.arange(L, device=x.device)).view(1, 1, L, 1)
            causal = k_positions.unsqueeze(2) > q_pos
            attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(causal, -1e4)

        # ---------------- Valid-key mask for current chunk ----------------
        # Past keys are always valid (they were valid when cached).
        current_valid = torch.gather(valid_mask, 1, top_indices)  # (B, k_tokens)
        if past_k is not None:
            # Assume past keys are all valid (they were cached after masking).
            all_valid = torch.cat(
                [
                    torch.ones(B, past_len, device=x.device, dtype=torch.bool),
                    current_valid,
                ],
                dim=1,
            )  # (B, past_len + k_tokens)
        else:
            all_valid = current_valid
        attn_scores = attn_scores.masked_fill(~all_valid.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2), -1e4)

        probs = F.softmax(attn_scores, dim=-1).to(x.dtype)
        probs = torch.nan_to_num(probs, nan=0.0)
        probs = self.dropout(probs)

        # head_mask handling
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                probs = probs * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(probs.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                probs = probs * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(
                    probs.dtype
                )
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                probs = probs * head_mask.to(probs.dtype)

        attn_out = (
            torch.matmul(probs, v_all)
            .permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
            .contiguous()
            .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        )

        # ---------------- Update KV-cache ----------------
        if past_key_value is not None:
            if hasattr(past_key_value, "update"):
                # HF DynamicCache: store the FULL k_new, v_new (not pruned),
                # so that future queries can access them.
                past_key_value.update(k_new, v_new, layer_idx)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return attn_out, (probs if output_attentions else None)

        out = self.out_lin(attn_out)
        return out, (probs if output_attentions else None)

Progressive Token Pruning attention.

Forward -----= 1. Score each token (score_network or shared scorer). 2. Force-keep the first token ([CLS]). 3. Keep the top-k tokens (k = ceil(L · keep_ratio)). 4. Compute attention over the reduced set of keys/values. 5. Return output with the original (B, L, D) shape.

KV-cache: supported for decoder inference.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

config: Any | None = None,

is_decoder: bool = False,
**kwargs: Any)
Expand source code 
class SVDMambaSub(nn.Module):
    is_custom_attention: bool = False

    def __init__(self, d_model: int = 768, config: Any | None = None, rank: int | None = None, is_decoder: bool = False, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.d_model = d_model
        self.ssm = SimpleSSM(d_model=d_model, config=config, is_decoder=is_decoder)
        self.in_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        
        # Zero initialization for output projection to safely inject Mamba (ReZero)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.bias)

        self._ssm_supports_state = self._ssm_accepts_past_state()

    def _ssm_accepts_past_state(self) -> bool:
        import inspect
        try:
            sig = inspect.signature(self.ssm.forward)
            return "past_state" in sig.parameters or "state" in sig.parameters
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            return False

    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None, past_state: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, Any | None]:
        x_proj = self.in_proj(hidden_states)
        ssm_result: Any = None
        present_state: Any | None = None

        if self._ssm_supports_state:
            try:
                ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_state)
            except TypeError:
                try:
                    ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, state=past_state)
                except Exception:
                    ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)
        else:
            ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)

        if isinstance(ssm_result, tuple):
            ssm_out = ssm_result[0]
            if len(ssm_result) > 1: present_state = ssm_result[1]
        else:
            ssm_out = ssm_result

        out = self.out_proj(ssm_out)
        return out, present_state

Base class for all neural network modules.

Your models should also subclass this class.

Modules can also contain other Modules, allowing them to be nested in a tree structure. You can assign the submodules as regular attributes::

import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


class Model(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv2d(1, 20, 5)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv2d(20, 20, 5)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(x))
        return F.relu(self.conv2(x))

Submodules assigned in this way will be registered, and will also have their parameters converted when you call :meth:to, etc.

Note

As per the example above, an __init__() call to the parent class must be made before assignment on the child.

:ivar training: Boolean represents whether this module is in training or evaluation mode. :vartype training: bool

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None, past_state: Any | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, Any | None]:

    ssm_result: Any = None
    present_state: Any | None = None

    if self._ssm_supports_state:
        try:
            ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, past_state=past_state)
        except TypeError:
            try:
                ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask, state=past_state)
            except Exception:
                ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)
    else:
        ssm_result = self.ssm(x_proj, attention_mask=attention_mask)

    if isinstance(ssm_result, tuple):
        ssm_out = ssm_result[0]
        if len(ssm_result) > 1: present_state = ssm_result[1]
    else:
        ssm_out = ssm_result

    out = self.out_proj(ssm_out)
    return out, present_state

Define the computation performed at every call.

Should be overridden by all subclasses.

Note

Although the recipe for forward pass needs to be defined within this function, one should call the :class:Module instance afterwards instead of this since the former takes care of running the registered hooks while the latter silently ignores them.

class SharedTokenScorer (dim: int, hidden: int = 64)
Expand source code 
class SharedTokenScorer(nn.Module):
    """
    Optional token scorer that is shared across all PTP layers to reduce
    parameter overhead. When `share_scorer=True` is passed to all PTP
    instances, they reference the same scorer module.
    """

    def __init__(self, dim: int, hidden: int = 64) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(dim, hidden),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(hidden, 1),
        )

    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        return torch.sigmoid(self.net(x)).squeeze(-1)  # (B, L)

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("simple_ssm")
class SimpleSSM(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_bidirectional: bool = True
    triton_kernel: str = "state_update"

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        rank: int | None = 64,
        d_model: int | None = None,
        num_heads: int | None = None,
        num_kv_heads: int | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)
        self.config = config

        def _get(k: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
            for obj in (config, getattr(config, "compression_config", None)):
                if obj is not None:
                    if isinstance(obj, dict) and k in obj and obj[k] is not None:
                        return obj[k]
                    elif hasattr(obj, k):
                        val = getattr(obj, k)
                        if val is not None:
                            return val
            return kwargs.get(k, default)

        dim = d_model or _get("hidden_size") or _get("d_model") or _get("dim") or 64
        self.dim = int(dim)

        heads = num_heads or _get("num_attention_heads") or _get("num_heads") or _get("n_head") or 4
        self.num_heads = int(heads)

        kv_heads = (
            num_kv_heads or _get("num_key_value_heads") or _get("num_kv_heads") or self.num_heads
        )
        self.num_kv_heads = int(kv_heads)
        self.head_dim = int(_get("head_dim", self.dim // max(1, self.num_heads)))
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))

        self.lora_rank = int(_get("lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)))
        self.lora_alpha = float(_get("lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", lora_alpha)))
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0

        # Projections
        self.in_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        # Recurrence decay gate
        self.decay_param = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(self.num_heads, 1, 1))

        # Architecture detection
        model_type = str(_get("model_type", "")).lower()
        is_dec = _get("is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if any(
            model_type.startswith(pfx)
            for pfx in ("gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "opt", "bloom")
        ):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)

        is_nested = False
        if old_attn is not None:
            src_cls = old_attn.__class__.__name__
            if (
                "Roberta" in src_cls
                or "Bert" in src_cls
                or (hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"))
            ):
                is_nested = True
        if not is_nested and model_type in ["roberta", "bert"] and not self.is_decoder:
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)
        self.triton_kwargs = {}

        if old_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(old_attn)

    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: Any) -> None:
        if old_attn is None:
            return

        src_cls = old_attn.__class__.__name__
        if (
            "Roberta" in src_cls
            or "Bert" in src_cls
            or (hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"))
        ):
            self.is_nested_encoder = True

        target_dtype = self.in_proj.weight.dtype
        with torch.no_grad():
            w, b = None, None
            try:
                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "q")
                if w is None:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(old_attn, "in")
            except Exception:
                pass

            if w is None and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
                c_w = getattr(
                    getattr(old_attn.c_attn, "base_layer", old_attn.c_attn),
                    "weight",
                    None,
                )
                if c_w is not None:
                    w_t = c_w.data.t() if c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1] else c_w.data
                    split = w_t.shape[0] // 3
                    w = w_t[:split, :]
                    self.is_decoder = True

            if w is None:
                for cand in ("q_lin", "query", "q_proj", "in_proj"):
                    mod = getattr(old_attn, cand, None)
                    if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                        w = mod.weight.data
                        b = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
                        if b is not None:
                            b = b.data
                        break

            if w is not None:
                d_out = min(w.shape[0], self.dim)
                d_in = min(w.shape[1], self.dim)
                self.in_proj.weight.data[:d_out, :d_in].copy_(w[:d_out, :d_in].to(target_dtype))
                if b is not None and self.in_proj.bias is not None:
                    self.in_proj.bias.data[: min(b.shape[0], self.dim)].copy_(
                        b[: min(b.shape[0], self.dim)].to(target_dtype)
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            self.in_proj.freeze_base_weights()
            self.out_proj.freeze_base_weights()

    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            return hidden_states, None

        while isinstance(hidden_states, (tuple, list)):
            hidden_states = hidden_states[0]

        target_dtype = hidden_states.dtype
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H = self.num_heads
        d = self.head_dim

        # In-projection
        u = self.in_proj(hidden_states).to(target_dtype)
        u_heads = u.view(B, L, H, d).transpose(1, 2)  # [B, H, L, d]

        # RoPE
        pos_emb = position_embeddings or kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        pos_ids = kwargs.get("position_ids")
        if pos_emb is not None and isinstance(pos_emb, (tuple, list)) and len(pos_emb) >= 2:
            cos, sin = pos_emb[0], pos_emb[1]
            if cos is not None and sin is not None:
                u_heads, _ = apply_rotary_pos_emb(u_heads, u_heads, cos, sin, position_ids=pos_ids)

        decay = torch.sigmoid(self.decay_param.to(target_dtype)).view(1, H, 1, 1)
        log_decay = torch.log(decay.clamp(min=1e-4, max=1.0 - 1e-4))

        pos = torch.arange(L, device=hidden_states.device, dtype=target_dtype)
        pos_diff = pos.unsqueeze(-1) - pos.unsqueeze(-2)

        if self.is_decoder:
            # Replace O(L^2) dense matrix with O(L) recurrent scan
            y_heads = torch.empty_like(u_heads)
            state = torch.zeros(B, H, 1, d, device=u_heads.device, dtype=target_dtype)
            decay_val = decay.to(target_dtype)
            for t in range(L):
                state = state * decay_val + u_heads[:, :, t:t+1, :]
                y_heads[:, :, t:t+1, :] = state
            y = y_heads.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, H * d)
        else:
            dist = torch.abs(pos_diff).unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)
            decay_mat = torch.exp((dist * log_decay).clamp(min=-30.0, max=0.0))
            y = (decay_mat.to(target_dtype) @ u_heads).transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, H * d)
        if y.shape[-1] != self.dim:
            if y.shape[-1] < self.dim:
                y = F.pad(y, (0, self.dim - y.shape[-1]))
            else:
                y = y[..., : self.dim]

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = y.to(target_dtype)
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(y).to(target_dtype)

        if attention_mask is not None and attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            out = out * attention_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(target_dtype)

        return out.to(target_dtype), None

class SinkAttention (config=None, num_sink_tokens: int = 4, window_size: int = 4096, **kwargs)
Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("sink_attention")
class SinkAttention(BaseAttention):
    """Attention with dedicated sink tokens that always get attention."""
    
    supports_causal: bool = True
    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_rope: bool = True
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        config=None,
        num_sink_tokens: int = 4,
        window_size: int = 4096,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        super().__init__(config)
        self.num_sink_tokens = num_sink_tokens
        self.window_size = window_size
    
    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor, attention_mask=None, **kwargs):
        B, N, D = hidden_states.shape
        
        if N <= self.window_size:
            return hidden_states, None
        
        sinks = hidden_states[:, :self.num_sink_tokens, :]
        recent = hidden_states[:, -self.window_size + self.num_sink_tokens:, :]
        combined = torch.cat([sinks, recent], dim=1)
        
        return combined, None

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("span_expanded_attention")
class SpanExpandedAttention(BaseAttention):
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any,
        rank: int | None = None,
        expansion_span: int = 1024,
        num_sink_tokens: int = 4,
        use_lora: bool = True,
        lora_rank: int = 8,
        pretrained_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank or 64)
        self.config = config
        comp_config = getattr(config, "compression_config", None)

        self.hidden_size: int = getattr(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768))
        self.dim = self.hidden_size
        self.num_heads: int = getattr(
            config, "num_attention_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)
        )
        self.num_kv_heads: int = getattr(
            config,
            "num_key_value_heads",
            kwargs.get("num_key_value_heads", self.num_heads),
        )
        self.head_dim: int = getattr(
            config,
            "head_dim",
            kwargs.get("head_dim", self.dim // self.num_heads),
        )
        self.all_head_size: int = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_kv_head_size: int = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim

        # ---- Span expansion configuration ----
        self.expansion_span: int = int(expansion_span) if expansion_span else 1024
        # Number of leading "sink" tokens to always preserve in the cache.
        # StreamingLLM (Xiao et al. 2024) shows the first few tokens act as
        # attention sinks and must be kept for long-context stability.
        self.num_sink_tokens: int = max(0, min(int(num_sink_tokens), self.expansion_span))

        is_dec = getattr(config, "is_decoder", kwargs.get("is_decoder", False))
        if kwargs.get("is_causal", False):
            is_dec = True
        self.is_decoder = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_causal = bool(is_dec)
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_rank",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_rank", kwargs.get("lora_rank", lora_rank)),
            )
            if config
            else lora_rank
        )
        self.lora_alpha = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "lora_alpha",
                getattr(comp_config, "lora_alpha", kwargs.get("lora_alpha", 32.0)),
            )
            if config
            else 32.0
        )
        self.freeze_base = self.lora_rank > 0
        self.kv_repeat = max(1, self.num_heads // max(1, self.num_kv_heads))
        self.supports_gqa = True

        # Projections
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.dim,
            self.all_kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.all_head_size,
            self.dim,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=self.freeze_base,
        )

        self.query = self.q_proj
        self.key = self.k_proj
        self.value = self.v_proj

        dropout_p = (
            getattr(
                config,
                "dropout",
                getattr(
                    config,
                    "hidden_dropout_prob",
                    getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1),
                ),
            )
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        source_attn = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("source_attn")
        if source_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(source_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Weight copying (unchanged, production-hardened)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, src: Any) -> None:
        if src is None:
            return

        cls_name = src.__class__.__name__
        has_out_lin = hasattr(src, "out_lin") or hasattr(src, "c_proj") or hasattr(src, "o_proj")
        is_nested = False
        if (
            (
                hasattr(src, "self")
                and hasattr(src.self, "query")
                and hasattr(src, "output")
                and hasattr(src.output, "dense")
            )
            or (
                cls_name
                in [
                    "BertSelfAttention",
                    "RobertaSelfAttention",
                    "XLMRobertaSelfAttention",
                    "ElectraSelfAttention",
                    "DebertaSelfAttention",
                ]
                or (cls_name.endswith("SelfAttention") and "distil" not in cls_name.lower())
            )
            and not has_out_lin
        ):
            is_nested = True
        self.is_nested_encoder = bool(is_nested)

        extracted: dict[str, tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]] = {
            "q": (None, None),
            "k": (None, None),
            "v": (None, None),
            "o": (None, None),
        }

        # 1. GPT-2 fused c_attn
        if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and src.c_attn is not None:
            c_attn_mod = src.c_attn
            base_c = getattr(c_attn_mod, "base_layer", c_attn_mod)
            c_w = getattr(base_c, "weight", getattr(c_attn_mod, "weight", None))
            if c_w is not None:
                c_w = c_w.data.clone()
                if (
                    (c_attn_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (base_c.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                    or (c_w.ndim == 2 and c_w.shape[0] < c_w.shape[1])
                ):
                    c_w = c_w.t().contiguous()
                split_size = c_w.size(0) // 3
                bias_mod = getattr(base_c, "bias", getattr(c_attn_mod, "bias", None))
                c_b = bias_mod.data.clone() if bias_mod is not None else None
                extracted["q"] = (
                    c_w[:split_size, :],
                    c_b[:split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["k"] = (
                    c_w[split_size : 2 * split_size, :],
                    c_b[split_size : 2 * split_size] if c_b is not None else None,
                )
                extracted["v"] = (
                    c_w[2 * split_size :, :],
                    c_b[2 * split_size :] if c_b is not None else None,
                )

            if hasattr(src, "c_proj") and src.c_proj is not None:
                c_proj_mod = src.c_proj
                base_o = getattr(c_proj_mod, "base_layer", c_proj_mod)
                o_w = getattr(base_o, "weight", getattr(c_proj_mod, "weight", None))
                if o_w is not None:
                    o_w = o_w.data.clone()
                    if (
                        (c_proj_mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (base_o.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D")
                        or (o_w.ndim == 2 and o_w.shape[0] < o_w.shape[1])
                    ):
                        o_w = o_w.t().contiguous()
                    bias_o = getattr(base_o, "bias", getattr(c_proj_mod, "bias", None))
                    extracted["o"] = (
                        o_w,
                        bias_o.data.clone() if bias_o is not None else None,
                    )

        # 2. RoBERTa nested
        elif hasattr(src, "self") and hasattr(src.self, "query"):
            s = src.self
            for proj_key, sub_name in [("q", "query"), ("k", "key"), ("v", "value")]:
                mod = getattr(s, sub_name, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    extracted[proj_key] = (
                        mod.weight.data.clone(),
                        mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                    )
            if hasattr(src, "output") and hasattr(src.output, "dense"):
                out_mod = src.output.dense
                extracted["o"] = (
                    out_mod.weight.data.clone(),
                    out_mod.bias.data.clone() if out_mod.bias is not None else None,
                )

        # 3. Flat modules (GPT-2 alt, Qwen, LLaMA, DistilBERT)
        else:
            proj_candidates = {
                "q": ["q_lin", "q_proj", "query"],
                "k": ["k_lin", "k_proj", "key"],
                "v": ["v_lin", "v_proj", "value"],
                "o": ["out_lin", "out_proj", "dense", "o_proj"],
            }
            for proj_key, names in proj_candidates.items():
                for name in names:
                    if hasattr(src, name):
                        mod = getattr(src, name)
                        if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                            extracted[proj_key] = (
                                mod.weight.data.clone(),
                                mod.bias.data.clone() if mod.bias is not None else None,
                            )
                            break

        missing = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing:
            try:
                from neuralops.core.adapters import UniversalAdapter

                adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                for k in missing:
                    w, b = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, k)
                    if w is not None:
                        extracted[k] = (w.clone(), b.clone() if b is not None else None)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

        missing_after = [
            k
            for k, (w, _) in extracted.items()
            if w is None and (k != "o" or not self.is_nested_encoder)
        ]
        if missing_after:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SpanExpandedAttention copy_weights failed: missing mandatory projections {missing_after} from {src.__class__.__name__}."
            )

        target_map = {
            "q": self.q_proj,
            "k": self.k_proj,
            "v": self.v_proj,
            "o": self.out_proj,
        }

        with torch.no_grad():
            for proj_key, target_mod in target_map.items():
                w, b = extracted[proj_key]
                if w is None:
                    if proj_key == "o" and self.is_nested_encoder:
                        nn.init.eye_(target_mod.weight)
                        if target_mod.bias is not None:
                            nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)
                        continue
                    else:
                        logger.warning(
                            "SpanExpandedAttention copy_weights: missing %s, using initialized weights",
                            proj_key,
                        )

                if w.shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w)
                elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_mod.weight.shape:
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.t().contiguous())
                elif (
                    w.shape[0] < target_mod.weight.shape[0]
                    and target_mod.weight.shape[0] % w.shape[0] == 0
                ):
                    repeats = target_mod.weight.shape[0] // w.shape[0]
                    target_mod.weight.copy_(w.repeat(repeats, 1))
                else:
                    min_0 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                    min_1 = min(target_mod.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                    target_mod.weight.zero_()
                    target_mod.weight[:min_0, :min_1].copy_(w[:min_0, :min_1])

                if b is not None and target_mod.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_mod.bias.shape:
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b)
                    elif (
                        b.shape[0] < target_mod.bias.shape[0]
                        and target_mod.bias.shape[0] % b.shape[0] == 0
                    ):
                        repeats = target_mod.bias.shape[0] // b.shape[0]
                        target_mod.bias.copy_(b.repeat(repeats))
                    else:
                        min_b = min(target_mod.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_mod.bias.zero_()
                        target_mod.bias[:min_b].copy_(b[:min_b])
                elif target_mod.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(target_mod.bias)

                fnorm = torch.linalg.norm(target_mod.weight.float()).item()
                if fnorm < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning(
                        "SpanExpandedAttention copy_weights: %s has near-zero norm (%.2e)",
                        proj_key,
                        fnorm,
                    )

        if self.freeze_base:
            for mod in [self.q_proj, self.k_proj, self.v_proj, self.out_proj]:
                mod.freeze_base_weights()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cache management helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _get_past_kv(
        self, past_key_value: Any
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None, int]:
        """Extract (past_k, past_v, past_len) from various cache formats."""
        if past_key_value is None:
            return None, None, 0

        if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
            past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            return past_k, past_v, past_k.shape[-2]

        if hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
            past_k = past_key_value.key_cache[0]
            past_v = (
                past_key_value.value_cache[0] if hasattr(past_key_value, "value_cache") else None
            )
            return past_k, past_v, past_k.shape[-2]

        return None, None, 0

    def _truncate_cache_with_sinks(
        self, k: torch.Tensor, v: torch.Tensor
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        """
        Apply sliding-window truncation while preserving the first N sink tokens.

        StreamingLLM (Xiao et al., 2024): the first few tokens accumulate a
        disproportionate amount of attention and act as "attention sinks".
        Dropping them causes severe perplexity degradation in long contexts.
        We therefore always preserve the first `num_sink_tokens` entries.
        """
        span = self.expansion_span
        if span <= 0 or k.shape[-2] <= span:
            return k, v

        sink = self.num_sink_tokens
        if sink <= 0:
            # Pure rolling cache, no sinks preserved
            return k[..., -span:, :], v[..., -span:, :]

        if span <= sink:
            # Pathological config: keep only sinks, drop the rest
            return k[..., :span, :], v[..., :span, :]

        # Keep first `sink` tokens + most recent `span - sink` tokens
        sink_k = k[..., :sink, :]
        sink_v = v[..., :sink, :]
        recent_k = k[..., -(span - sink) :, :]
        recent_v = v[..., -(span - sink) :, :]
        return torch.cat([sink_k, recent_k], dim=-2), torch.cat([sink_v, recent_v], dim=-2)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None, tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None]:
        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, Dh = self.num_heads, self.head_dim

        # ---- QKV projections ----
        q = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, Dh).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        k = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
        v = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(B, L, self.num_kv_heads, Dh).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)

        # ---- Extract past cache ----
        past_k, past_v, past_len = self._get_past_kv(past_key_value)

        # ---- RoPE with past_len offset ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            cos_curr = (
                cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :] if cos.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else cos
            )
            sin_curr = (
                sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :] if sin.shape[-2] >= past_len + L else sin
            )
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos_curr, sin_curr)

        # ---- GQA repeat (before cache concat, so cache stays compact) ----
        # Note: If using GQA, we store the compact KV in cache and expand on use.
        # Here we expand first, which is simpler but uses more cache memory.
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            # Re-expand past cache if it was stored compact
            if past_k is not None and past_k.shape[1] != self.num_heads:
                past_k = repeat_kv(past_k, repeats)
                past_v = repeat_kv(past_v, repeats)
            k = repeat_kv(k, repeats)
            v = repeat_kv(v, repeats)

        # ---- Concatenate with past cache ----
        if past_k is not None:
            k = torch.cat([past_k, k], dim=-2)
            v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=-2)

        # ---- Sliding-window truncation with sink preservation ----
        k, v = self._truncate_cache_with_sinks(k, v)

        # ---- Present cache for next step ----
        # Store compact (un-repeated) KV if GQA is used to save memory:
        if self.num_heads != self.num_kv_heads and k.shape[1] == self.num_heads:
            repeats = self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads
            # Take every `repeats`-th head to recover compact form
            # (repeat_kv duplicates contiguously, so index 0, repeats, 2*repeats, ...)
            compact_k = k[:, ::repeats, :, :].contiguous()
            compact_v = v[:, ::repeats, :, :].contiguous()
            present_key_value = (compact_k, compact_v)
        else:
            present_key_value = (k, v)

        scale = Dh**-0.5

        # ---- Fast path: PyTorch SDPA ----
        if attention_mask is None and head_mask is None and not output_attentions:
            try:
                _qs = q.half() if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
                _ks = k.half() if k.dtype == torch.float32 else k
                _vs = v.half() if v.dtype == torch.float32 else v
                # is_causal only meaningful when processing > 1 token at once.
                # When past cache is present and we process 1 token, no causal mask needed.
                _is_causal = bool(self.is_decoder) and L > 1 and past_len == 0
                _sdpa_out = torch.nn.functional.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    _qs,
                    _ks,
                    _vs,
                    is_causal=_is_causal,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                _sdpa_out = (
                    _sdpa_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                    .transpose(1, 2)
                    .contiguous()
                    .view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
                )
                _sdpa_out = torch.nan_to_num(_sdpa_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                out = self.out_proj(_sdpa_out) if not self.is_nested_encoder else _sdpa_out
                return out, None, present_key_value
            except Exception:
                pass  # fall through to manual path

        # ---- Manual path ----
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        # Causal masking: query i (position past_len + i) attends to keys 0..(past_len + i)
        is_causal = self.is_decoder or kwargs.get("is_causal", False)
        if is_causal and L > 1:
            kv_len = k.shape[-2]
            causal_mask = torch.triu(
                torch.full((L, kv_len), -1e4, device=scores.device),
                diagonal=1 + past_len,
            )
            scores = scores + causal_mask.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0)

        # External attention mask (padding etc.)
        if attention_mask is not None:
            scores = apply_attention_mask(scores, attention_mask)

        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
        attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        # Head mask
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1 and head_mask.size(0) == self.num_heads:
                attn = attn * head_mask.view(1, self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(attn.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2 and head_mask.size(1) == self.num_heads:
                attn = attn * head_mask.view(head_mask.size(0), self.num_heads, 1, 1).to(attn.dtype)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 4:
                attn = attn * head_mask.to(attn.dtype)

        context = (
            torch.matmul(attn, v).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, L, self.all_head_size)
        )

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            return context, (attn if output_attentions else None), present_key_value

        out = self.out_proj(context)
        return out, (attn if output_attentions else None), present_key_value
