Expand source code 
@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("squeezing_heads_distill", supports_causal=True, supports_gqa=True)
class SqueezingHeadsDistiller(BaseAttention):
    """
    Attention wrapper that keeps only the top-k most important heads
    (based on pretrained Q/K/V Frobenius norms) while preserving GQA structure.

    Effective head count:
        num_kept_heads = round(head_keep_ratio * num_heads)
        q_head_size   = num_kept_heads * head_dim
        out_head_size = num_kept_heads * head_dim

    KV projections preserve all num_kv_heads (GQA). Kept Q heads are grouped
    to matching KV heads via GQA index mapping.

    Parameter reduction:
        Q : num_heads   * head_dim * hidden  →  num_kept_heads * head_dim * hidden
        O : hidden * num_heads * head_dim    →  hidden * num_kept_heads * head_dim
        K/V: unchanged (GQA-compatible)
    """

    supports_gqa: bool = True
    supports_causal: bool = True
    is_custom_attention: bool = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: Any | None = None,
        old_attn: nn.Module | None = None,
        rank: int | None = None,
        head_keep_ratio: float | None = None,
        lora_rank: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: float = 32.0,
        is_decoder: bool | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(config, rank)

        # ---- 1. Dimensions ----
        self.hidden_size = int(_cfg_get(config, "hidden_size", kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)))
        self.num_heads = int(
            _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", kwargs.get("num_attention_heads", 12)),
            )
        )

        cfg_hd = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", None)
        kw_hd = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if cfg_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(cfg_hd)
        elif kw_hd is not None:
            self.head_dim = int(kw_hd)
        elif old_attn is not None:
            self.head_dim = None
            for attr in ("q_proj", "query", "q_lin"):
                if hasattr(old_attn, attr):
                    qw = getattr(old_attn, attr).weight
                    self.head_dim = qw.shape[0] // max(1, self.num_heads)
                    break
            if self.head_dim is None:
                self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads)
        else:
            self.head_dim = self.hidden_size // max(1, self.num_heads)

        cfg_kv = _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if cfg_kv is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv)
        elif kwargs.get("num_kv_heads") is not None:
            self.num_kv_heads = int(kwargs["num_kv_heads"])
        else:
            self.num_kv_heads = self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))

        # ---- 2. Compute num_kept_heads ----
        # SQ2-FIX-01: derive head_keep_ratio from `rank` if not explicitly given.
        if head_keep_ratio is not None:
            hkr = float(head_keep_ratio)
        elif rank is not None and rank > 0:
            # If rank <= num_heads, interpret as "keep this many heads"
            if rank <= self.num_heads:
                hkr = float(rank) / float(self.num_heads)
            else:
                # Otherwise interpret as "target head_dim"
                effective_hd = min(int(rank), self.head_dim)
                hkr = float(effective_hd) / float(self.head_dim)
        else:
            hkr = 1.0
        self.head_keep_ratio = max(1.0 / self.num_heads, min(1.0, hkr))

        self.num_kept_heads = max(
            1, min(self.num_heads, int(round(self.num_heads * self.head_keep_ratio)))
        )
        self.head_keep_ratio = self.num_kept_heads / self.num_heads

        self.kept_q_head_size = self.num_kept_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_head_size = self.kept_q_head_size

        # GQA mapping: which KV head does each kept Q head map to?
        # Kept heads are the top-k by importance; we must remember their original indices.
        self.num_kv_groups = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)
        self.kept_head_indices: torch.Tensor | None = None  # filled by copy_weights

        # ---- 3. Decoder detection ----
        self.is_decoder = _detect_decoder(config, is_decoder, old_attn)
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False

        self.lora_rank = int(lora_rank if lora_rank is not None else 16)
        self.lora_alpha = float(lora_alpha)

        # ---- 4. Projections ----
        # SQ2-FIX-01: Q and O are sized to num_kept_heads, not num_heads.
        self.q_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kept_q_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.k_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.v_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.hidden_size,
            self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )
        self.out_proj = LoRALinear(
            self.kept_q_head_size,
            self.hidden_size,
            bias=True,
            lora_rank=self.lora_rank,
            lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True,
        )

        dropout_p = float(
            getattr(config, "dropout", getattr(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        # ---- 5. Copy weights from source ----
        src_attn = old_attn if old_attn is not None else kwargs.get("pretrained_attn")
        if src_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(src_attn)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Aliases
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def query(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def key(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def q_lin(self):
        return self.q_proj

    @property
    def k_lin(self):
        return self.k_proj

    @property
    def v_lin(self):
        return self.v_proj

    @property
    def out_lin(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_proj

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Head importance: SQ2-FIX-02
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _compute_head_importance(
        self,
        w_q: torch.Tensor | None,
        w_k: torch.Tensor | None,
        w_v: torch.Tensor | None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor:
        """
        Compute per-head importance as the sum of Frobenius norms of the
        corresponding Q, K, V slices.

        Returns: (num_heads,) tensor of importance scores.
        """
        H = self.num_heads
        D = self.head_dim
        device = None
        for w in (w_q, w_k, w_v):
            if w is not None:
                device = w.device
                break
        if device is None:
            device = torch.device("cpu")

        score = torch.zeros(H, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)

        if w_q is not None and w_q.shape[0] >= H * D:
            w_q_heads = w_q[: H * D].view(H, D, -1)
            score += w_q_heads.float().pow(2).sum(dim=(1, 2))

        if w_k is not None and w_k.shape[0] >= self.num_kv_heads * D:
            # K has num_kv_heads; expand each KV head to the Q heads it serves.
            w_k_heads = w_k[: self.num_kv_heads * D].view(self.num_kv_heads, D, -1)
            k_score = w_k_heads.float().pow(2).sum(dim=(1, 2))  # (num_kv_heads,)
            # Repeat each KV head `num_kv_groups` times to align with Q heads.
            rep = self.num_kv_groups
            k_expanded = k_score.repeat_interleave(rep)
            if k_expanded.numel() < H:
                k_expanded = k_expanded.repeat((H + k_expanded.numel() - 1) // k_expanded.numel())[
                    :H
                ]
            else:
                k_expanded = k_expanded[:H]
            score += k_expanded

        if w_v is not None and w_v.shape[0] >= self.num_kv_heads * D:
            w_v_heads = w_v[: self.num_kv_heads * D].view(self.num_kv_heads, D, -1)
            v_score = w_v_heads.float().pow(2).sum(dim=(1, 2))
            rep = self.num_kv_groups
            v_expanded = v_score.repeat_interleave(rep)
            if v_expanded.numel() < H:
                v_expanded = v_expanded.repeat((H + v_expanded.numel() - 1) // v_expanded.numel())[
                    :H
                ]
            else:
                v_expanded = v_expanded[:H]
            score += v_expanded

        return score

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights with head selection
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn: nn.Module) -> None:
        with torch.no_grad():
            w_q = b_q = w_k = b_k = w_v = b_v = w_o = b_o = None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            has_out_lin = any(hasattr(old_attn, a) for a in ("out_lin", "c_proj", "o_proj"))
            if has_out_lin:
                self.is_nested_encoder = False
            elif (
                hasattr(old_attn, "self")
                and hasattr(old_attn.self, "query")
                and hasattr(old_attn, "output")
                and hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense")
            ) or src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
            else:
                self.is_nested_encoder = False

            # Determine nested container vs flat
            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    dense = old_attn.output.dense
                    w_o = dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = dense.bias.detach() if dense.bias is not None else None
            else:
                src = old_attn

            # ---- Pattern 1: GPT-2 fused c_attn ----
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_attn_w = src.c_attn.weight.detach()
                c_attn_b = src.c_attn.bias.detach() if src.c_attn.bias is not None else None
                p_cls = src.c_attn.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_cls)
                    or hasattr(src.c_attn, "nf")
                    or (
                        c_attn_w.shape[0] == self.hidden_size
                        and c_attn_w.shape[1] != self.hidden_size
                    )
                ):
                    c_attn_w = c_attn_w.t().contiguous()

                H = self.hidden_size
                w_q = c_attn_w[:H, :]
                w_k = c_attn_w[H : 2 * H, :]
                w_v = c_attn_w[2 * H : 3 * H, :]
                if c_attn_b is not None:
                    b_q = c_attn_b[:H]
                    b_k = c_attn_b[H : 2 * H]
                    b_v = c_attn_b[2 * H : 3 * H]

                out_w = src.c_proj.weight.detach()
                p_proj_cls = src.c_proj.__class__.__name__
                if (
                    ("Conv1D" in p_proj_cls)
                    or hasattr(src.c_proj, "nf")
                    or (out_w.shape[0] == self.hidden_size and out_w.shape[1] != self.hidden_size)
                ):
                    out_w = out_w.t().contiguous()
                w_o = out_w
                b_o = src.c_proj.bias.detach() if src.c_proj.bias is not None else None

            # ---- Pattern 2: BERT/RoBERTa nested ----
            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                w_q = src.query.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.query.bias.detach() if src.query.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.key.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.key.bias.detach() if src.key.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.value.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.value.bias.detach() if src.value.bias is not None else None
                if w_o is None:
                    for attr in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, attr):
                            proj = getattr(src, attr)
                            w_o = proj.weight.detach()
                            b_o = proj.bias.detach() if proj.bias is not None else None
                            break

            # ---- Pattern 3: Qwen/LLaMA ----
            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                w_q = src.q_proj.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_proj.bias.detach() if src.q_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_proj.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_proj.bias.detach() if src.k_proj.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_proj.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_proj.bias.detach() if src.v_proj.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    w_o = src.o_proj.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.o_proj.bias.detach() if src.o_proj.bias is not None else None

            # ---- Pattern 4: DistilBERT ----
            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                w_q = src.q_lin.weight.detach()
                b_q = src.q_lin.bias.detach() if src.q_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_k = src.k_lin.weight.detach()
                b_k = src.k_lin.bias.detach() if src.k_lin.bias is not None else None
                w_v = src.v_lin.weight.detach()
                b_v = src.v_lin.bias.detach() if src.v_lin.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    w_o = src.out_lin.weight.detach()
                    b_o = src.out_lin.bias.detach() if src.out_lin.bias is not None else None

            # ---- Fallback: UniversalAdapter ----
            if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter(old_attn)
                    extracted = adapter.extract_qkv_out()
                    if extracted:
                        w_q = w_q if w_q is not None else extracted.get("q_weight")
                        b_q = b_q if b_q is not None else extracted.get("q_bias")
                        w_k = w_k if w_k is not None else extracted.get("k_weight")
                        b_k = b_k if b_k is not None else extracted.get("k_bias")
                        w_v = w_v if w_v is not None else extracted.get("v_weight")
                        b_v = b_v if b_v is not None else extracted.get("v_bias")
                        if w_o is None:
                            w_o = extracted.get("out_weight")
                            b_o = extracted.get("out_bias")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            missing = [n for n, w in (("q", w_q), ("k", w_k), ("v", w_v)) if w is None]
            if missing:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"SqueezingHeadsDistiller.copy_weights: missing mandatory "
                    f"projections {missing} from {src_cls_name}."
                )

            # Frobenius checks
            for name, w in (("w_q", w_q), ("w_k", w_k), ("w_v", w_v)):
                if torch.norm(w.float(), p="fro").item() < 1e-4:
                    logger.warning("SqueezingHeadsDistiller: %s near-zero Frobenius norm", name)

            # ---- Head importance & selection: SQ2-FIX-02 ----
            importance = self._compute_head_importance(w_q, w_k, w_v)  # (num_heads,)
            num_kept = self.num_kept_heads
            if num_kept < self.num_heads:
                kept_idx, _ = torch.topk(importance, num_kept, largest=True)
                kept_idx, _ = torch.sort(kept_idx)  # stable
            else:
                kept_idx = torch.arange(self.num_heads, device=importance.device)
            self.kept_head_indices = kept_idx.to(torch.long).cpu()

            logger.debug(
                "SqueezingHeadsDistiller: keeping %d/%d heads (indices=%s)",
                num_kept,
                self.num_heads,
                kept_idx.tolist(),
            )

            # ---- Build Q slices for kept heads ----
            D = self.head_dim
            H = self.num_heads
            w_q_heads = w_q[: H * D].view(H, D, -1)  # (H, D, hidden)
            b_q_heads = None
            if b_q is not None and b_q.shape[0] >= H * D:
                b_q_heads = b_q[: H * D].view(H, D)

            kept_q_w = w_q_heads[kept_idx].reshape(num_kept * D, w_q_heads.shape[-1])
            self.q_proj.weight.zero_()
            self.q_proj.weight.copy_(kept_q_w.to(self.q_proj.weight.dtype))

            if b_q_heads is not None and self.q_proj.bias is not None:
                kept_q_b = b_q_heads[kept_idx].reshape(num_kept * D)
                self.q_proj.bias.zero_()
                self.q_proj.bias.copy_(kept_q_b.to(self.q_proj.bias.dtype))

            # ---- K, V: copy ALL num_kv_heads (GQA-safe) ----
            self._copy_kv_full(self.k_proj, w_k, b_k)
            self._copy_kv_full(self.v_proj, w_v, b_v)

            # ---- Output projection ----
            # O must map from kept_q_head_size back to hidden.
            if self.is_nested_encoder:
                # Nested encoder bypasses out_proj entirely.
                nn.init.eye_(self.out_proj.weight)
                if self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(self.out_proj.bias)
            elif w_o is not None:
                # w_o shape: (hidden, num_heads*D). Take kept head columns.
                if w_o.shape == (self.hidden_size, H * D):
                    w_o_heads = w_o.view(self.hidden_size, H, D)
                    kept_o_w = w_o_heads[:, kept_idx, :].reshape(self.hidden_size, num_kept * D)
                    self.out_proj.weight.zero_()
                    self.out_proj.weight.copy_(kept_o_w.to(self.out_proj.weight.dtype))
                elif w_o.shape == (H * D, self.hidden_size):
                    # Transposed
                    w_o_t = w_o.t().contiguous()
                    w_o_heads = w_o_t.view(self.hidden_size, H, D)
                    kept_o_w = w_o_heads[:, kept_idx, :].reshape(self.hidden_size, num_kept * D)
                    self.out_proj.weight.zero_()
                    self.out_proj.weight.copy_(kept_o_w.to(self.out_proj.weight.dtype))
                else:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        f"SqueezingHeadsDistiller: unexpected w_o shape "
                        f"{tuple(w_o.shape)}; expected "
                        f"({self.hidden_size}, {H * D}) or ({H * D}, {self.hidden_size})."
                    )
                if b_o is not None and self.out_proj.bias is not None:
                    m = min(self.out_proj.bias.shape[0], b_o.shape[0])
                    self.out_proj.bias.zero_()
                    self.out_proj.bias[:m].copy_(b_o[:m].to(self.out_proj.bias.dtype))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # K/V copy helper: preserve ALL KV heads
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _copy_kv_full(self, target: LoRALinear, w: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor | None) -> None:
        """Copy the full KV projection, sized to num_kv_heads * head_dim.

        Handles source having num_heads heads by mean-pooling to num_kv_heads
        (i.e., converting MHA → GQA). Never truncates heads silently.
        """
        target.weight.zero_()
        if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
            target.bias.zero_()

        target_out = target.weight.shape[0]
        target_in = target.weight.shape[1]
        D = self.head_dim

        # Case A: exact shape match
        if w.shape[0] == target_out and w.shape[1] == target_in:
            target.weight.copy_(w.to(target.weight.dtype))
            if b is not None and target.bias is not None and b.shape[0] == target_out:
                target.bias.copy_(b.to(target.bias.dtype))
            return

        # Case B: source has more heads (MHA → GQA pooling)
        src_out = w.shape[0]
        if src_out > target_out and src_out % D == 0 and target_out % D == 0:
            src_heads = src_out // D
            tgt_heads = target_out // D
            if src_heads % tgt_heads == 0:
                rep = src_heads // tgt_heads
                w_heads = w[:, :target_in].reshape(src_heads, D, target_in)
                w_pooled = w_heads.view(tgt_heads, rep, D, target_in).mean(dim=1)
                w_pooled = w_pooled.reshape(target_out, target_in)
                target.weight.copy_(w_pooled.to(target.weight.dtype))
                if b is not None and target.bias is not None:
                    b_heads = b.reshape(src_heads, D)
                    b_pooled = b_heads.view(tgt_heads, rep, D).mean(dim=1).reshape(target_out)
                    target.bias.copy_(b_pooled.to(target.bias.dtype))
                return

        # Case C: shape mismatch we cannot resolve
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"SqueezingHeadsDistiller: cannot map K/V weight from "
            f"{tuple(w.shape)} to target {tuple(target.weight.shape)}."
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Mask helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _prepare_valid_mask(attention_mask, B, N_kv, device):
        if attention_mask is None:
            return None
        if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                return attention_mask
            if attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                return attention_mask > -1e4
            return attention_mask > 0
        if attention_mask.dim() == 4:
            if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                v = attention_mask
            elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                v = attention_mask > -1e4
            else:
                v = attention_mask > 0
            return v.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported attention_mask dim: {attention_mask.dim()}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # KV-cache helper
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def _read_past_kv(past_key_value, layer_idx=0):
        if past_key_value is None:
            return None, None, 0
        if hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
            idx = min(layer_idx, len(past_key_value.key_cache) - 1)
            k = past_key_value.key_cache[idx]
            v = past_key_value.value_cache[idx]
            return k, v, k.shape[-2]
        if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 2:
            k, v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            return k, v, k.shape[-2]
        return None, None, 0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,
        attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        head_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_hidden_states: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        encoder_attention_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
        past_key_value: Any | None = None,
        output_attentions: bool | None = False,
        output_hidden_states: bool | None = False,
        return_dict: bool | None = True,
        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor] | None = None,
        layer_idx: int = 0,
        *args: Any,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor | None]:
        B, L, _ = hidden_states.shape
        device = hidden_states.device

        is_cross = encoder_hidden_states is not None
        kv_input = encoder_hidden_states if is_cross else hidden_states
        L_kv_new = kv_input.shape[1]

        # ---- Projections ----
        q = (
            self.q_proj(hidden_states)
            .view(B, L, self.num_kept_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        k_new = (
            self.k_proj(kv_input)
            .view(B, L_kv_new, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )
        v_new = (
            self.v_proj(kv_input)
            .view(B, L_kv_new, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim)
            .transpose(1, 2)
            .contiguous()
        )

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        past_k_probe, _, past_len_rope = self._read_past_kv(past_key_value, layer_idx)
        if position_embeddings is not None and not is_cross:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            cos = cos[..., past_len_rope : past_len_rope + L, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len_rope : past_len_rope + L, :]
            q, k_new = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k_new, cos, sin)

        # ---- KV cache ----
        past_k, past_v, past_len = self._read_past_kv(past_key_value, layer_idx)
        if past_k is not None and not is_cross:
            k_cat = torch.cat([past_k, k_new], dim=-2)
            v_cat = torch.cat([past_v, v_new], dim=-2)
        else:
            k_cat = k_new
            v_cat = v_new

        if past_key_value is not None and hasattr(past_key_value, "update") and not is_cross:
            past_key_value.update(k_new, v_new, layer_idx)

        N_kv = k_cat.shape[2]

        # ---- GQA expansion ----
        # kept Q heads map to KV heads via (kept_idx // num_kv_groups).
        # Here we simply expand KV to num_kv_groups * num_kv_heads, then select
        # the subset corresponding to kept heads.
        if self.num_kv_heads < self.num_heads:
            # Expand KV to full num_heads then gather kept heads.
            k_full = repeat_kv(k_cat, self.num_kv_groups)  # (B, num_heads, N_kv, D)
            v_full = repeat_kv(v_cat, self.num_kv_groups)
            if self.kept_head_indices is not None and self.num_kept_heads < self.num_heads:
                idx = self.kept_head_indices.to(k_full.device)
                k_full = k_full.index_select(1, idx)
                v_full = v_full.index_select(1, idx)
        else:
            k_full = k_cat
            v_full = v_cat

        # ---- Mask ----
        eff_mask = encoder_attention_mask if is_cross else attention_mask
        token_valid = self._prepare_valid_mask(eff_mask, B, N_kv, device)

        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)

        # ---- SDPA fast path ----
        use_sdpa = not output_attentions and head_mask is None
        if use_sdpa:
            q_sdp = q.to(torch.float16) if q.dtype == torch.float32 else q
            k_sdp = k_full.to(torch.float16) if k_full.dtype == torch.float32 else k_full
            v_sdp = v_full.to(torch.float16) if v_full.dtype == torch.float32 else v_full

            attn_bias_sdp = None
            if token_valid is not None:
                attn_bias_sdp = ((token_valid[:, None, None, :].float() - 1.0) * 1e4).to(
                    q_sdp.dtype
                )

            is_causal_sdp = self.is_decoder and not is_cross and past_len == 0 and L > 1

            try:
                attn_out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(
                    q_sdp,
                    k_sdp,
                    v_sdp,
                    attn_mask=attn_bias_sdp,
                    is_causal=is_causal_sdp,
                    dropout_p=self.dropout.p if self.training else 0.0,
                )
                attn_out = (
                    attn_out.to(hidden_states.dtype)
                    .transpose(1, 2)
                    .contiguous()
                    .view(B, L, self.kept_q_head_size)
                )
                attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
                attn_probs_ret = None
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug("SDPA failed (%s); manual path.", e)
                use_sdpa = False

        if not use_sdpa:
            scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), k_full.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

            if self.is_decoder and not is_cross:
                if past_len > 0:
                    abs_q = (past_len + torch.arange(L, device=device)).unsqueeze(1)
                    abs_k = torch.arange(N_kv, device=device).unsqueeze(0)
                    causal = abs_k <= abs_q
                else:
                    causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(L, N_kv, device=device, dtype=torch.bool))
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~causal.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0), -1e4)

            if token_valid is not None:
                scores = scores.masked_fill(~token_valid[:, None, None, :].bool(), -1e4)

            scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            probs = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(hidden_states.dtype)
            probs = self.dropout(probs)

            # head_mask: apply to (B, num_kept_heads, 1, 1)
            if head_mask is not None:
                if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                    hm = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                    hm = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
                elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                    hm = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
                else:
                    hm = head_mask
                if hm.shape[1] != self.num_kept_heads and hm.shape[1] == self.num_heads:
                    # head_mask is for the FULL head count; select kept heads.
                    if self.kept_head_indices is not None:
                        idx = self.kept_head_indices.to(hm.device)
                        hm = hm.index_select(1, idx)
                probs = probs * hm.to(probs.dtype)

            attn_out = (
                torch.matmul(probs, v_full)
                .transpose(1, 2)
                .contiguous()
                .view(B, L, self.kept_q_head_size)
            )
            attn_out = torch.nan_to_num(attn_out, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
            attn_probs_ret = probs if output_attentions else None
        else:
            attn_probs_ret = None

        # ---- Output ----
        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            out = attn_out
        else:
            out = self.out_proj(attn_out)

        if out.shape[-1] != hidden_states.shape[-1]:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"SqueezingHeadsDistiller: output dim {out.shape[-1]} != "
                f"input dim {hidden_states.shape[-1]}."
            )

        return out, attn_probs_ret

Attention wrapper that keeps only the top-k most important heads (based on pretrained Q/K/V Frobenius norms) while preserving GQA structure.

Effective head count: num_kept_heads = round(head_keep_ratio * num_heads) q_head_size = num_kept_heads * head_dim out_head_size = num_kept_heads * head_dim

KV projections preserve all num_kv_heads (GQA). Kept Q heads are grouped to matching KV heads via GQA index mapping.

Parameter reduction: Q : num_heads * head_dim * hidden → num_kept_heads * head_dim * hidden O : hidden * num_heads * head_dim → hidden * num_kept_heads * head_dim K/V: unchanged (GQA-compatible)

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

@ATTENTION_REGISTRY.register("token_compressed_attention")
class TokenCompressedAttention(BaseAttention):
    """
    Token-Compressed Attention v3.

    Compresses K/V tokens while preserving:
      - Token 0 (CLS / first token)
      - A local window of recent tokens (causal decoding)
      - Top-k learned-importance tokens

    Uses Straight-Through Estimator for differentiable token selection.
    """

    supports_gqa = True
    supports_causal = True
    is_custom_attention = True

    def __init__(
        self,
        config=None,
        rank=64,
        compression_ratio=0.5,
        local_window=32,
        per_layer_ratio=False,
        use_ste=True,
        pretrained_attn=None,
        lora_rank=None,
        lora_alpha=None,
        comp_config=None,
        is_decoder=None,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        try:
            super().__init__(config, rank)
        except TypeError:
            super().__init__(config)

        src = pretrained_attn if pretrained_attn is not None else kwargs.get("old_attn")

        # ---- hidden_size ----
        dim = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            dim = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "hidden_size",
                _cfg_get(config, "dim", _cfg_get(config, "d_model", None)),
            )
        if dim is None and comp_config is not None:
            dim = _cfg_get(comp_config, "hidden_size", _cfg_get(comp_config, "dim", None))
        if dim is None and src is not None:
            dim = getattr(src, "hidden_size", getattr(src, "embed_dim", None))
            if dim is None:
                for attr in ("q_proj", "q_lin", "query"):
                    m = getattr(src, attr, None)
                    if m is not None and hasattr(m, "in_features"):
                        dim = m.in_features
                        break
            if dim is None and hasattr(src, "c_attn"):
                w = src.c_attn.weight
                dim = w.shape[0] if w.shape[0] < w.shape[1] else w.shape[1]
        if dim is None:
            dim = kwargs.get("hidden_size", 768)
        self.dim = int(dim)
        self.hidden_size = self.dim

        # ---- num_heads ----
        num_heads = kwargs.get("num_heads")
        if num_heads is None and config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            num_heads = _cfg_get(
                config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(config, "n_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_heads", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None and comp_config is not None:
            num_heads = _cfg_get(
                comp_config,
                "num_attention_heads",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "n_heads", None),
            )
        if num_heads is None and src is not None:
            num_heads = getattr(
                src,
                "num_heads",
                getattr(src, "num_attention_heads", getattr(src, "n_head", None)),
            )
        if num_heads is None:
            num_heads = max(1, self.dim // 64)
        self.num_heads = max(1, int(num_heads))

        # ---- head_dim ----
        head_dim = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            head_dim = _cfg_get(config, "head_dim", None)
        if head_dim is None:
            head_dim = kwargs.get("head_dim")
        if head_dim is None:
            head_dim = self.dim // self.num_heads
        self.head_dim = int(head_dim)

        # ---- num_kv_heads ----
        cfg_kv = None
        if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            cfg_kv = _cfg_get(config, "num_key_value_heads", _cfg_get(config, "num_kv_heads", None))
        if cfg_kv is None and comp_config is not None:
            cfg_kv = _cfg_get(
                comp_config,
                "num_key_value_heads",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "num_kv_heads", None),
            )
        if cfg_kv is None and src is not None:
            cfg_kv = getattr(src, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(src, "num_kv_heads", None))
        self.num_kv_heads = int(cfg_kv) if cfg_kv is not None else self.num_heads
        self.num_kv_heads = max(1, min(self.num_kv_heads, self.num_heads))
        self.num_key_value_groups = max(1, self.num_heads // self.num_kv_heads)

        self.q_head_size = self.num_heads * self.head_dim
        self.kv_head_size = self.num_kv_heads * self.head_dim
        self.all_head_size = self.q_head_size
        self.all_kv_head_size = self.kv_head_size

        # ---- compression ratio ----
        self.base_compression_ratio = float(
            kwargs.get(
                "compression_ratio",
                _cfg_get(comp_config, "compression_ratio", compression_ratio),
            )
        )
        self.base_compression_ratio = max(0.1, min(1.0, self.base_compression_ratio))
        self.per_layer_ratio = bool(per_layer_ratio)
        self.local_window = int(
            kwargs.get("local_window", _cfg_get(comp_config, "local_window", local_window))
        )
        self.use_ste = bool(use_ste)

        # ---- LoRA config ----
        rl = kwargs.get("lora_rank")
        if rl is None and comp_config is not None:
            rl = _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_rank")
        if rl is None and config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            rl = _cfg_get(config, "lora_rank")
        if rl is None:
            rl = lora_rank if lora_rank is not None else 16
        self.lora_rank = int(rl)

        ra = kwargs.get("lora_alpha")
        if ra is None and comp_config is not None:
            ra = _cfg_get(comp_config, "lora_alpha")
        if ra is None and config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            ra = _cfg_get(config, "lora_alpha")
        if ra is None:
            ra = lora_alpha if lora_alpha is not None else 32.0
        self.lora_alpha = float(ra)

        # ---- decoder detection ----
        if is_decoder is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(is_decoder)
        elif kwargs.get("is_decoder") is not None:
            self.is_decoder = bool(kwargs["is_decoder"])
        elif src is not None and getattr(src, "is_decoder", False):
            self.is_decoder = True
        elif config is not None and not isinstance(config, int):
            mt = str(_cfg_get(config, "model_type", "")).lower()
            self.is_decoder = bool(_cfg_get(config, "is_decoder", False)) or any(
                mt.startswith(p)
                for p in (
                    "gpt", "qwen", "llama", "mistral", "gemma", "phi",
                    "opt", "bloom", "falcon", "olmo", "mpt", "stablelm",
                )
            )
        else:
            self.is_decoder = False
        self.is_causal = self.is_decoder
        self.is_nested_encoder = False
        self.rotary_emb = getattr(src, "rotary_emb", None) if src is not None else None

        # Store layer info for per-layer ratio
        self._layer_idx = kwargs.get("layer_idx", 0)
        self._total_layers = kwargs.get("total_layers", 12)

        # ---- device/dtype from source ----
        proj_device = None
        proj_dtype = None
        if src is not None:
            for attr in ("q_lin", "q_proj", "query", "c_attn"):
                mod = getattr(src, attr, None)
                if mod is not None and hasattr(mod, "weight"):
                    proj_device = mod.weight.device
                    proj_dtype = mod.weight.dtype
                    break

        # ---- projections ----
        self.q_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.q_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype,
        )
        self.k_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype,
        )
        self.v_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.dim, self.kv_head_size,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype,
        )
        self.out_lin = LoRALinear(
            self.q_head_size, self.dim,
            bias=True, lora_rank=self.lora_rank, lora_alpha=self.lora_alpha,
            freeze_base=True, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype,
        )

        # Aliases
        self.q_proj = self.q_lin
        self.k_proj = self.k_lin
        self.v_proj = self.v_lin
        self.out_proj = self.out_lin
        self.query = self.q_lin
        self.key = self.k_lin
        self.value = self.v_lin

        # ---- score_net: zero-init last layer ----
        self.score_net = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(self.dim, 64, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(64, 1, device=proj_device, dtype=proj_dtype),
        )
        with torch.no_grad():
            nn.init.zeros_(self.score_net[2].weight)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.score_net[2].bias)

        dropout_p = (
            _cfg_get(config, "dropout", _cfg_get(config, "attention_probs_dropout_prob", 0.1))
            if config is not None and not isinstance(config, int)
            else 0.1
        )
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout_p)

        if pretrained_attn is not None:
            self.copy_weights(pretrained_attn)

    # ---- aliases ----
    @property
    def dense(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def c_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    @property
    def o_proj(self):
        return self.out_lin

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Per-layer compression ratio
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _get_compression_ratio(self) -> float:
        """Returns the effective compression ratio for this layer."""
        if not self.per_layer_ratio:
            return self.base_compression_ratio
        # Layer 0: 0.8, Last layer: 0.3 (linearly decreasing)
        progress = self._layer_idx / max(1, self._total_layers - 1)
        ratio = 0.8 - 0.5 * progress
        # Blend with base ratio
        return max(0.15, min(0.95, ratio * self.base_compression_ratio / 0.5))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # copy_weights
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def copy_weights(self, old_attn):
        with torch.no_grad():
            if getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None) is not None:
                self.rotary_emb = old_attn.rotary_emb

            w_q = b_q = w_k = b_k = w_v = b_v = w_o = b_o = None

            src_cls_name = getattr(old_attn, "__class__", type(old_attn)).__name__
            if src_cls_name in ("BertSelfAttention", "RobertaSelfAttention") or (
                hasattr(old_attn, "query")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "dense")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "out_proj")
                and not hasattr(old_attn, "c_proj")
            ):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True

            if hasattr(old_attn, "self") and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
                self.is_nested_encoder = True
                src = old_attn.self
                if hasattr(old_attn.output, "dense"):
                    dense = getattr(old_attn.output.dense, "base_layer", old_attn.output.dense)
                    w_o = dense.weight.detach()
                    b_o = dense.bias.detach() if dense.bias is not None else None
            else:
                src = old_attn

            def _unwrap(layer):
                return getattr(layer, "base_layer", layer)

            # GPT-2 fused c_attn
            if hasattr(src, "c_attn") and hasattr(src, "c_proj"):
                c_attn = _unwrap(src.c_attn)
                c_proj = _unwrap(src.c_proj)
                cw = c_attn.weight.detach()
                cb = c_attn.bias.detach() if c_attn.bias is not None else None
                if cw.dim() == 2 and cw.shape[0] == self.dim and cw.shape[1] != self.dim:
                    cw = cw.t().contiguous()
                qd, kd, vd = self.q_head_size, self.kv_head_size, self.kv_head_size
                w_q = cw[:qd]
                w_k = cw[qd : qd + kd]
                w_v = cw[qd + kd : qd + kd + vd]
                if cb is not None:
                    b_q = cb[:qd]
                    b_k = cb[qd : qd + kd]
                    b_v = cb[qd + kd : qd + kd + vd]
                ow = c_proj.weight.detach()
                if ow.dim() == 2 and ow.shape[0] == self.dim and ow.shape[1] != self.dim:
                    ow = ow.t().contiguous()
                w_o = ow
                b_o = c_proj.bias.detach() if c_proj.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "query") and hasattr(src, "key") and hasattr(src, "value"):
                q_l, k_l, v_l = _unwrap(src.query), _unwrap(src.key), _unwrap(src.value)
                w_q = q_l.weight.detach()
                b_q = q_l.bias.detach() if q_l.bias is not None else None
                w_k = k_l.weight.detach()
                b_k = k_l.bias.detach() if k_l.bias is not None else None
                w_v = v_l.weight.detach()
                b_v = v_l.bias.detach() if v_l.bias is not None else None
                if w_o is None:
                    for a in ("dense", "out_proj", "o_proj", "c_proj"):
                        if hasattr(src, a):
                            p = _unwrap(getattr(src, a))
                            w_o = p.weight.detach()
                            b_o = p.bias.detach() if p.bias is not None else None
                            break

            elif hasattr(src, "q_proj") and hasattr(src, "k_proj") and hasattr(src, "v_proj"):
                q_l, k_l, v_l = _unwrap(src.q_proj), _unwrap(src.k_proj), _unwrap(src.v_proj)
                w_q = q_l.weight.detach()
                b_q = q_l.bias.detach() if q_l.bias is not None else None
                w_k = k_l.weight.detach()
                b_k = k_l.bias.detach() if k_l.bias is not None else None
                w_v = v_l.weight.detach()
                b_v = v_l.bias.detach() if v_l.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "o_proj"):
                    o_l = _unwrap(src.o_proj)
                    w_o = o_l.weight.detach()
                    b_o = o_l.bias.detach() if o_l.bias is not None else None

            elif hasattr(src, "q_lin") and hasattr(src, "k_lin") and hasattr(src, "v_lin"):
                q_l, k_l, v_l = _unwrap(src.q_lin), _unwrap(src.k_lin), _unwrap(src.v_lin)
                w_q = q_l.weight.detach()
                b_q = q_l.bias.detach() if q_l.bias is not None else None
                w_k = k_l.weight.detach()
                b_k = k_l.bias.detach() if k_l.bias is not None else None
                w_v = v_l.weight.detach()
                b_v = v_l.bias.detach() if v_l.bias is not None else None
                if hasattr(src, "out_lin"):
                    o_l = _unwrap(src.out_lin)
                    w_o = o_l.weight.detach()
                    b_o = o_l.bias.detach() if o_l.bias is not None else None

            if w_q is None or w_k is None or w_v is None:
                try:
                    adapter = UniversalAdapter()
                    if w_q is None:
                        w_q, b_q = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "q")
                    if w_k is None:
                        w_k, b_k = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "k")
                    if w_v is None:
                        w_v, b_v = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "v")
                    if w_o is None:
                        w_o, b_o = adapter.get_weight_bias(src, "o")
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug("UniversalAdapter fallback failed: %s", e)

            missing = []
            if w_q is None:
                missing.append("Q")
            if w_k is None:
                missing.append("K")
            if w_v is None:
                missing.append("V")
            if w_o is None and not self.is_nested_encoder:
                missing.append("O")
            if missing:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"TokenCompressedAttention.copy_weights: missing {missing} from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
                )

            def _copy(target_layer, w, b):
                if w is not None:
                    if w.shape == target_layer.weight.shape:
                        target_layer.weight.data.copy_(w.to(target_layer.weight.dtype))
                    elif w.t().contiguous().shape == target_layer.weight.shape:
                        target_layer.weight.data.copy_(
                            w.t().contiguous().to(target_layer.weight.dtype)
                        )
                    else:
                        m0 = min(target_layer.weight.shape[0], w.shape[0])
                        m1 = min(target_layer.weight.shape[1], w.shape[1])
                        target_layer.weight.data.zero_()
                        target_layer.weight.data[:m0, :m1].copy_(
                            w[:m0, :m1].to(target_layer.weight.dtype)
                        )
                if b is not None and target_layer.bias is not None:
                    if b.shape == target_layer.bias.shape:
                        target_layer.bias.data.copy_(b.to(target_layer.bias.dtype))
                    else:
                        mb = min(target_layer.bias.shape[0], b.shape[0])
                        target_layer.bias.data.zero_()
                        target_layer.bias.data[:mb].copy_(b[:mb].to(target_layer.bias.dtype))
                elif target_layer.bias is not None:
                    target_layer.bias.data.zero_()
                target_layer.weight.requires_grad = False
                if target_layer.bias is not None:
                    target_layer.bias.requires_grad = False

            _copy(self.q_lin, w_q, b_q)
            _copy(self.k_lin, w_k, b_k)
            _copy(self.v_lin, w_v, b_v)
            if w_o is not None:
                _copy(self.out_lin, w_o, b_o)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Token importance computation
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _compute_token_importance(self, x, q, k, valid_mask_2d):
        """
        Computes token importance as a combination of:
          1. Energy (norm of hidden state)
          2. Learned saliency (score_net)
          3. Attention-based score (query-key similarity, sampled)

        Returns: (B, L) importance scores
        """
        B, L, D = x.shape
        x_f = x.float()

        # 1. Energy term
        energy = torch.norm(x_f, p=2, dim=-1)  # (B, L)

        # 2. Learned saliency
        score_dev = next(self.score_net.parameters()).device
        score_dtype = next(self.score_net.parameters()).dtype
        learned = (
            self.score_net(x_f.to(score_dev, score_dtype))
            .squeeze(-1)
            .to(x_f.device, x_f.dtype)
        )  # (B, L)

        # 3. Attention-based term (sampled, no grad)
        # Use first few heads and queries to get rough attention importance
        with torch.no_grad():
            H_sample = min(2, self.num_heads)
            L_sample = min(64, L)
            q_sample = q[:, :H_sample, :L_sample, :]  # (B, H_s, L_s, Dh)
            k_sample = k[:, :H_sample, :, :]  # (B, H_s, L, Dh)
            # scale
            scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(self.head_dim)
            attn_sample = torch.matmul(q_sample.float(), k_sample.float().transpose(-2, -1)) * scale
            attn_sample = F.softmax(attn_sample, dim=-1)  # (B, H_s, L_s, L)
            # Average over heads and query positions -> importance per key
            attn_importance = attn_sample.mean(dim=(1, 2))  # (B, L)
            # Normalize
            attn_importance = attn_importance / (attn_importance.max(dim=-1, keepdim=True).values + 1e-6)

        # Combine
        total_importance = energy + learned + 0.5 * attn_importance
        return total_importance

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Token selection (with STE for differentiability)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _select_compressed_tokens(
        self,
        x,
        q,
        k_tensor,
        v_tensor,
        valid_mask_2d,
    ):
        """
        Selects top-k tokens based on importance.

        Returns:
            k_comp, v_comp, top_indices, comp_mask, ste_mask
        """
        B, L, D = x.shape
        H_kv, Dh = self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim

        # Fast path for very short sequences
        if L <= 2:
            top_indices = torch.arange(L, device=x.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, L)
            comp_mask = valid_mask_2d if valid_mask_2d is not None else None
            ste_mask = torch.ones(B, L, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
            return k_tensor, v_tensor, top_indices, comp_mask, ste_mask

        # ---- compute importance ----
        total_importance = self._compute_token_importance(x, q, k_tensor, valid_mask_2d)

        # ---- effective compression ratio ----
        eff_ratio = self._get_compression_ratio()
        k_keep = max(2, int(math.ceil(L * eff_ratio)))
        k_keep = min(k_keep, L)

        # ---- mask padding ----
        if valid_mask_2d is not None:
            total_importance = total_importance.masked_fill(~valid_mask_2d, -1e4)

        # ---- all-masked safety ----
        all_masked = (total_importance <= -1e4).all(dim=-1)
        if all_masked.any():
            total_importance = total_importance.clone()
            total_importance[all_masked, 0] = 0.0

        # ---- CLS preservation ----
        cls_boost = torch.full(
            (B, 1), 1e4, device=x.device, dtype=total_importance.dtype
        )
        total_importance = torch.cat(
            [cls_boost, total_importance[:, 1:]], dim=-1
        )

        # ---- Local window always-on (causal decoding) ----
        if self.is_decoder and self.local_window > 0:
            w = min(self.local_window, L)
            local_boost = torch.zeros_like(total_importance)
            local_boost[:, -w:] = 1e3
            total_importance = total_importance + local_boost

        # ---- Top-k selection ----
        _, top_indices = torch.topk(total_importance, k_keep, dim=-1)
        top_indices, _ = torch.sort(top_indices, dim=-1)

        # ---- Gather K/V ----
        top_idx_exp = top_indices.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, H_kv, -1, Dh)
        k_comp = torch.gather(k_tensor, 2, top_idx_exp)
        v_comp = torch.gather(v_tensor, 2, top_idx_exp)

        # ---- STE mask (differentiable) ----
        ste_mask = None
        if self.use_ste and self.training:
            # Build hard mask
            hard_mask = torch.zeros_like(total_importance)
            hard_mask.scatter_(-1, top_indices, 1.0)

            # Soft mask from softmax
            soft_mask = F.softmax(total_importance, dim=-1)

            # Straight-through: forward = hard, backward = soft
            ste_mask = hard_mask + (soft_mask - soft_mask.detach())

        comp_mask = None
        if valid_mask_2d is not None:
            comp_mask = torch.gather(valid_mask_2d, 1, top_indices)

        return k_comp, v_comp, top_indices, comp_mask, ste_mask

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Forward
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def forward(
        self,
        hidden_states,
        attention_mask=None,
        head_mask=None,
        encoder_hidden_states=None,
        encoder_attention_mask=None,
        past_key_value=None,
        output_attentions=False,
        output_hidden_states=False,
        return_dict=True,
        position_embeddings=None,
        layer_idx=0,
        *args,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        if encoder_hidden_states is not None:
            raise NotImplementedError("TokenCompressedAttention: cross-attention not supported.")

        B, L, D = hidden_states.shape
        H, H_kv, Dh = self.num_heads, self.num_kv_heads, self.head_dim
        device = hidden_states.device
        dtype = hidden_states.dtype

        q = self.q_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, H, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        k = self.k_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()
        v = self.v_lin(hidden_states).view(B, L, H_kv, Dh).transpose(1, 2).contiguous()

        # ---- RoPE ----
        if position_embeddings is None:
            position_embeddings = kwargs.get("position_embeddings")
        if position_embeddings is not None:
            cos, sin = position_embeddings
            past_len = 0
            if past_key_value is not None:
                if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)) and len(past_key_value) >= 1:
                    past_len = past_key_value[0].shape[-2]
                elif hasattr(past_key_value, "key_cache") and past_key_value.key_cache:
                    past_len = past_key_value.key_cache[0].shape[-2]
            cos = cos[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            sin = sin[..., past_len : past_len + L, :]
            q, k = apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin)

        # ---- valid mask ----
        valid_mask_2d = None
        if attention_mask is not None:
            if attention_mask.dim() == 2:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    valid_mask_2d = attention_mask
                elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    valid_mask_2d = attention_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    valid_mask_2d = attention_mask > 0
            elif attention_mask.dim() == 4:
                if attention_mask.dtype == torch.bool:
                    v_mask = attention_mask
                elif attention_mask.dtype.is_floating_point:
                    v_mask = attention_mask > -1e4
                else:
                    v_mask = attention_mask > 0
                valid_mask_2d = v_mask.any(dim=-2).squeeze(1)

        # ---- KV-cache mode: prefill or decode ----
        is_kv_cache_mode = past_key_value is not None

        # FIXED: Global topk selection violates causality. Disable compression entirely if is_decoder.
        if self.is_decoder:
            is_kv_cache_mode = True # Force fallback
            
        if is_kv_cache_mode and self.is_decoder:
            if isinstance(past_key_value, (tuple, list)):
                past_k, past_v = past_key_value[0], past_key_value[1]
            else:
                past_k = getattr(past_key_value, "key_cache", [None])[0]
                past_v = getattr(past_key_value, "value_cache", [None])[0]

            if past_k is not None and past_v is not None:
                full_k = torch.cat([past_k, k], dim=2)
                full_v = torch.cat([past_v, v], dim=2)
            else:
                full_k, full_v = k, v

            # Standard attention for decoder (causality preservation)
            full_k_use = full_k
            full_v_use = full_v
            top_idx = None
            comp_valid = None
            ste_mask = None
        elif is_kv_cache_mode:
                # Prefill: apply compression normally
                full_k_use, full_v_use, top_idx, comp_valid, ste_mask = self._select_compressed_tokens(
                    hidden_states, q, full_k, full_v, valid_mask_2d
                )
        else:
            # Standard mode
            k_comp, v_comp, top_idx, comp_valid, ste_mask = self._select_compressed_tokens(
                hidden_states, q, k, v, valid_mask_2d
            )
            full_k_use, full_v_use = k_comp, v_comp

        if H_kv != H:
            full_k_use = repeat_kv(full_k_use, self.num_key_value_groups)
            full_v_use = repeat_kv(full_v_use, self.num_key_value_groups)

        # ---- Attention ----
        scale = 1.0 / math.sqrt(Dh)
        scores = torch.matmul(q.float(), full_k_use.float().transpose(-2, -1).contiguous()) * scale

        # ---- Causal mask (on original positions) ----
        if self.is_decoder and top_idx is not None:
            N_k = full_k_use.shape[-2]
            # FIXED: Shift q_pos by N_k - L so queries correctly attend to their past during generation.
            q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=device).view(1, 1, L, 1)
            k_orig = top_idx.unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
            causal_mask = k_orig > q_pos
            scores = scores.masked_fill(causal_mask, -1e4)

            # Safety: force-keep CLS if all kept tokens are future
            all_future = causal_mask.all(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            if all_future.any():
                cls_pos = top_idx == 0
                cls_slot = cls_pos.long().argmax(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                cls_slot = cls_slot.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, 1, L, 1)
                scores = scores.scatter(-1, cls_slot, 0.0)

        elif self.is_decoder and top_idx is None:
            # KV-cache mode with past + current
            N_k = full_k_use.shape[-2]
            q_pos = torch.arange(N_k - L, N_k, device=device).view(1, 1, L, 1)
            k_pos = torch.arange(N_k, device=device).view(1, 1, 1, N_k)
            causal_mask = k_pos > q_pos
            scores = scores.masked_fill(causal_mask, -1e4)

        # ---- Padding mask ----
        if comp_valid is not None:
            cm = comp_valid[:, None, None, :]
            if self.is_decoder and top_idx is not None:
                cls_mask = (top_idx == 0)[:, None, None, :]
                cm = cm | cls_mask
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~cm, -1e4)

        scores = torch.nan_to_num(scores, nan=-1e4, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)
        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).to(dtype)
        attn = torch.nan_to_num(attn, nan=0.0)
        attn = self.dropout(attn)

        # ---- STE gradient injection ----
        if ste_mask is not None and self.training:
            # ste_mask: (B, L) -> reduce to (B, 1, 1, L) for broadcasting
            ste_b = ste_mask[:, None, None, :]
            # Normalize ste_mask to sum to 1 over kept tokens
            ste_norm = ste_b / (ste_b.sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True) + 1e-6)
            # Blend: attn = attn + (ste_norm - ste_norm.detach()) * small_factor
            # This injects gradient into score_net
            attn = attn + 0.01 * (ste_norm - ste_norm.detach())

        # ---- Head mask ----
        if head_mask is not None:
            if head_mask.dim() == 1:
                head_mask = head_mask.view(1, -1, 1, 1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 2:
                head_mask = head_mask.unsqueeze(-1).unsqueeze(-1)
            elif head_mask.dim() == 3:
                head_mask = head_mask[:, :, :1].unsqueeze(-1)
            attn = attn * head_mask.to(attn.dtype)

        context = torch.matmul(attn, full_v_use)
        context = context.transpose(1, 2).contiguous().view(B, L, self.q_head_size)
        context = torch.nan_to_num(context, nan=0.0, posinf=1e4, neginf=-1e4)

        if self.is_nested_encoder:
            output = context
        else:
            output = self.out_lin(context)

        if output.shape[-1] != D:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"TokenCompressedAttention: output dim {output.shape[-1]} != hidden_size {D}"
            )

        # Return cache if requested
        new_cache = None
        if kwargs.get("use_cache", False):
            new_cache = (full_k_use if top_idx is None else full_k_use, full_v_use)

        if new_cache is not None:
            return output, new_cache

        return output, (attn if output_attentions else None)

Token-Compressed Attention v3.

Compresses K/V tokens while preserving: - Token 0 (CLS / first token) - A local window of recent tokens (causal decoding) - Top-k learned-importance tokens

Uses Straight-Through Estimator for differentiable token selection.

Initialize internal Module state, shared by both nn.Module and ScriptModule.

Ancestors

  • BaseAttention
  • torch.nn.modules.module.Module

class UniversalAdapter
Expand source code 
class UniversalAdapter:
    """Universal adapter supporting BERT, DistilBERT, RoBERTa, LLaMA, GPT2, DeBERTa, etc."""

    @staticmethod
    def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any, proj: str) -> tuple:
        """Extract (weight, bias) for projection 'q', 'k', 'v', or 'o'.

        Handles:
        - RoBERTa/BERT: query/key/value attributes on .self sub-module
        - LLaMA/Qwen: q_proj/k_proj/v_proj on attention module directly
        - GPT-2: c_attn (fused, already handled by fused-QKV path); c_proj
        - DistilBERT: q_lin/k_lin/v_lin/out_lin
        - LoRALinear: unwraps .base_layer to get the frozen base weight
        - Conv1D: transposes weight from [in, out] to [out, in]
        """
        target = getattr(old_attn, "self", old_attn)
        candidate_names = CANONICAL_PROJECTION_ALIASES.get(proj, [])

        def _unwrap(mod: Any) -> Any:
            """Unwrap LoRALinear / merged adapter layers to their base_layer."""
            if mod is None:
                return mod
            # LoRALinear stores the original frozen layer as base_layer
            if hasattr(mod, "base_layer") and hasattr(mod.base_layer, "weight"):
                return mod.base_layer
            return mod

        def _extract(mod: Any):
            """Return (weight_data, bias) from a module, or None if not a valid weight-holder."""
            mod = _unwrap(mod)
            if mod is None:
                return None
            if not hasattr(mod, "weight") or mod.weight is None:
                return None
            import torch
            import torch.nn as nn

            w = mod.weight
            # Must be either an nn.Parameter or a plain Tensor (Conv1D)
            if not (isinstance(w, nn.Parameter) or isinstance(w, torch.Tensor)):
                return None
            w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
            # Projections must strictly be 2D weight matrices (excludes 1D norms, 3D convolutions, etc.)
            if w.ndim != 2:
                return None
            # Conv1D stores weights transposed: [in_features, out_features]
            is_conv1d = (mod.__class__.__name__ == "Conv1D") or hasattr(mod, "nf")
            if is_conv1d or (w.shape[0] < w.shape[1]):
                w = w.t().contiguous()
            bias = getattr(mod, "bias", None)
            bias = bias.data if (bias is not None and hasattr(bias, "data")) else bias
            # Qwen3.5 Gated Q: query is fused with gate (dim 0 has 2 * num_heads * head_dim)
            if proj == "q" and (
                hasattr(old_attn, "q_norm")
                or old_attn.__class__.__name__ == "Qwen3_5Attention"
                or hasattr(old_attn, "k_norm")
            ):
                cfg = getattr(old_attn, "config", None)
                tc = getattr(cfg, "text_config", cfg)
                h_dim = getattr(tc, "head_dim", 256)
                n_heads = getattr(tc, "num_attention_heads", getattr(old_attn, "num_heads", 8))
                if w.shape[0] == n_heads * h_dim * 2:
                    w = (
                        w.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim, -1)[:, 0, :, :]
                        .reshape(n_heads * h_dim, -1)
                        .contiguous()
                    )
                    if bias is not None:
                        bias = (
                            bias.view(n_heads, 2, h_dim)[:, 0, :]
                            .reshape(n_heads * h_dim)
                            .contiguous()
                        )
            return w, bias

        # 1. Search in target (old_attn.self or old_attn)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(target, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(target, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 2. Search in old_attn.output (BERT / RoBERTa output projection 'o')
        if proj == "o" and hasattr(old_attn, "output"):
            out_mod = old_attn.output
            if hasattr(out_mod, "dense"):
                result = _extract(out_mod.dense)
                if result is not None:
                    return result
            result = _extract(out_mod)
            if result is not None:
                return result

        # 3. Search directly in old_attn (catches cases where there's no .self sub-module)
        for name in candidate_names:
            if hasattr(old_attn, name):
                result = _extract(getattr(old_attn, name))
                if result is not None:
                    return result

        # 4. Fallback for Fused GPT-2 c_attn (must precede positional fallback)
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "c_attn"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.c_attn)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :], (b[:split_b] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :], (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :], (b[2 * split_b :] if b is not None else None)

        # 4b. Fallback for Qwen3.5 / fused in_proj_qkv
        if proj in ("q", "k", "v") and hasattr(old_attn, "in_proj_qkv"):
            fused_result = _extract(old_attn.in_proj_qkv)
            if fused_result is not None:
                w, b = fused_result
                k_dim = getattr(old_attn, "key_dim", getattr(old_attn, "k_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                v_dim = getattr(old_attn, "value_dim", getattr(old_attn, "v_dim", w.size(0) // 3))
                q_dim = w.size(0) - k_dim - v_dim if (k_dim + v_dim < w.size(0)) else k_dim
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:q_dim, :], (b[:q_dim] if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim : q_dim + k_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim, :], (
                        b[q_dim + k_dim : q_dim + k_dim + v_dim] if b is not None else None
                    )

        # 4c. PyTorch nn.MultiheadAttention in_proj_weight / separate proj_weight
        for obj in (target, old_attn):
            if (
                proj in ("q", "k", "v")
                and hasattr(obj, "in_proj_weight")
                and obj.in_proj_weight is not None
            ):
                w = (
                    obj.in_proj_weight.data
                    if hasattr(obj.in_proj_weight, "data")
                    else obj.in_proj_weight
                )
                b = getattr(obj, "in_proj_bias", None)
                b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                split_w = w.size(0) // 3
                split_b = b.size(0) // 3 if b is not None else 0
                if proj == "q":
                    return w[:split_w, :].clone(), (b[:split_b].clone() if b is not None else None)
                elif proj == "k":
                    return w[split_w : 2 * split_w, :].clone(), (
                        b[split_b : 2 * split_b].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )
                elif proj == "v":
                    return w[2 * split_w :, :].clone(), (
                        b[2 * split_b :].clone() if b is not None else None
                    )

            if proj in ("q", "k", "v"):
                attr_w = f"{proj}_proj_weight"
                if hasattr(obj, attr_w) and getattr(obj, attr_w) is not None:
                    w = getattr(obj, attr_w)
                    w = w.data if hasattr(w, "data") else w
                    b = getattr(obj, f"{proj}_proj_bias", None)
                    b = b.data if (b is not None and hasattr(b, "data")) else b
                    return w.clone(), (b.clone() if b is not None else None)

        # 5. Positional fallback: scan all Linear children of target in declaration order.
        #    Only valid if at least 3 children exist (the typical pattern for BERT/RoBERTa variants).
        ordered_proj_map = {"q": 0, "k": 1, "v": 2}
        if proj in ordered_proj_map:
            children_with_weight = [
                (n, m) for n, m in target.named_children() if _extract(m) is not None
            ]
            if len(children_with_weight) >= 3:
                idx = ordered_proj_map[proj]
                if idx < len(children_with_weight):
                    return _extract(children_with_weight[idx][1])

        # 6. Fallback for 'o' projection when replacing leaf self-attention without local out_proj
        # (e.g. RoBERTa/BERT RobertaSelfAttention where output.dense resides in the parent container).
        # Returning an Identity matrix ensures:
        #   (a) custom modules don't crash during weight copying, and
        #   (b) context passes through transparently to parent.output.dense without double projection!
        if proj == "o":
            for mod in target.modules():
                if (
                    hasattr(mod, "weight")
                    and isinstance(getattr(mod, "weight", None), torch.Tensor)
                    and mod.weight.ndim == 2
                ):
                    d = mod.weight.shape[0]
                    eye_w = torch.eye(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    zero_b = torch.zeros(d, dtype=mod.weight.dtype, device=mod.weight.device)
                    return eye_w, zero_b

        raise ValueError(
            f"Could not extract '{proj}' projection weights from {type(old_attn).__name__}"
        )

    @staticmethod
    def get_rotary_emb(old_attn: Any) -> Any | None:
        """Return rotary embeddings if present."""
        return getattr(old_attn, "rotary_emb", None)

    @staticmethod
    def get_kv_heads(config: Any) -> int:
        """Return number of key-value heads for GQA/MQA."""
        return getattr(config, "num_key_value_heads", getattr(config, "num_attention_heads", 12))

def get_weight_bias(old_attn: Any,
